[APD_SRC_CO] Full IsCO and exact residue-map contract
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/ResMapBijective.lean#L9-L75
class IsCO (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] : Prop
    extends IsLocalRing R, IsNoetherianRing R where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal R) R
  isLocalHom : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)
  res_surj : ∀ x : R, ∃ a : O, x - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R

attribute [instance] IsCO.complete IsCO.isLocalHom

instance IsCO.self (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] : IsCO O O where
  complete := inferInstance
  isLocalHom := ⟨fun _ h => h⟩
  res_surj x := ⟨x, by simp⟩

section Res

variable (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]

theorem resMap_bijective : Function.Bijective (ResidueField.map (algebraMap O R)) := by
  refine ⟨(ResidueField.map (algebraMap O R)).injective, fun y => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := residue_surjective y
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
  refine ⟨residue O a, ?_⟩
  rw [ResidueField.map_residue]
  exact ((Ideal.Quotient.eq).2 ha).symm

def res : R →ₐ[O] ResidueField O :=
  { ((RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm.toRingHom.comp (residue R)) with
    commutes' := fun a => by
      show (RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm (residue R (algebraMap O R a)) = residue O a
      rw [RingEquiv.symm_apply_eq]
      exact (ResidueField.map_residue _ _).symm }

theorem res_eq_zero_iff (x : R) : res O R x = 0 ↔ x ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  show (RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm (residue R x) = 0 ↔ _
  rw [map_eq_zero_iff _ (RingEquiv.injective _), residue_eq_zero_iff]

variable {O R} in

theorem res_map {R' : Type} [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (x : R) :
    res O R' (f x) = res O R x := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
  have h1 : res O R x = algebraMap O _ a := by
    have := (res_eq_zero_iff O R _).2 ha
    rwa [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at this
  have hm : ∀ y ∈ maximalIdeal R, f y ∈ maximalIdeal R' := fun y hy => by
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) (f y)
    by_cases hu : IsUnit b
    · exfalso
      have h2 : IsUnit (y - algebraMap O R b) := by
        by_contra hn
        have h3 : y - algebraMap O R b ∈ maximalIdeal R := hn
        have h4 : algebraMap O R b ∈ maximalIdeal R := by simpa using Ideal.sub_mem _ hy h3
        exact h4 (hu.map _)
      have h5 := h2.map f
      rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes] at h5
      exact hb h5
    · have h6 : algebraMap O R' b ∈ maximalIdeal R' := fun h => hu (IsLocalHom.map_nonunit b h)
      simpa using Ideal.add_mem _ hb h6
  have h2 := (res_eq_zero_iff O R' _).2 (hm _ ha)
  rw [map_sub, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at h2
  rw [h2, h1]

end Res

def AdicCont {Γ R : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CommRing R] (I : Ideal R) (f : Γ → R) : Prop :=
  ∀ N : ℕ, IsLocallyConstant fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (f g)


[APD_SRC_SPACE] Original NRep, IsBar, IsLift, Space, actual A/B and all named ideals
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/LeAdicClosure.lean#L9-L219
def w (n : ℕ) (i : Fin n) : ℤ := if n = 3 then 1 - (i : ℕ) else (n : ℤ) - 1 - 2 * (i : ℕ)

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (F : Frame l k) (n : ℕ)

def ordProd {H K : Type} [CommRing K] {n : ℕ} (r : H → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) (ψ : Fin n → H → K) (g : Fin n → H) :
    Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K := (List.ofFn fun i => r (g i) - ψ i (g i) • (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)).prod

theorem ordProd_map {H K K' : Type} [CommRing K] [CommRing K'] {n : ℕ} (f : K →+* K')
    (r : H → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) (ψ : Fin n → H → K) (g : Fin n → H) :
    ordProd (fun s => f.mapMatrix (r s)) (fun i s => f (ψ i s)) g = f.mapMatrix (ordProd r ψ g) := by
  simp only [ordProd, map_list_prod, List.map_ofFn]
  congr 1
  refine congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun i => ?_)
  simp only [Function.comp_apply, map_sub]
  congr 1
  ext a b
  by_cases h : a = b <;> simp [h, Matrix.one_apply]

@[ext] structure NRep (K : Type) [CommRing K] where
  r : F.G0 →* Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K
  ψ : ∀ v, Fin n → F.Gv v →* Kˣ
  pol : ∀ g, r (F.star g) = Jmat K n * (r g)ᵀ * Jmat K n
  diag : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → (r F.g0 - r (F.star F.g0)) i j = 0
  ord : ∀ v g, ordProd (fun s => r (F.loc0 v s)) (fun i s => (ψ v i s : K)) g = 0

namespace NRep

variable {F n} {K K' : Type} [CommRing K] [CommRing K']

def map (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) : NRep F n K' where
  r := (f.mapMatrix : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K →+* _).toMonoidHom.comp ρ.r
  ψ v i := (Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp (ρ.ψ v i)
  pol g := by
    show f.mapMatrix (ρ.r (F.star g)) = Jmat K' n * (f.mapMatrix (ρ.r g))ᵀ * Jmat K' n
    rw [ρ.pol, map_mul, map_mul]
    simp only [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Jmat_map, Matrix.transpose_map]
  diag i j h := by
    show f (ρ.r F.g0 i j) - f (ρ.r (F.star F.g0) i j) = 0
    rw [← map_sub]
    exact (congrArg f (ρ.diag i j h)).trans (map_zero f)
  ord v g := (ordProd_map f _ _ g).trans (by rw [ρ.ord, map_zero])

@[simp] theorem map_r (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin n) :
    (ρ.map f).r g i j = f (ρ.r g i j) := rfl

@[simp] theorem map_ψ (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) :
    ((ρ.map f).ψ v i g : K') = f (ρ.ψ v i g) := rfl

theorem map_map {K'' : Type} [CommRing K''] (f : K →+* K') (f' : K' →+* K'') (ρ : NRep F n K) :
    (ρ.map f).map f' = ρ.map (f'.comp f) := rfl

def restrict {O : Type} [CommRing O] [Algebra O K] (ρ : NRep F n K) (A : Subalgebra O K)
    (h1 : ∀ g i j, ρ.r g i j ∈ A) (h2 : ∀ v i g, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) ∈ A) : NRep F n A where
  r :=
    { toFun := fun g => Matrix.of fun i j => ⟨ρ.r g i j, h1 g i j⟩
      map_one' := by
        ext i j
        by_cases h : i = j <;> simp [h, Matrix.one_apply]
      map_mul' := fun g h => by
        ext i j
        simp [Matrix.mul_apply] }
  ψ v i :=
    { toFun := fun g => ⟨⟨ρ.ψ v i g, h2 v i g⟩, ⟨ρ.ψ v i g⁻¹, h2 v i g⁻¹⟩, Subtype.ext (by simp), Subtype.ext (by simp)⟩
      map_one' := by ext; simp
      map_mul' := fun g h => by ext; simp }
  pol g := by
    refine Matrix.map_injective (f := (A.val : A →+* K)) Subtype.val_injective ?_
    show _ = (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _
    rw [map_mul, map_mul]
    simp only [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Jmat_map]
    exact ρ.pol g
  diag i j h := Subtype.ext (ρ.diag i j h)
  ord v g := by
    refine Matrix.map_injective (f := (A.val : A →+* K)) Subtype.val_injective ?_
    show (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _ = (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _
    rw [← ordProd_map, map_zero]
    exact ρ.ord v g

def Cont (I : Ideal K) (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop :=
  (∀ i j, AdicCont I fun g => ρ.r g i j) ∧ ∀ v i, AdicCont I fun g => (ρ.ψ v i g : K)

def IsBar (f : k →+* K) (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop :=
  (∀ g i, ρ.r g i i = f ((F.Ψ g ^ w n i : kˣ) : k)) ∧ ∀ v i g, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) = f ((F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ w n i : kˣ) : k)

def IsDiag (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop := ∀ g i j, i ≠ j → ρ.r g i j = 0

def triP (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, j < i ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def triM (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, i < j ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def ee (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, Odd (w n i - w n j) ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def OffTri (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ¬ ρ.triP ≤ p ∧ ¬ ρ.triM ≤ p

def TI (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ρ.OffTri p ∧ (n = 3 → ¬ ρ.ee ≤ p)

def TE (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ρ.OffTri p ∧ n = 3 ∧ ρ.ee ≤ p

def Coin (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) (v : F.V) (i j : Fin n) : Prop :=
  ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) - ρ.ψ v j g ∈ p

def mon (ρ : NRep F n K) (j : ℤ) : Set K :=
  {x | ∃ L : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n), (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = j ∧
    x = (L.map fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod}

end NRep

def Irr {H K : Type} [Field K] {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (r : H → Matrix ι ι K) : Prop :=
  ∀ U : Submodule K (ι → K), (∀ g, ∀ u ∈ U, r g *ᵥ u ∈ U) → U = ⊥ ∨ U = ⊤

def adicClosure {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R] (S : Subalgebra O R) :
    Subalgebra O R where
  carrier := {x | ∀ N : ℕ, ∃ y ∈ S, x - y ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ N}
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb N := by
    obtain ⟨x, hx, h1⟩ := ha N
    obtain ⟨y, hy, h2⟩ := hb N
    refine ⟨x * y, mul_mem hx hy, ?_⟩
    have : a * b - x * y = (a - x) * b + x * (b - y) := by ring
    rw [this]
    exact add_mem (Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ h1) (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ h2)
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb N := by
    obtain ⟨x, hx, h1⟩ := ha N
    obtain ⟨y, hy, h2⟩ := hb N
    refine ⟨x + y, add_mem hx hy, ?_⟩
    rw [add_sub_add_comm]
    exact add_mem h1 h2
  algebraMap_mem' c N := ⟨_, S.algebraMap_mem c, by simp⟩

theorem le_adicClosure {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R] (S : Subalgebra O R) :
    S ≤ adicClosure S := fun x hx _ => ⟨x, hx, by simp⟩

class IsCoef (l : outParam ℕ) (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] : Prop where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O
  fin : Finite (ResidueField O)
  char : CharP (ResidueField O) l

attribute [instance] IsCoef.complete IsCoef.fin IsCoef.char

section Space

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (n : ℕ)

variable {F n} in

def IsLift {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (ρ : NRep F n R) : Prop :=
  ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal R) ∧ (ρ.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O)).IsBar (RingHom.id _) ∧
    (ρ.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O)).IsDiag

structure Space : Type 1 where
  R : Type
  [cr : CommRing R]
  [al : Algebra O R]
  [co : IsCO O R]
  ρ : NRep F n R
  lift : IsLift O ρ
  univ : ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (ρ' : NRep F n C), IsLift O ρ' →
    ∃! f : R →ₐ[O] C, ρ.map (f : R →+* C) = ρ'

attribute [instance] Space.cr Space.al Space.co

namespace Space

variable {O F n} (S : Space O F n)

def B : Subalgebra O S.R :=
  adicClosure (Algebra.adjoin O (S.ρ.mon 0 ∪ {x | ∃ v i g, x = (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R)}))

def A : Subalgebra O S.R := Algebra.adjoin O ((S.B : Set S.R) ∪ {x | ∃ g i j, x = S.ρ.r g i j})

theorem B_le_A : S.B ≤ S.A := fun _ hx => Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl hx)

theorem ψ_mem (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) : (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R) ∈ S.B :=
  le_adicClosure _ (Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨v, i, g, rfl⟩))

theorem mon_mem {x : S.R} (hx : x ∈ S.ρ.mon 0) : x ∈ S.B := le_adicClosure _ (Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl hx))

theorem r_mem (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin n) : S.ρ.r g i j ∈ S.A := Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨g, i, j, rfl⟩)

def inc : S.B →ₐ[O] S.A := Subalgebra.inclusion S.B_le_A

instance : Algebra S.B S.A := (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A).toAlgebra

instance : IsScalarTower O S.B S.A := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl

def u : NRep F n S.A := S.ρ.restrict S.A S.r_mem fun v i g => S.B_le_A (S.ψ_mem v i g)

def ψB (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) : S.B := ⟨S.ρ.ψ v i g, S.ψ_mem v i g⟩

def aB (g : F.G0) (i : Fin n) : S.B := ⟨S.ρ.r g i i, S.mon_mem ⟨[(g, i, i)], by simp, by simp⟩⟩

def trB (g : F.G0) : S.B := ∑ i, S.aB g i

def mB : Ideal S.B := (maximalIdeal S.R).comap (S.B.val : S.B →+* S.R)

def π (p : Ideal S.A) : Ideal S.B := p.comap (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A)

def gr (j : ℤ) : Submodule S.B S.A := Submodule.span S.B (S.u.mon j)

def IsHom (I : Ideal S.A) : Prop := I = Ideal.span {x | x ∈ I ∧ ∃ j, x ∈ S.gr j}

def lamId : Ideal S.B :=
  (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O S.B) ⊔
    Ideal.span {x | ∃ v i g, g ∈ F.Iv v ∧ F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1 ∧ x = S.ψB v i g - 1}

def FinLam (I : Ideal S.B) : Prop := S.mB ≤ (I ⊔ S.lamId).radical

def BB : Set (PrimeSpectrum S.A) := {p | S.mB ≤ S.π p.asIdeal ∧ ¬ S.u.triP ≤ p.asIdeal}

structure Loc (x : Ideal S.A) : Type 1 where
  O' : Type
  [cO : CommRing O']

namespace Loc

variable {S} {x : Ideal S.A} (L : S.Loc x)

def ρ : NRep F n L.R := S.u.map (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R)

def im (P : Ideal L.R) : Ideal S.A := P.comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R)

def π (P : Ideal L.R) : Ideal S.B := S.π (L.im P)

end Loc

end Space

end Space

structure IsThickMod {F : Frame l k} {Q : Type} [CommRing Q] (ρ : NRep F 3 Q) (r : F.G0 →* Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) Q) :
    Prop where
  even : ∀ g i j, ¬ Odd (w 3 i - w 3 j) → r g i j = ρ.r g i j
  sub : ∀ g, r g 1 0 = 0 ∧ r g 1 2 = 0
  ord : ∀ v g, ordProd (fun s => r (F.loc0 v s)) (fun i s => (ρ.ψ v i s : Q)) g = 0


[APD_SRC_FRAME] Full original Frame, EC and Ok; original finite-field Euler scope
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ContinuousCoboundaries2.lean#L124-L214
  [tgv : ∀ v, IsTopologicalGroup (Gv v)]
  [cv : ∀ v, CompactSpace (Gv v)]
  [t2v : ∀ v, T2Space (Gv v)]
  [tdv : ∀ v, TotallyDisconnectedSpace (Gv v)]
  loc0 : ∀ v, Gv v →* del.ker
  Iv : ∀ v, Subgroup (Gv v)
  dv : V → ℕ
  Inf : Type
  [fI : Fintype Inf]
  cw : Inf → Γ
  eps : Γ →* ℤ_[l]ˣ
  Ψ : Γ →* kˣ
  g0 : del.ker

attribute [instance] Frame.grp Frame.top Frame.tg Frame.cpt Frame.t2 Frame.td Frame.fV Frame.gv Frame.tv Frame.tgv
  Frame.cv Frame.t2v Frame.tdv Frame.fI

namespace Frame

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (F : Frame l k)

abbrev G0 : Type := F.del.ker

def loc (v : F.V) : F.Gv v →* F.Γ := F.del.ker.subtype.comp (F.loc0 v)

def conj : F.G0 →* F.G0 := (MulAut.conjNormal F.c : MulAut F.G0).toMonoidHom

def star (g : F.G0) : F.G0 := F.conj g⁻¹

def loc2 (p : F.V × Bool) : F.Gv p.1 →* F.G0 := if p.2 then F.conj.comp (F.loc0 p.1) else F.loc0 p.1

def d : ℕ := ∑ v, F.dv v

def om (K : Type) [CommRing K] [CharP K l] : F.Γ →* Kˣ :=
  (Units.map ((ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) K).comp PadicInt.toZMod).toMonoidHom).comp F.eps

def delK (K : Type) [CommRing K] : F.Γ →* Kˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom K).toMonoidHom).comp F.del

def Nv (v : F.V) : Subgroup (F.Gv v) := F.Iv v ⊓ (F.eps.comp (F.loc v)).ker

def ext {k' : Type} [Field k'] (f : k →+* k') : Frame l k' :=
  { F with Ψ := (Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp F.Ψ }

def EC : Prop :=
  ∀ (K W : Type) [Field K] [Finite K] [CharP K l] [AddCommGroup W] [Module K W] [Module.Finite K W]
    (ρ : Representation K F.Γ W) (b : F.V → Submodule K W), IsOpen (ρ.ker : Set F.Γ) →
    (∀ v s, ∀ X ∈ b v, ρ (F.loc v s) X ∈ b v) →
    (∀ f : W →ₗ[K] K, (∀ g X, f (ρ g X) = F.om K g * f X) → f = 0) →
    Module.Finite K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) ∧
    Module.finrank K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) + Module.finrank K (coneH0 ρ b) + ∑ v, F.dv v * Module.finrank K (b v) ≤
      Module.finrank K (H1C F.loc ρ b) + ∑ w, Module.finrank K (LinearMap.ker (ρ (F.cw w) - 1))

structure Ok [CharP k l] (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  fin : Finite k
  del_open : IsOpen (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)
  c_sq : F.c * F.c = 1
  del_c : F.del F.c = -1
  loc_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (F.loc0 v)
  eps_cont : Continuous F.eps
  Ψ_open : IsOpen (F.Ψ.ker : Set F.Γ)
  Ψ_c : F.Ψ F.c = 1
  baseSubgroup_spec : F.Ψ F.g0 ^ 2 ≠ 1
  phi : PhiCond F.Γ l
  card_inf : Fintype.card F.Inf = F.d
  cw_sq : ∀ w, F.cw w * F.cw w = 1
  del_cw : ∀ w, F.del (F.cw w) = -1
  f2a : ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g ≠ 1
  f2b : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j ≠ 1
  f2c : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ g, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j * F.delK k g ≠ 1
  f3 : ∀ v, ∃ g, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ 2 ≠ 1
  f4 : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f4' : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ g : F.G0, F.om k g = F.Ψ g ^ j
  Ψ_N : ∀ v, ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1

  gen : ∀ v, ∃ (u : Fin (F.dv v) → F.Gv v) (a₀ : Fin (F.dv v)), (∀ a, u a ∈ F.Iv v ∧ F.Ψ (F.loc v (u a)) = 1) ∧
    (∀ a, a ≠ a₀ → u a ∈ F.Nv v) ∧
    ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.Gv v →* A, IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (F.Gv v)) →
      (∀ a, χ (u a) = 1) → ∀ g ∈ F.Iv v, χ g = 1

  cl : ∃ h : ℕ, h ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.G0 →* A,
    IsOpen (χ.ker : Set F.G0) → (∀ p : F.V × Bool, ∀ g ∈ F.Iv p.1, χ (F.loc2 p g) = 1) → ∀ g, χ g ^ h = 1
  ec : F.EC
  b1p : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  b1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc2 (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹.comp F.del.ker.subtype)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  h1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (1 : F.Γ →* kˣ)) fun _ => ⊤) ≤ 1 + C₀

def Big (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop := 3 * C₀ + 5 + 2 < F.d ∧ ∀ v, 5 < F.dv v

end Frame


[APD_SRC_COCHAINS] Exact strict cocycles, boundaries, H1C, coneH0 and coneH2
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ContinuousCoboundaries2.lean#L11-L111

variable {k G W V : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W]
  {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* G) (ρ : Representation k G W) (b : V → Submodule k W)

def coneH0 : Submodule k W where
  carrier := {X | (∀ g, ρ g X = X) ∧ ∀ v, X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2⟩ ⟨b1, b2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_add, a1, b1], fun v => add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v)⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨fun g => map_zero _, fun v => zero_mem _⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_smul, a1], fun v => Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v)⟩

def Z1C : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ s t, x.1 (s * t) = x.1 s + ρ s (x.1 t)) ∧
    ∀ v s, x.1 (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (x.2 v) - x.2 v) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun s t => ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun s t => by simp [a2], fun v s => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries1 : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ X : W, (∀ s, x.1 s = ρ s X - X) ∧ ∀ v, x.2 v - X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨X, a1, a2⟩ ⟨Y, b1, b2⟩
    refine ⟨X + Y, fun s => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a1, b1, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨X, a1, a2⟩
    refine ⟨c • X, fun s => by simp [a1, smul_sub], fun v => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

abbrev H1C : Type := Z1C loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ b).comap (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype

def continuousCocycles2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ v, IsLocallyConstant (x.2 v)) ∧
    (∀ s t u, ρ s (x.1 (t, u)) + x.1 (s, t * u) = x.1 (s * t, u) + x.1 (s, t)) ∧
    ∀ v s t, x.1 (loc v s, loc v t) - dOne (ρ.comp (loc v)) (x.2 v) (s, t) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3, b4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun v => (a2 v).add (b2 v), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]
      rw [add_add_add_comm, a3, b3, add_add_add_comm]
    · convert add_mem (a4 v s t) (b4 v s t) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, dOne, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, fun _ => IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp [dOne]⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun v => (a2 v).comp (c • ·), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.smul_fst, Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, ← smul_add, a3]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a4 v s t) using 1
      simp [dOne, smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ (β : G → W) (Y : V → W), IsLocallyConstant β ∧ x.1 = dOne ρ β ∧
    ∀ v s, x.2 v s - (β (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (Y v) - Y v)) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨β', Y', b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨β + β', Y + Y', a1.add b1, ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, dOne, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, 0, IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by funext p; simp [dOne], by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨c • β, c • Y, a1.comp (c • ·), ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp [a2, dOne, smul_sub]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
      simp [smul_sub]

abbrev coneH2 : Type := continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries2 loc ρ b).comap (continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b).subtype

end Cone

def chRep {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) : Representation k G k where
  toFun g := (χ g : k) • LinearMap.id
  map_one' := by ext; simp
  map_mul' g h := by ext; simp [mul_comm]

@[simp] theorem chRep_apply {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) (g : G) (x : k) :
    chRep χ g x = χ g * x := rfl

structure Frame (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] : Type 1 where
  Γ : Type
  [grp : Group Γ]


[APD_SRC_CONE] Original IsTr, IsPsi, Integral and all five Cone clauses
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/Integral.lean#L11-L48
variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ}

variable (O F n) in

def IsTr {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (t : F.G0 → C) : Prop :=
  AdicCont (maximalIdeal C) t ∧ ∀ g, res O C (t g) = ∑ i, ((F.Ψ g ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)

variable (O F n) in

def IsPsi {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (χ : ∀ v, Fin n → F.Gv v → C) : Prop :=
  ∀ v i, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C) (χ v i) ∧
    ∀ g, res O C (χ v i g) = ((F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)

variable (O) in

structure Integral (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {K : Type} [CommRing K] [Algebra C K]
    (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop where
  tr : ∃ t : F.G0 → C, IsTr O F n t ∧ ∀ g, algebraMap C K (t g) = (ρ.r g).trace
  psi : ∃ χ : ∀ v, Fin n → F.Gv v → C, IsPsi O F n χ ∧ ∀ v i g, algebraMap C K (χ v i g) = ρ.ψ v i g
  ze : ∃ ζ : C, res O C ζ = (F.Ψ F.g0 : ResidueField O) - ((F.Ψ F.g0)⁻¹ : (ResidueField O)ˣ) ∧
    ∀ i, (ρ.r F.g0 - ρ.r (F.star F.g0)) i i = (w n i : K) * algebraMap C K ζ

variable (O) in

structure Cone (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {K : Type} [CommRing K] [Algebra C K]
    (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop where
  dg : ∀ g i, ∃ c : C, algebraMap C K c = ρ.r g i i ∧
    res O C c = ((F.Ψ g ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)
  cyc : ∀ L : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n), (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0 →
    (∃ s ∈ L, s.2.1 ≠ s.2.2) → ∃ c ∈ maximalIdeal C, algebraMap C K c = (L.map fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod
  fg : ∀ i j, (Submodule.span C (Set.range fun g => ρ.r g i j)).FG
  cont : ∀ i j (N : ℕ), IsLocallyConstant fun g =>
    (maximalIdeal C ^ N • Submodule.span C (Set.range fun g => ρ.r g i j)).mkQ (ρ.r g i j)
  psi : ∃ χ : ∀ v, Fin n → F.Gv v → C, IsPsi O F n χ ∧ ∀ v i g, algebraMap C K (χ v i g) = ρ.ψ v i g

variable (O) in

def IsResMap (C : Type) [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] [Algebra O C] (f : ResidueField O →+* ResidueField C) : Prop :=


[APD_SRC_PRES_CONTRACT] Original IsResMap, local contraction, Pres and separate EPres record
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/Integral.lean#L50-L87

namespace Space

variable (S : Space O F n)

def x0 : Ideal S.A := S.mB.map (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A)

def td (η : Ideal S.A) : Cardinal := Algebra.trdeg (S.B ⧸ η.comap (algebraMap S.B S.A)) (S.A ⧸ η)

namespace Loc

variable {S} {x : Ideal S.A} (L : S.Loc x)

def b : S.B →+* L.R := (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R).comp (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A)

def Pres : Prop :=
  ∃ (N N' : ℕ) (p : MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O' →ₐ[L.O'] L.R) (f : Fin N' → MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O'),
    N = N' + (n * F.d + 1) ∧ Function.Surjective p ∧ RingHom.ker p = Ideal.span (Set.range f)

def par (a g : ℕ) : Fin a ⊕ Fin g → ZMod 2 := Sum.elim (fun _ => 0) fun _ => 1

structure EPres where
  a : ℕ
  b : ℕ
  g : ℕ
  p : MvPowerSeries (Fin a ⊕ Fin g) L.O' →ₐ[L.O'] L.R
  Fe : Fin b → MvPowerSeries (Fin a ⊕ Fin g) L.O'
  Go : Fin g → MvPowerSeries (Fin a ⊕ Fin g) L.O'
  num : a = b + (n * F.d + 1)
  surj : Function.Surjective p
  ker : RingHom.ker p = Ideal.span (Set.range Fe ∪ Set.range Go)
  even : ∀ i, Fe i ∈ wtPart L.O' (par a g) 0
  odd : ∀ i, Go i ∈ wtPart L.O' (par a g) 1
  min : ∀ f ∈ RingHom.ker p, ∀ s, MvPowerSeries.coeff (Finsupp.single s 1) f ∈ maximalIdeal L.O'
  ent : ∀ s i j, L.ρ.r s i j ∈ (wtPart L.O' (par a g) ((w n i - w n j : ℤ) : ZMod 2)).map p.toLinearMap
  base : ∀ y : S.B, L.b y ∈ (wtPart L.O' (par a g) 0).map p.toLinearMap
  ee : L.ρ.ee = Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => p (MvPowerSeries.X (.inr i)))



[APD_SRC_BOX_DEFS] Original Box, Of, Good and NRep.Univ
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PresDefs.lean#L9-L112
def UpT {C : Type} [Zero C] {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) : Prop := ∀ i j, j < i → M i j = 0

def bor {K : Type} [CommRing K] {n : ℕ} (g : GL (Fin n) K) : Submodule K (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) where
  carrier := {X | UpT ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * X * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K))}
  add_mem' {X Y} hX hY i j h := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.add_apply, hX i j h, hY i j h, add_zero]
  zero_mem' i j _ := by simp
  smul_mem' c X hX i j h := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.smul_apply, hX i j h, smul_zero]

def lieT (K : Type) [CommRing K] (n : ℕ) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K := Matrix.diagonal fun i => ((w n i : ℤ) : K)

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k]

@[ext] structure Box (F : Frame l k) (n : ℕ) (C : Type) [CommRing C] where
  r : F.Γ →* GG n C
  nu : ∀ s, (r s).right = F.del s
  g : F.V → GL (Fin n) C
  tri : ∀ v s, UpT ((g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * (r (F.loc v s)).mat *
    (((g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C))

variable {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ}

namespace Box

variable {C C' : Type} [CommRing C] [CommRing C']

def map (f : C →+* C') (B : Box F n C) : Box F n C' where
  r := (GG.map f).comp B.r
  nu := B.nu
  g v := GeneralLinearGroup.map f (B.g v)
  tri v s i j h := by
    have e : ((GeneralLinearGroup.map f (B.g v) : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') *
        (((GG.map f).comp B.r) (F.loc v s)).mat *
        (((GeneralLinearGroup.map f (B.g v))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') =
        f.mapMatrix ((B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * (B.r (F.loc v s)).mat *
          (((B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)) := by
      rw [map_mul, map_mul, ← map_inv]
      rfl
    rw [e]
    exact (congrArg f (B.tri v s i j h)).trans (map_zero f)

theorem map_map {C'' : Type} [CommRing C''] (f : C →+* C') (f' : C' →+* C'') (B : Box F n C) :
    (B.map f).map f' = B.map (f'.comp f) := rfl

def Cont (I : Ideal C) (B : Box F n C) : Prop := ∀ i j, AdicCont I fun s => (B.r s).mat i j

structure Of (B : Box F n C) (ρ : NRep F n C) : Prop where
  atc : (B.r F.c).mat = 1
  res : ∀ s : F.G0, (B.r s).mat = ρ.r s
  psi : ∀ v i s, ((B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * ρ.r (F.loc0 v s) *
    (((B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)) i i = ρ.ψ v i s

theorem Of.map {B : Box F n C} {ρ : NRep F n C} (h : B.Of ρ) (f : C →+* C') : (B.map f).Of (ρ.map f) where
  atc := by
    show ((B.r F.c).mat).map f = 1
    rw [h.atc]
    exact Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero f) (map_one f)
  res s := by
    show ((B.r s).mat).map f = _
    rw [h.res]
    rfl
  psi v i s := by
    have e : (((B.map f).g v : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') * (ρ.map f).r (F.loc0 v s) *
        ((((B.map f).g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') =
        f.mapMatrix ((B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * ρ.r (F.loc0 v s) *
          (((B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)) := by
      rw [map_mul, map_mul]
      rfl
    rw [e]
    exact congrArg f (h.psi v i s)

def ad (B : Box F n C) : Representation C F.Γ (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) := adRep B.r

def b (B : Box F n C) (v : F.V) : Submodule C (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) := bor (B.g v)

end Box

structure NRep.Good {K : Type} [Field K] (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop where
  two : (2 : K) ≠ 0
  zd : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → (ρ.r F.g0 - ρ.r (F.star F.g0)) i i ≠ (ρ.r F.g0 - ρ.r (F.star F.g0)) j j
  ψd : ∀ v, ∃ s, ∀ i j, i ≠ j → ρ.ψ v i s ≠ ρ.ψ v j s

section CO

variable (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]

abbrev NRep.bar {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (ρ : NRep F n C) : NRep F n (ResidueField O) :=
  ρ.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O)

abbrev Box.bar {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (B : Box F n C) : Box F n (ResidueField O) :=
  B.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O)

structure NRep.Univ {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (ρ : NRep F n R) : Prop where
  cont : ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal R)
  univ : ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (ρ' : NRep F n C), ρ'.Cont (maximalIdeal C) →
    ρ'.bar O = ρ.bar O → ∃! g : R →ₐ[O] C, ρ.map (g : R →+* C) = ρ'

abbrev Tan (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] : Submodule (ResidueField O) (R →ₗ[O] ResidueField O) :=
  PtDer (res O R) (ResidueField O)

def Box.tanCo {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (B : Box F n R) :
    (R →ₗ[O] ResidueField O) →ₗ[ResidueField O] (F.Γ → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) where
  toFun D s := (B.r s).mat.map D *


[APD_SRC_OBS_DEFS] Exact tangent, square-zero map, SetLift, HasObs and IsMinPres
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PresDefs.lean#L114-L189
  map_add' D D' := funext fun s => by
    rw [Pi.add_apply, ← Matrix.add_mul]
    rfl
  map_smul' c D := funext fun s => by
    rw [Pi.smul_apply, RingHom.id_apply, ← Matrix.smul_mul]
    rfl

structure IsSq {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (μ : ResidueField O →+ C) : Prop where
  mul : ∀ a c, μ (res O C a * c) = a * μ c
  mem : ∀ c, μ c ∈ maximalIdeal C

variable {O}

open Classical in

def evK {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (K : Ideal C) (u : K →ₗ[O] ResidueField O) (x : C) : ResidueField O :=
  if h : x ∈ K then u ⟨x, h⟩ else 0

theorem evK_of_mem {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] {K : Ideal C} (u : K →ₗ[O] ResidueField O) {x : C}
    (h : x ∈ K) : evK K u x = u ⟨x, h⟩ := dif_pos h

variable {C' C : Type} [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] [CommRing C] [Algebra O C]

structure SetLift (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (B : Box F n C) where
  r : F.Γ → GL (Fin n) C'
  r_cont : ∀ i j, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C') fun s => r s i j
  r_map : ∀ s, GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (r s) = (B.r s).left
  g : F.V → GL (Fin n) C'
  g_map : ∀ v, GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (g v) = B.g v
  t : ∀ v, F.Gv v → GL (Fin n) C'
  t_cont : ∀ v i j, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C') fun s => t v s i j
  t_tri : ∀ v s, UpT (t v s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C')
  t_map : ∀ v s, GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (t v s) = B.g v * (B.r (F.loc v s)).left * (B.g v)⁻¹

namespace SetLift

variable {p : C' →ₐ[O] C} {B : Box F n C} (D : SetLift p B)

def om (x : F.Γ × F.Γ) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' :=
  ((D.r x.1 * sgnAct n C' (F.del x.1) (D.r x.2) * (D.r (x.1 * x.2))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') - 1

def a (v : F.V) (s : F.Gv v) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' :=
  ((D.r (F.loc v s) * ((D.g v)⁻¹ * D.t v s * D.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') - 1

def co : (RingHom.ker p →ₗ[O] ResidueField O) →ₗ[ResidueField O]
    (F.Γ × F.Γ → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) × (∀ v, F.Gv v → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) where
  toFun u := (fun x => (D.om x).map (evK _ u), fun v s => (D.a v s).map (evK _ u))
  map_add' u u' := by
    have e : ∀ x, evK _ (u + u') x = evK _ u x + evK _ u' x := fun x => by
      unfold evK
      split_ifs <;> simp
    ext <;> simp [e]
  map_smul' c u := by
    have e : ∀ x, evK _ (c • u) x = c * evK _ u x := fun x => by
      unfold evK
      split_ifs <;> simp
    ext <;> simp [e]

end SetLift

variable (O) in

def HasObs (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (H : Type) [AddCommGroup H] [Module (ResidueField O) H] : Prop :=
  ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (p : C' →ₐ[O] R), Function.Surjective p →
    maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ →
    ∃ ob : (RingHom.ker p →ₗ[O] ResidueField O) →ₗ[ResidueField O] H, ∀ u, ob u = 0 →
      ∀ (C'' : Type) [CommRing C''] [Algebra O C''] [IsCO O C''] (q : C' →ₐ[O] C'') (p'' : C'' →ₐ[O] R),
        p''.comp q = p → Function.Surjective q → (∀ y : RingHom.ker p, u y = 0 → q y = 0) →
        ∃ s : R →ₐ[O] C'', p''.comp s = AlgHom.id O R

def IsMinPres {σ R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (p : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] R) : Prop :=
  Function.Surjective p ∧ ∀ f ∈ RingHom.ker p, ∀ s, MvPowerSeries.coeff (Finsupp.single s 1) f ∈ maximalIdeal O

end CO

end UpperTheory.V.Fr


[APD_SRC_TAN] Full pr_tan, including dual-number cardinality and arbitrary local O
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/TanFinite.lean#L1-L93
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.PresDefs

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

section
variable {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]

private theorem hom_eq_res (g : R →ₐ[O] ResidueField O) (x : R) : g x = res O R x := by
  have hsurj : Function.Surjective g := by
    intro y
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective y
    exact ⟨algebraMap O R a, by rw [g.commutes, IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq]⟩
  have hker : RingHom.ker (g : R →+* ResidueField O) = maximalIdeal R :=
    IsLocalRing.eq_maximalIdeal (RingHom.ker_isMaximal_of_surjective _ hsurj)
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
  have h1 : g (x - algebraMap O R a) = 0 := by
    have hm : x - algebraMap O R a ∈ RingHom.ker (g : R →+* ResidueField O) := by
      rw [hker]
      exact ha
    exact (RingHom.mem_ker).1 hm
  have h2 : res O R (x - algebraMap O R a) = 0 := (res_eq_zero_iff O R _).2 ha
  rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h1 h2
  rw [h1, h2, AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes]

private theorem tan_mul (D : Tan O R) (f g : R) :
    D.1 (f * g) = res O R f * D.1 g + res O R g * D.1 f := D.2 f g

private theorem tan_one (D : Tan O R) : D.1 1 = 0 := by
  simpa using tan_mul D 1 1

private theorem tan_alg (D : Tan O R) (a : O) : D.1 (algebraMap O R a) = 0 := by
  rw [Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one, map_smul, tan_one, smul_zero]

private theorem tan_finite : Module.Finite (ResidueField O) (Tan O R) := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := (IsNoetherian.noetherian (maximalIdeal R) : (maximalIdeal R).FG)
  let φ : Tan O R →ₗ[ResidueField O] (s → ResidueField O) :=
    { toFun := fun D i => D.1 (i : R)
      map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
      map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
  refine Module.Finite.of_injective φ ((injective_iff_map_eq_zero φ).2 fun D hD => ?_)
  have h0 : ∀ i ∈ s, D.1 i = 0 := fun i hi => congrFun hD ⟨i, hi⟩
  have hm : ∀ y ∈ maximalIdeal R, D.1 y = 0 := by
    intro y hy
    rw [← hs] at hy
    induction hy using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem z hz => exact h0 z hz
    | zero => exact map_zero _
    | add a b _ _ ha hb => rw [map_add, ha, hb, add_zero]
    | smul c z hz ih =>
      have hz' : z ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
        rw [← hs]
        exact hz
      rw [smul_eq_mul, tan_mul, ih, (res_eq_zero_iff O R z).2 hz', mul_zero, zero_mul, add_zero]
  apply Subtype.ext
  apply LinearMap.ext
  intro x
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
  have h := hm _ ha
  rw [map_sub, tan_alg, sub_zero] at h
  exact h

private noncomputable def tanEquiv : (R →ₐ[O] DualNumber (ResidueField O)) ≃ Tan O R where
  toFun f := ⟨{ toFun := fun x => (f x).snd
                map_add' := fun x y => by simp
                map_smul' := fun a x => by simp }, fun x y => by
    have h : ∀ z, (f z).fst = res O R z := fun z =>
      hom_eq_res ((TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom O (ResidueField O) (ResidueField O)).comp f) z
    show (f (x * y)).snd = res O R x • (f y).snd + res O R y • (f x).snd
    rw [map_mul, DualNumber.snd_mul, h, h, smul_eq_mul, smul_eq_mul, mul_comm (f x).snd]⟩
  invFun D :=
    { toFun := fun x => TrivSqZeroExt.inl (res O R x) + TrivSqZeroExt.inr (D.1 x)
      map_one' := by ext <;> simp [tan_one]
      map_mul' := fun x y => by ext <;> simp [tan_mul, mul_comm]
      map_zero' := by ext <;> simp
      map_add' := fun x y => by ext <;> simp
      commutes' := fun a => by
        ext
        · simp [TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl']
        · simp [tan_alg, TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl'] }
  left_inv f := by
    have h : ∀ z, (f z).fst = res O R z := fun z =>
      hom_eq_res ((TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom O (ResidueField O) (ResidueField O)).comp f) z
    refine AlgHom.ext fun x => ?_
    ext <;> simp [h]
  right_inv D := Subtype.ext (LinearMap.ext fun x => by simp)

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_tan :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R], Module.Finite (ResidueField O) (Tan O R) ∧ Nat.card (R →ₐ[O] (DualNumber (ResidueField O))) = Nat.card (Tan O R) := by
  intro O _ _ R _ _ _
  exact ⟨tan_finite, Nat.card_congr tanEquiv⟩


[APD_SRC_MINIMAL] Minimal-presentation, relation-detection and tangent-count algebra; APD does not claim full obs_pres
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/ObsPres.lean#L11-L305
private theorem op_min {A ι : Type} [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [IsNoetherianRing A] [Fintype ι]
    (K K0 : Ideal A) (f : ι → A) (h : K = K0 ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range f)) :
    ∃ s : Finset ι, K = K0 ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range fun u : s => f u) ∧
      ∀ c : s → A, ∑ u, c u * f u ∈ K0 ⊔ maximalIdeal A * K → ∀ u, c u ∈ maximalIdeal A := by
  classical
  have hex : ∃ n, ∃ s : Finset ι, s.card = n ∧ K = K0 ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range fun u : s => f u) := by
    refine ⟨_, Finset.univ, rfl, ?_⟩
    rw [h]
    congr 2
    ext x
    simp
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hK⟩ := Nat.find_spec hex
  refine ⟨s, hK, fun c hc u => ?_⟩
  by_contra hu
  have hun : IsUnit (c u) := by
    by_contra h'
    exact hu h'
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hun
  refine Nat.find_min hex (m := (s.erase u).card) (by rw [← hs]; exact Finset.card_erase_lt_of_mem u.2)
    ⟨s.erase u, rfl, ?_⟩
  set N : Ideal A := K0 ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range fun w : s.erase u => f w) with hN
  have hmem : ∀ w : s, w ≠ u → f w ∈ N := fun w hw =>
    Ideal.mem_sup_right (Ideal.subset_span ⟨⟨w, Finset.mem_erase.2 ⟨fun e => hw (Subtype.ext e), w.2⟩⟩, rfl⟩)
  have hNK : N ≤ K := by
    rw [hK]
    refine sup_le_sup_left (Ideal.span_mono ?_) _
    rintro _ ⟨w, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨⟨w, Finset.mem_of_mem_erase w.2⟩, rfl⟩
  refine le_antisymm ?_ hNK
  refine Submodule.le_of_le_smul_of_le_jacobson_bot (I := maximalIdeal A) (IsNoetherian.noetherian K)
    (maximalIdeal_le_jacobson _) ?_
  have h1 : f u ∈ N ⊔ maximalIdeal A • K := by
    have h2 : ∑ w, c w * f w ∈ N ⊔ maximalIdeal A • K :=
      (sup_le_sup_right (le_sup_left : K0 ≤ N) (maximalIdeal A * K)) hc
    rw [← Finset.add_sum_erase _ _ (Finset.mem_univ u)] at h2
    have h3 : ∑ w ∈ Finset.univ.erase u, c w * f w ∈ N ⊔ maximalIdeal A • K :=
      Ideal.mem_sup_left (Ideal.sum_mem _ fun w hw =>
        Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ (hmem w (Finset.mem_erase.1 hw).1))
    have h4 := Ideal.mul_mem_left _ (↑v⁻¹ : A) (sub_mem h2 h3)
    rwa [add_sub_cancel_right, ← hv, Units.inv_mul_cancel_left] at h4
  conv_lhs => rw [hK]
  refine sup_le (le_sup_of_le_left le_sup_left) (Ideal.span_le.2 ?_)
  rintro _ ⟨w, rfl⟩
  by_cases hw : w = u
  · subst hw; exact h1
  · exact Ideal.mem_sup_left (hmem w hw)

private theorem op_mem_max {O σ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (x : MvPowerSeries σ O) :
    x ∈ maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries σ O) ↔ constantCoeff x ∈ maximalIdeal O := by
  rw [mem_maximalIdeal, mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, mem_nonunits_iff, isUnit_iff_constantCoeff]

private theorem op_loc {O R R' : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]
    [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (x : R) :
    f x ∈ maximalIdeal R' ↔ x ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]

private theorem op_loc_sq {O R R' : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]
    [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f : R →ₐ[O] R') {x : R} (hx : x ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ 2) :
    f x ∈ maximalIdeal R' ^ 2 := by
  have h : (maximalIdeal R).map f ≤ maximalIdeal R' :=
    Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.2 fun y hy => (op_loc f y).2 hy
  have := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem f hx
  rw [Ideal.map_pow] at this
  exact Ideal.pow_right_mono h 2 this

private theorem op_taylor {O σ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Fintype σ] (g : MvPowerSeries σ O) :
    ∃ c : σ → O, g - algebraMap O _ (constantCoeff g) - ∑ s, c s • X s ∈ maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries σ O) ^ 2 := by
  obtain ⟨k, -, hk⟩ := GlobalTheory.exists_eq_sum_X_mul (n := 0) (g := g - C (constantCoeff g)) fun d hd => by
    have : d = 0 := (Finsupp.degree_eq_zero_iff d).1 (by simpa using hd)
    subst this
    simp
  refine ⟨fun s => constantCoeff (k s), ?_⟩
  rw [show algebraMap O (MvPowerSeries σ O) (constantCoeff g) = C (constantCoeff g) from rfl, hk,
    ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  refine Ideal.sum_mem _ fun s _ => ?_
  have : X s * k s - constantCoeff (k s) • X s = X s * (k s - C (constantCoeff (k s))) := by
    rw [smul_eq_C_mul]; ring
  rw [this, pow_two]
  refine Ideal.mul_mem_mul ?_ ?_ <;> rw [op_mem_max] <;> simp

private theorem op_detect {O B A R κ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing B] [Algebra O B] [IsCO O B]
    [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCO O A] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [Fintype κ]
    (i : B →ₐ[O] A) (p : A →ₐ[O] R) (hm : maximalIdeal A * RingHom.ker p = ⊥)
    (hker : RingHom.ker p ≤ (maximalIdeal B).map i ⊔ maximalIdeal A ^ 2)
    (ε : κ → A) (hε : ∀ u, p (ε u) = 0) (hind : ∀ c : κ → O, ∑ u, c u • ε u = 0 → ∀ u, c u ∈ maximalIdeal O)
    (c : κ → O)
    (H : ∀ (A' : Type) [CommRing A'] [Algebra O A'] [IsCO O A'] (p' : A →ₐ[O] A') (p'' : A' →ₐ[O] R) (η : A'),
      Function.Surjective p' → p''.comp p' = p → (∀ x ∈ maximalIdeal A', x * η = 0) → p'' η = 0 →
      (∀ u, p' (ε u) = c u • η) → ∃ s : R →ₐ[O] A', p''.comp s = AlgHom.id O R ∧ s.comp (p.comp i) = p'.comp i) :
    ∀ u, c u ∈ maximalIdeal O := by
  classical
  intro u0
  by_contra h0
  obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := (notMem_maximalIdeal.1 h0).exists_right_inv
  have hz : ∀ x ∈ maximalIdeal A, ∀ y, p y = 0 → x * y = 0 := fun x hx y hy => by
    have := Ideal.mul_mem_mul hx (show y ∈ RingHom.ker p from hy)
    rwa [hm] at this
  let δ : κ → A := fun u => ε u - (c u * d) • ε u0
  have hδ : ∀ u, p (δ u) = 0 := fun u => by simp [δ, hε]
  let I : Ideal A := Ideal.span (Set.range δ)
  have hI : I ≤ RingHom.ker p := Ideal.span_le.2 (by rintro _ ⟨u, rfl⟩; exact hδ u)
  let p'' : (A ⧸ I) →ₐ[O] R := Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ I p hI
  let p' : A →ₐ[O] A ⧸ I := Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I
  have hp' : Function.Surjective p' := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
  have hpp : ∀ x, p'' (p' x) = p x := fun x => rfl
  haveI : Nontrivial (A ⧸ I) := p''.toRingHom.domain_nontrivial
  haveI : IsCO O (A ⧸ I) := co_quot O A _ p' hp'
  obtain ⟨s, hs1, hs2⟩ := H (A ⧸ I) p' p'' (p' (d • ε u0)) hp' (AlgHom.ext hpp)
    (fun x hx => by
      obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := hp' x
      rw [← map_mul, hz x ((op_loc p' x).1 hx) _ (by simp [hε]), map_zero])
    (by rw [hpp]; simp [hε])
    (fun u => by
      rw [← map_smul, smul_smul]
      exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.2 (Ideal.subset_span ⟨u, rfl⟩))
  have hs1' : ∀ x, p'' (s x) = x := fun x => DFunLike.congr_fun hs1 x
  have hs : Function.Surjective s := by
    refine co_surj O R _ s fun x hx => ?_
    obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hp' (x - s (p'' x))
    have h1 : y ∈ RingHom.ker p := by
      show p y = 0
      rw [← hpp, hy, map_sub, hs1', sub_self]
    have h2 : x = s (p'' x) + p' y := by rw [hy]; ring
    rw [h2]
    refine add_mem (Ideal.mem_sup_left (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ((op_loc p'' x).2 hx))) ?_
    obtain ⟨a, ha, b, hb, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.1 (hker h1)
    rw [map_add]
    refine Submodule.add_mem_sup ?_ (op_loc_sq p' hb)
    have h3 : (maximalIdeal B).map i ≤ ((maximalIdeal R).map s).comap p' :=
      Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.2 fun t ht => by
        show p' (i t) ∈ (maximalIdeal R).map s
        have h4 : s (p (i t)) = p' (i t) := DFunLike.congr_fun hs2 t
        rw [← h4]
        exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ((op_loc p _).2 ((op_loc i _).2 ht))
    exact h3 ha
  have h5 : ε u0 ∈ I := by
    obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hs (p' (ε u0))
    have : y = 0 := by rw [← hs1' y, hy, hpp, hε]
    rw [this, map_zero] at hy
    exact Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.1 hy.symm
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun.1 h5
  choose b hb using fun u => IsCO.res_surj (O := O) (a u)
  have h6 : ∑ u, b u • δ u = ε u0 := by
    rw [← ha]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun u _ => ?_
    have := hz _ (hb u) _ (hδ u)
    rw [sub_mul, sub_eq_zero] at this
    rw [this, Algebra.smul_def]
  have h7 := hind (fun u => b u - if u = u0 then 1 + ∑ v, b v * (c v * d) else 0) (by
    simp only [sub_smul, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ite_smul, zero_smul, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, if_true,
      add_smul, one_smul, Finset.sum_smul]
    simp only [δ, smul_sub, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, smul_smul] at h6
    linear_combination h6)
  have h8 : ∑ u, (b u - if u = u0 then 1 + ∑ v, b v * (c v * d) else 0) * (c u * d) ∈ maximalIdeal O :=
    Ideal.sum_mem _ fun u _ => Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (h7 u)
  simp only [sub_mul, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ite_mul, zero_mul, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, if_true,
    hd, mul_one] at h8
  rw [sub_add_cancel_right, neg_mem_iff] at h8
  exact (maximalIdeal.isMaximal O).ne_top ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).2 h8)

private theorem op_ker {O B A R σ σB τ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [CommRing B] [Algebra O B] [IsCO O B]
    [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCO O A] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [Fintype σ] [Fintype σB] [Fintype τ]
    (e : σB ⊕ τ ≃ σ) (i : B →ₐ[O] A) (p : A →ₐ[O] R) (π : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] A) (hπ : Function.Surjective π)
    (h1 : ∀ s, ∃ b ∈ maximalIdeal B, π (X (e (.inl s))) = i b)
    (h2 : ∀ c : τ → O, ∑ t, c t • p (π (X (e (.inr t)))) ∈ (maximalIdeal B).map (p.comp i) ⊔ maximalIdeal R ^ 2 →
      ∀ t, c t ∈ maximalIdeal O) :
    RingHom.ker p ≤ (maximalIdeal B).map i ⊔ maximalIdeal A ^ 2 := by
  haveI : IsCO O (MvPowerSeries σ O) := co_mvps O O σ
  intro x hx
  obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hπ x
  have hx' : p (π g) = 0 := hx
  have hg : constantCoeff g ∈ maximalIdeal O := by
    rw [← op_mem_max, ← op_loc π, ← op_loc p, hx']
    exact zero_mem _
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := op_taylor g
  have key : ∀ (T : Type) [CommRing T] [Algebra O T] [IsCO O T] (q : A →ₐ[O] T),
      q (π g) - ∑ t, c (e (.inr t)) • q (π (X (e (.inr t)))) ∈
        (maximalIdeal B).map (q.comp i) ⊔ maximalIdeal T ^ 2 := by
    intro T _ _ _ q
    have k1 := op_loc_sq (q.comp π) hc
    rw [map_sub, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, map_sum, ← e.sum_comp, Fintype.sum_sum_type] at k1
    simp only [map_smul, AlgHom.comp_apply] at k1
    have k2 : algebraMap O T (constantCoeff g) ∈ (maximalIdeal B).map (q.comp i) := by
      rw [← (q.comp i).commutes]
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ((op_loc (Algebra.ofId O B) _).2 hg)
    have k3 : ∑ s, c (e (.inl s)) • q (π (X (e (.inl s)))) ∈ (maximalIdeal B).map (q.comp i) := by
      refine Ideal.sum_mem _ fun s _ => ?_
      obtain ⟨b, hb, hb'⟩ := h1 s
      rw [hb', Algebra.smul_def]
      exact Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem (q.comp i) hb)
    have := Submodule.add_mem_sup (add_mem k2 k3) k1
    convert this using 1
    ring
  have k4 := key R p
  rw [hx', zero_sub, neg_mem_iff] at k4
  have k5 := h2 _ k4
  have k6 := key A (AlgHom.id O A)
  simp only [AlgHom.id_apply, AlgHom.id_comp] at k6
  have k7 : ∑ t, c (e (.inr t)) • π (X (e (.inr t))) ∈ maximalIdeal A ^ 2 := by
    refine Ideal.sum_mem _ fun t _ => ?_
    rw [Algebra.smul_def, pow_two]
    refine Ideal.mul_mem_mul ((op_loc (Algebra.ofId O A) _).2 (k5 t)) ((op_loc π _).2 ?_)
    rw [op_mem_max]; simp
  simpa using add_mem k6 (Ideal.mem_sup_right k7)

private theorem op_min_fin {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [IsNoetherianRing A]
    (K K0 : Ideal A) (h : K0 ≤ K) :
    ∃ (n : ℕ) (f : Fin n → A), K = K0 ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range f) ∧
      ∀ c : Fin n → A, ∑ u, c u * f u ∈ K0 ⊔ maximalIdeal A * K → ∀ u, c u ∈ maximalIdeal A := by
  obtain ⟨m, g, hg⟩ := Submodule.fg_iff_exists_fin_generating_family.1 (IsNoetherian.noetherian K)
  have hg' : Ideal.span (Set.range g) = K := hg
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hmin⟩ := op_min K K0 g (by rw [hg', sup_eq_right.2 h])
  let e := (Fintype.equivFin s).symm
  refine ⟨_, fun u => g (e u), ?_, fun c hc u => ?_⟩
  · rw [hs]
    congr 2
    exact (e.surjective.range_comp fun u : s => g u).symm
  · have := hmin (fun v => c (e.symm v)) (by
      rw [← e.sum_comp]
      simpa using hc) (e u)
    simpa using this

private theorem op_min_pres (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] :
    ∃ (a : ℕ) (π : MvPowerSeries (Fin a) O →ₐ[O] R), Function.Surjective π ∧
      RingHom.ker π ≤ (maximalIdeal O).map (Algebra.ofId O (MvPowerSeries (Fin a) O)) ⊔ maximalIdeal _ ^ 2 := by
  obtain ⟨a, z, hz, hmin⟩ := op_min_fin (maximalIdeal R) ((maximalIdeal O).map (Algebra.ofId O R))
    (Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.2 fun x hx => (op_loc (Algebra.ofId O R) x).2 hx)
  haveI : IsCO O (MvPowerSeries (Fin a) O) := co_mvps O O _
  have hzm : ∀ s, z s ∈ maximalIdeal R := fun s => hz ▸ Ideal.mem_sup_right (Ideal.subset_span ⟨s, rfl⟩)
  obtain ⟨π, ⟨-, hX⟩, -⟩ := co_ev O O R (Fin a) (Algebra.ofId O R) z hzm
  refine ⟨a, π, co_surj O _ R π (le_sup_of_le_left ?_), ?_⟩
  · rw [hz]
    refine sup_le (Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.2 fun x hx => ?_) (Ideal.span_le.2 ?_)
    · show algebraMap O R x ∈ (maximalIdeal _).map π
      rw [← π.commutes]
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ((op_loc (Algebra.ofId O _) x).2 hx)
    · rintro _ ⟨s, rfl⟩
      rw [← hX]
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ (by rw [op_mem_max]; simp)
  · refine op_ker (Equiv.emptySum Empty (Fin a)) (Algebra.ofId O _) π (AlgHom.id O _) Function.surjective_id
      (fun s => s.elim) fun c hc t => ?_
    rw [← op_loc (Algebra.ofId O R)]
    refine hmin (fun t => algebraMap O R (c t)) ?_ t
    rw [Subsingleton.elim (π.comp _) (Algebra.ofId O R), pow_two] at hc
    simpa [hX, Algebra.smul_def] using hc

private theorem op_count (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]
    (a : ℕ) (π : MvPowerSeries (Fin a) O →ₐ[O] R) (hπ : Function.Surjective π)
    (hk : RingHom.ker π ≤ (maximalIdeal O).map (Algebra.ofId O (MvPowerSeries (Fin a) O)) ⊔ maximalIdeal _ ^ 2) :
    Nat.card (R →ₐ[O] DualNumber (ResidueField O)) = Nat.card (ResidueField O) ^ a := by
  haveI : IsCO O (MvPowerSeries (Fin a) O) := co_mvps O O _
  haveI : IsCO O (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) := (co_res O).2
  have hm : ∀ x : DualNumber (ResidueField O),
      x ∈ maximalIdeal (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) ↔ x.fst = 0 := fun x => by
    rw [mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, TrivSqZeroExt.isUnit_iff_isUnit_fst, isUnit_iff_ne_zero, not_not]
  have hsq : maximalIdeal (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) ^ 2 ≤ ⊥ := by
    rw [sq, Ideal.mul_le]
    intro x hx y hy
    rw [hm] at hx hy
    rw [Ideal.mem_bot]
    ext <;> simp [hx, hy]
  have hev := fun v : Fin a → ResidueField O => co_ev O O (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) (Fin a) (Algebra.ofId O _)
    (fun s => TrivSqZeroExt.inr (v s)) fun s => (hm _).2 (by simp)
  have hb : Function.Bijective fun (f : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber (ResidueField O)) (s : Fin a) => (f (π (X s))).snd := by
    have hX : ∀ (f : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber (ResidueField O)) (s : Fin a),
        (f.comp π) (X s) = TrivSqZeroExt.inr (f (π (X s))).snd := fun f s => by
      refine TrivSqZeroExt.ext ?_ rfl
      exact (hm _).1 ((op_loc (f.comp π) _).2 (by rw [op_mem_max]; simp))
    refine ⟨fun f g hfg => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
    · have : f.comp π = g.comp π := (hev fun s => (f (π (X s))).snd).unique
        ⟨fun x => AlgHom.commutes _ x, hX f⟩ ⟨fun x => AlgHom.commutes _ x, fun s => by
          rw [hX g]; exact congrArg _ (congrFun hfg s).symm⟩
      ext1 x
      obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := hπ x
      exact DFunLike.congr_fun this x
    · obtain ⟨F, ⟨-, hF⟩, -⟩ := hev v
      have hle : ∀ x ∈ RingHom.ker π, F x = 0 := fun x hx => by
        obtain ⟨y, hy, w, hw, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.1 (hk hx)
        have h1 : F w = 0 := hsq (op_loc_sq F hw)
        have h2 : (maximalIdeal O).map (Algebra.ofId O (MvPowerSeries (Fin a) O)) ≤ RingHom.ker F :=
          Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.2 fun c hc => by
            show F (algebraMap O _ c) = 0
            rw [F.commutes, IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O (ResidueField O), ResidueField.algebraMap_eq,
              (residue_eq_zero_iff c).2 hc, map_zero]
        rw [map_add, h1, add_zero]
        exact h2 hy
      refine ⟨AlgHom.liftOfSurjective π hπ F hle, funext fun s => ?_⟩
      show (AlgHom.liftOfSurjective π hπ F hle (π (X s))).snd = v s
      exact congrArg TrivSqZeroExt.snd ((AlgHom.liftOfSurjective_apply π hπ F hle _).trans (hF s))
  rw [Nat.card_eq_of_bijective _ hb, Nat.card_fun]
  simp



[APD_SRC_DUALB] Full pr_dualb for every original finite minimal kernel family
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrDualb.lean#L5-L45
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_dualb :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (K : Ideal C) (κ : Type) [Fintype κ] (ε : κ → C), maximalIdeal C * K = ⊥ → K = Ideal.span (Set.range ε) → (∀ c : κ → O, ∑ u, c u • ε u = 0 → ∀ u, c u ∈ maximalIdeal O) → ∃ co : κ → K →ₗ[O] ResidueField O, ∀ y : K, ∃ a : κ → O, (∀ u, residue O (a u) = co u y) ∧ (y : C) = ∑ u, a u • ε u := by
  intro O _ _ C _ _ _ K κ _ ε hK hspan hind
  have hε : ∀ u, ε u ∈ K := fun u => by
    rw [hspan]; exact Ideal.subset_span ⟨u, rfl⟩
  have hex : ∀ y : K, ∃ a : κ → O, (y : C) = ∑ u, a u • ε u := by
    rintro ⟨y, hy⟩
    have hy' : y ∈ Ideal.span (Set.range ε) := hspan ▸ hy
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun.1 hy'
    choose a ha using fun u => IsCO.res_surj (O := O) (c u)
    refine ⟨a, ?_⟩
    show y = _
    rw [← hc]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun u _ => ?_
    have h0 : (c u - algebraMap O C (a u)) * ε u ∈ maximalIdeal C * K :=
      Ideal.mul_mem_mul (ha u) (hε u)
    rw [hK, Ideal.mem_bot, sub_mul, sub_eq_zero] at h0
    rw [h0, Algebra.smul_def]
  have huniq : ∀ a b : κ → O, ∑ u, a u • ε u = ∑ u, b u • ε u →
      ∀ u, residue O (a u) = residue O (b u) := by
    intro a b hab u
    have h := hind (fun u => a u - b u) (by
      simp only [sub_smul, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, hab, sub_self]) u
    rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, residue_eq_zero_iff]
    exact h
  choose a ha using hex
  refine ⟨fun u =>
    { toFun := fun y => residue O (a y u)
      map_add' := fun x y => ?_
      map_smul' := fun c x => ?_ }, fun y => ⟨a y, fun u => rfl, ha y⟩⟩
  · rw [← map_add]
    refine huniq (a (x + y)) (fun u => a x u + a y u) ?_ u
    simp only [add_smul, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    rw [← ha, ← ha, ← ha]
    rfl
  · have h := huniq (a (c • x)) (fun u => c * a x u) (by
      simp only [mul_smul, ← Finset.smul_sum]
      rw [← ha, ← ha]
      rfl) u
    rw [h, RingHom.id_apply, map_mul, Algebra.smul_def]
    rfl


[APD_SRC_MAZUR] Full pr_mazur with exact tangent variables and obstruction-dimension relations
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrMazur.lean#L8-L52
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_mazur :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [Finite (ResidueField O)] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (H : Type) [AddCommGroup H] [Module (ResidueField O) H] [Module.Finite (ResidueField O) H], HasObs O R H → ∃ (p : MvPowerSeries (Fin (Module.finrank (ResidueField O) (Tan O R))) O →ₐ[O] R) (f : Fin (Module.finrank (ResidueField O) H) → MvPowerSeries (Fin (Module.finrank (ResidueField O) (Tan O R))) O), Function.Surjective p ∧ RingHom.ker p = Ideal.span (Set.range f) := by
  intro O _ _ _ _ _ R _ _ _ H _ _ _ hob
  obtain ⟨a, π, f, hπ, hker, hcard⟩ := obs_pres O R H (by
    intro A _ _ _ p hp hK κ _ ε hspan hmin
    obtain ⟨ob, hob'⟩ := hob A p hp hK
    obtain ⟨co, hco⟩ := pr_dualb O A (RingHom.ker p) κ ε hK hspan hmin
    refine ⟨fun u => ob (co u), ?_⟩
    intro c hc A' _ _ _ p' p'' η hp' hcomp hη hη0 hε
    have hw0 : ob (∑ u, residue O (c u) • co u) = 0 := by
      rw [map_sum]
      simp only [map_smul]
      exact hc
    refine hob' _ hw0 A' p' p'' hcomp hp' ?_
    intro y hy
    obtain ⟨b, hb, hy'⟩ := hco y
    rw [hy', map_sum]
    simp only [map_smul, hε, smul_smul]
    rw [← Finset.sum_smul]
    have hm : ∑ u, b u * c u ∈ maximalIdeal O := by
      rw [← residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sum]
      simp only [map_mul, hb]
      simp only [LinearMap.sum_apply, LinearMap.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul] at hy
      rw [← hy]
      exact Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun u _ => mul_comm _ _)
    haveI := IsCO.isLocalHom (O := O) (R := A')
    rw [Algebra.smul_def]
    exact hη _ (map_nonunit (algebraMap O A') _ hm))
  have hT := (pr_tan O R).2
  haveI := (pr_tan O R).1
  classical
  haveI := Fintype.ofFinite (ResidueField O)
  haveI : Finite (Tan O R) := Module.finite_of_finite (ResidueField O)
  haveI := Fintype.ofFinite (Tan O R)
  have h2 : 2 ≤ Fintype.card (ResidueField O) := Fintype.one_lt_card
  have hTc : Nat.card (Tan O R) =
      Fintype.card (ResidueField O) ^ Module.finrank (ResidueField O) (Tan O R) := by
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card]
    exact Module.card_eq_pow_finrank
  have ha : a = Module.finrank (ResidueField O) (Tan O R) := by
    apply Nat.pow_right_injective h2
    change Fintype.card (ResidueField O) ^ a = Fintype.card (ResidueField O) ^ _
    rw [← hTc, ← hT, hcard, Nat.card_eq_fintype_card]
  subst ha
  exact ⟨π, f, hπ, hker⟩


[APD_SRC_COUNT] Full pr_count over arbitrary fields and topological groups
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrCount.lean#L5-L90
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_count :
    ∀ {K G W V T : Type} [Field K] [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [AddCommGroup W] [Module K W] [Module.Finite K W] [AddCommGroup T] [Module K T] [Module.Finite K T] {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* G) (ρ : Representation K G W) (b : V → Submodule K W) (φ : T →ₗ[K] (G → W)), (∀ v Y, (∀ s, ρ (loc v s) Y - Y ∈ b v) → Y ∈ b v) → (∀ x ∈ Z1C loc ρ b, ∃ D X, ∀ s, φ D s = x.1 s + (ρ s X - X)) → Module.finrank K (H1C loc ρ b) ≤ Module.finrank K T ∧ ∀ (X₀ : W) (D₀ : T), X₀ ≠ 0 → (∀ s, φ D₀ s = ρ s X₀ - X₀) → Module.finrank K (H1C loc ρ b) + 1 ≤ Module.finrank K T + Module.finrank K (coneH0 ρ b) := by
  intro K G W V T _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ Gv _ loc ρ b φ hA hB
  let cob : W →ₗ[K] (G → W) := LinearMap.pi (fun s => ρ s - LinearMap.id)
  have hcob : ∀ X s, cob X s = ρ s X - X := fun X s => rfl
  let C := LinearMap.range cob
  let f : T →ₗ[K] (G → W) ⧸ C := C.mkQ ∘ₗ φ
  let Ψ : Z1C loc ρ b →ₗ[K] (G → W) ⧸ C :=
    C.mkQ ∘ₗ (LinearMap.fst K (G → W) (V → W)) ∘ₗ (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype
  have hle : Submodule.comap (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ b) ≤ LinearMap.ker Ψ := by
    rintro ⟨x, hx⟩ hxB
    rw [Submodule.mem_comap] at hxB
    obtain ⟨X, hX, -⟩ := hxB
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker]
    show C.mkQ x.1 = 0
    rw [Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero]
    exact ⟨X, funext fun s => ((hX s).trans (hcob X s).symm).symm⟩
  have hker : LinearMap.ker Ψ ≤ Submodule.comap (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ b) := by
    rintro ⟨x, hx⟩ hk
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at hk
    change C.mkQ x.1 = 0 at hk
    rw [Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero] at hk
    obtain ⟨X, hX⟩ := hk
    obtain ⟨-, -, hloc⟩ := hx
    rw [Submodule.mem_comap]
    refine ⟨X, fun s => (congrFun hX s).symm, fun v => ?_⟩
    have hmem : X - x.2 v ∈ b v := by
      refine hA v _ (fun s => ?_)
      have h1 := hloc v s
      convert h1 using 1
      rw [← hX, hcob, map_sub]
      abel
    have h2 := (b v).neg_mem hmem
    rwa [neg_sub] at h2
  let L := (Submodule.comap (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ b)).liftQ Ψ hle
  have hinj : Function.Injective L := by
    rw [← LinearMap.ker_eq_bot]
    exact Submodule.ker_liftQ_eq_bot _ _ _ hker
  have hrange : ∀ y, L y ∈ LinearMap.range f := by
    intro y
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ y
    obtain ⟨D, X, hD⟩ := hB z.1 z.2
    refine ⟨D, ?_⟩
    show C.mkQ (φ D) = C.mkQ z.1.1
    rw [Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.eq]
    refine ⟨X, funext fun s => ?_⟩
    show cob X s = φ D s - z.1.1 s
    rw [hcob, hD]
    abel
  let L' : H1C loc ρ b →ₗ[K] LinearMap.range f := L.codRestrict _ hrange
  have hinj' : Function.Injective L' := fun a c h => hinj (congrArg Subtype.val h)
  have h1 : Module.finrank K (H1C loc ρ b) ≤ Module.finrank K (LinearMap.range f) :=
    LinearMap.finrank_le_finrank_of_injective hinj'
  have h2 := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker f
  have h3 := LinearMap.finrank_range_le f
  refine ⟨h1.trans h3, ?_⟩
  intro X₀ D₀ hX₀ hD₀
  by_cases hinv : ∀ s, ρ s X₀ = X₀
  · have hH0 : X₀ ∈ coneH0 ρ b := by
      show (∀ g, ρ g X₀ = X₀) ∧ ∀ v, X₀ ∈ b v
      exact ⟨hinv, fun v => hA v X₀ (fun s => by rw [hinv, sub_self]; exact (b v).zero_mem)⟩
    have h4 : Module.finrank K (coneH0 ρ b) ≠ 0 := by
      rw [Ne, Submodule.finrank_eq_zero]
      intro h
      rw [h] at hH0
      exact hX₀ ((Submodule.mem_bot K).1 hH0)
    omega
  · push_neg at hinv
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hinv
    have hD0 : D₀ ∈ LinearMap.ker f := by
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker]
      show C.mkQ (φ D₀) = 0
      rw [Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero]
      exact ⟨X₀, funext fun t => (hcob X₀ t).trans (hD₀ t).symm⟩
    have hne : D₀ ≠ 0 := by
      rintro rfl
      apply hs
      have h5 := hD₀ s
      rw [map_zero, Pi.zero_apply] at h5
      exact (sub_eq_zero.1 h5.symm)
    have h4 : Module.finrank K (LinearMap.ker f) ≠ 0 := by
      rw [Ne, Submodule.finrank_eq_zero]
      intro h
      rw [h] at hD0
      exact hne ((Submodule.mem_bot K).1 hD0)
    omega


[APD_SRC_CL_SUB] Full cl_sub and residue-constant subalgebra construction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebraHeight/ClSub.lean#L1-L339
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebraHeight.IsLocalHom
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.ModuliProofs.IsNoetherianRingOfIsAdicComplete

open IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.CA ReciprocityTheory.CA

private theorem exists_subalgebra_of_residue_constants (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] :
    ∃ S : Subalgebra O R, ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  refine ⟨{ carrier := {c | ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R}
            mul_mem' := ?_, one_mem' := ?_, add_mem' := ?_, zero_mem' := ?_,
            algebraMap_mem' := ?_ }, fun c => Iff.rfl⟩
  · rintro x y ⟨a, ha⟩ ⟨b, hb⟩
    refine ⟨a * b, ?_⟩
    have e : x * y - algebraMap O R (a * b)
        = (x - algebraMap O R a) * y + algebraMap O R a * (y - algebraMap O R b) := by
      rw [map_mul]; ring
    rw [e]
    exact Ideal.add_mem _ (Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ ha) (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hb)
  · exact ⟨1, by simp⟩
  · rintro x y ⟨a, ha⟩ ⟨b, hb⟩
    refine ⟨a + b, ?_⟩
    have e : x + y - algebraMap O R (a + b)
        = (x - algebraMap O R a) + (y - algebraMap O R b) := by
      rw [map_add]; ring
    rw [e]
    exact Ideal.add_mem _ ha hb
  · exact ⟨0, by simp⟩
  · intro a
    exact ⟨a, by simp⟩

private theorem maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R)
    (x : R) (hx : x ∈ maximalIdeal R) : x ∈ S :=
  (hS x).2 ⟨0, by simpa using hx⟩

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_isUnit_iff (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R)
    (c : S) : IsUnit c ↔ (c : R) ∉ maximalIdeal R := by
  constructor
  · intro hc hm
    have : IsUnit (c : R) := hc.map S.val
    exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).1 hm this
  · intro hc
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := (hS c).1 c.2
    have hu : IsUnit (c : R) := by
      by_contra h
      exact hc ((IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).2 h)
    have ha' : IsUnit (algebraMap O R a) := by
      by_contra h
      have h1 : algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R := (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).2 h
      apply hc
      have e : (c : R) = (c - algebraMap O R a) + algebraMap O R a := by ring
      rw [e]
      exact Ideal.add_mem _ ha h1
    have hau : IsUnit a := IsUnit.of_map (algebraMap O R) a ha'
    obtain ⟨u, hu'⟩ := hu
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hau
    have hmem : (↑u⁻¹ : R) ∈ S := by
      refine (hS _).2 ⟨↑v⁻¹, ?_⟩
      have e : (↑u⁻¹ : R) - algebraMap O R ↑v⁻¹
          = (↑u⁻¹ : R) * algebraMap O R ↑v⁻¹ * (algebraMap O R a - (c : R)) := by
        have h1 : (↑u⁻¹ : R) * (c : R) = 1 := by rw [← hu']; simp
        have h2 : algebraMap O R ↑v⁻¹ * algebraMap O R a = 1 := by
          rw [← map_mul, ← hv]; simp
        linear_combination algebraMap O R ↑v⁻¹ * h1 - (↑u⁻¹ : R) * h2
      rw [e]
      apply Ideal.mul_mem_left
      have : algebraMap O R a - (c : R) = -((c : R) - algebraMap O R a) := by ring
      rw [this]
      exact (Ideal.neg_mem_iff _).2 ha
    refine isUnit_iff_exists_inv.2 ⟨⟨_, hmem⟩, ?_⟩
    apply Subtype.ext
    simp [← hu']

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_isLocalRing (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) :
    IsLocalRing S := by
  haveI : Nontrivial S := ⟨⟨0, 1, fun h => zero_ne_one (congrArg Subtype.val h)⟩⟩
  refine IsLocalRing.of_nonunits_add ?_
  intro a b ha hb
  rw [mem_nonunits_iff, residue_constant_subalgebra_isUnit_iff O R S hS, not_not] at ha hb ⊢
  simpa using Ideal.add_mem _ ha hb

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) [IsLocalRing S]
    (c : S) : c ∈ maximalIdeal S ↔ (c : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  rw [IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, residue_constant_subalgebra_isUnit_iff O R S hS, not_not]

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) [IsLocalRing S]
    (N : ℕ) (c : S) : c ∈ maximalIdeal S ^ N ↔ (c : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ N := by
  constructor
  · intro hc
    have h1 : Ideal.map S.val.toRingHom (maximalIdeal S) ≤ maximalIdeal R := by
      rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
      intro x hx
      exact (residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS x).1 hx
    have h2 := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem S.val.toRingHom hc
    rw [Ideal.map_pow] at h2
    exact Ideal.pow_right_mono h1 N h2
  · induction N generalizing c with
    | zero => intro _; simp
    | succ n ih =>
      intro hc
      rcases n with _ | n
      · simpa [residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS] using hc
      · rw [pow_succ'] at hc ⊢
        have key : ∀ x : R, x ∈ maximalIdeal R * maximalIdeal R ^ (n + 1) →
            ∃ hx : x ∈ S, (⟨x, hx⟩ : S) ∈ maximalIdeal S * maximalIdeal S ^ (n + 1) := by
          intro x hx
          refine Submodule.mul_induction_on hx ?_ ?_
          · intro i hi j hj
            have hjm : j ∈ maximalIdeal R := Ideal.pow_le_self (Nat.succ_ne_zero n) hj
            have hiS := maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS i hi
            have hjS := maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS j hjm
            refine ⟨S.mul_mem hiS hjS, ?_⟩
            have : (⟨i * j, S.mul_mem hiS hjS⟩ : S) = ⟨i, hiS⟩ * ⟨j, hjS⟩ := rfl
            rw [this]
            exact Ideal.mul_mem_mul ((residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS _).2 hi) (ih ⟨j, hjS⟩ hj)
          · rintro x y ⟨hx, hx'⟩ ⟨hy, hy'⟩
            exact ⟨S.add_mem hx hy, Ideal.add_mem _ hx' hy'⟩
        obtain ⟨_, h⟩ := key _ hc
        exact h

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_module_finite (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [Module.Finite O (ResidueField R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) :
    Module.Finite S R := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := Module.finite_def.1 (inferInstance : Module.Finite O (ResidueField R))
  let g := Function.surjInv (IsLocalRing.residue_surjective (R := R))
  have hg : ∀ z, IsLocalRing.residue R (g z) = z := Function.surjInv_eq _
  refine ⟨⟨insert 1 (s.image g), ?_⟩⟩
  rw [eq_top_iff]
  intro x _
  let f := (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O R (ResidueField R)).toLinearMap
  have hf : ∀ r, f r = IsLocalRing.residue R r := fun r => rfl
  have hx : IsLocalRing.residue R x ∈ Submodule.map f (Submodule.span O (g '' (s : Set _))) := by
    rw [Submodule.map_span, ← Set.image_comp]
    have : (f ∘ g) '' (s : Set _) = s := by
      ext z; simp [Function.comp, hf, hg]
    rw [this, hs]; trivial
  obtain ⟨y, hy, hxy⟩ := hx
  rw [hf] at hxy
  have hm : x - y ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
    rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sub, hxy, sub_self]
  have hmS := maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS _ hm
  have e : x = (⟨x - y, hmS⟩ : S) • (1 : R) + y := by
    rw [Subalgebra.smul_def]; simp
  rw [e]
  refine Submodule.add_mem _ (Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span (by simp))) ?_
  have hle : Submodule.span O (g '' (s : Set _))
      ≤ (Submodule.span S (↑(insert 1 (s.image g)) : Set R)).restrictScalars O := by
    refine le_trans (Submodule.span_le_restrictScalars O S _) ?_
    exact Submodule.restrictScalars_mono O (Submodule.span_mono (by simp [Set.subset_insert]))
  exact hle hy

private theorem isNoetherian_of_finite_torsion_by_maximalIdeal (A : Type) [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] (P : Type) [AddCommGroup P]
    [Module A P] [Module.Finite A P]
    (h : Module.IsTorsionBySet A P (maximalIdeal A : Set A)) : IsNoetherian A P := by
  letI : Module (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P := h.module
  haveI : IsScalarTower A (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P := h.isScalarTower
  letI : Field (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) := Ideal.Quotient.field _
  haveI : Module.Finite (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P := Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite A _ _
  haveI : IsNoetherian (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P := inferInstance
  refine ⟨fun N => ?_⟩
  let N' : Submodule (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P :=
    { carrier := N
      add_mem' := fun hx hy => N.add_mem hx hy
      zero_mem' := N.zero_mem
      smul_mem' := by
        intro c x hx
        obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective c
        have : (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal A) a) • x = a • x := by
          rw [← Ideal.Quotient.algebraMap_eq, algebraMap_smul]
        rw [this]
        exact N.smul_mem a hx }
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := IsNoetherian.noetherian N'
  refine ⟨t, ?_⟩
  rw [← Submodule.restrictScalars_span A (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective, ht]
  rfl

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_isNoetherian (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] [IsNoetherianRing R] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) [IsLocalRing S]
    [Module.Finite S R] : IsNoetherian S R := by
  refine ⟨fun N => ?_⟩
  let J : Submodule R R := Submodule.span R (N : Set R)
  let J' : Submodule S R := J.restrictScalars S
  let L' : Submodule S R := (maximalIdeal R • J).restrictScalars S
  have hJfg : J'.FG := Submodule.FG.restrictScalars (IsNoetherian.noetherian J)
  have hLfg : L'.FG := Submodule.FG.restrictScalars (IsNoetherian.noetherian (maximalIdeal R • J))
  have hNJ : N ≤ J' := by
    intro x hx
    rw [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem]
    exact Submodule.subset_span hx
  have hLJ : L' ≤ J' := by
    intro x hx
    rw [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem] at hx ⊢
    exact Submodule.smul_le_right hx
  have hJm : ∀ j ∈ J, ∀ y ∈ maximalIdeal R, y * j ∈ N := by
    intro j hj
    induction hj using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem x hx =>
      intro y hy
      have : y * x = (⟨y, maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS y hy⟩ : S) • x := by
        rw [Subalgebra.smul_def, smul_eq_mul]
      rw [this]
      exact N.smul_mem _ hx
    | zero => intro y _; simp
    | add x z _ _ hx hz =>
      intro y hy
      rw [mul_add]
      exact N.add_mem (hx y hy) (hz y hy)
    | smul r x _ hx =>
      intro y hy
      have : y * (r • x) = (y * r) * x := by rw [smul_eq_mul]; ring
      rw [this]
      exact hx _ (Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ hy)
  have hLN : L' ≤ N := by
    intro x hx
    rw [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem] at hx
    refine Submodule.smul_induction_on hx ?_ ?_
    · intro y hy j hj
      rw [smul_eq_mul]
      exact hJm j hj y hy
    · intro x z hx hz
      exact N.add_mem hx hz
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hJfg
  let K : Submodule S R := Submodule.span S (t : Set R)
  have hKJ : ∀ x ∈ K, x ∈ J := by
    intro x hx
    have : x ∈ J' := by rw [← ht]; exact hx
    rwa [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem] at this
  have hNK : N ≤ K := by
    intro x hx
    have := hNJ hx
    rw [← ht] at this
    exact this
  have hLK : L' ≤ K := by
    intro x hx
    have := hLJ hx
    rw [← ht] at this
    exact this
  haveI : Module.Finite S K := Module.Finite.iff_fg.2 ⟨t, rfl⟩
  let L0 : Submodule S K := L'.comap K.subtype
  let N0 : Submodule S K := N.comap K.subtype
  have hL0fg : L0.FG := by
    refine Submodule.fg_of_fg_map_injective K.subtype Subtype.val_injective ?_
    rw [Submodule.map_comap_subtype, inf_eq_right.2 hLK]
    exact hLfg
  have tors : Module.IsTorsionBySet S (K ⧸ L0) (maximalIdeal S : Set S) := by
    intro p x
    induction p using Submodule.Quotient.induction_on with
    | H j =>
      rw [← Submodule.Quotient.mk_smul, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero]
      change ((x : S) : R) • (j : R) ∈ (maximalIdeal R • J).restrictScalars S
      rw [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem]
      exact Submodule.smul_mem_smul ((residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS _).1 x.2) (hKJ _ j.2)
  haveI : IsNoetherian S (K ⧸ L0) := isNoetherian_of_finite_torsion_by_maximalIdeal S (K ⧸ L0) tors
  have hN0 : N0.FG := by
    refine Submodule.fg_of_fg_map_of_fg_inf_ker L0.mkQ (IsNoetherian.noetherian _) ?_
    rw [Submodule.ker_mkQ, inf_eq_right.2]
    · exact hL0fg
    · intro x hx
      exact hLN hx
  have e : Submodule.map K.subtype N0 = N := by
    rw [Submodule.map_comap_subtype, inf_eq_right.2 hNK]
  rw [← e]
  exact hN0.map _

private theorem smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem (A : Type) [CommRing A] (I : Ideal A) (x y : A) :
    x ≡ y [SMOD I • (⊤ : Submodule A A)] ↔ x - y ∈ I := by
  rw [SModEq.sub_mem, smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top]

private theorem isAdicComplete_residue_constant_subalgebra (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal R) R]
    (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) [IsLocalRing S] :
    IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal S) S := by
  refine { haus' := ?_, prec' := ?_ }
  · intro x hx
    apply Subtype.ext
    refine IsHausdorff.haus' (I := maximalIdeal R) (x : R) (fun n => ?_)
    rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem]
    have := hx n
    rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem, residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff O R S hS] at this
    simpa using this
  · intro f hf
    obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := IsPrecomplete.prec' (I := maximalIdeal R) (fun n => (f n : R)) (by
      intro m n hmn
      rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem]
      have := hf hmn
      rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem, residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff O R S hS] at this
      simpa using this)
    have h1 : (f 1 : R) - L ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
      have := hL 1
      rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem, pow_one] at this
      exact this
    have hLS : L ∈ S := by
      have e : L = (f 1 : R) - ((f 1 : R) - L) := by ring
      rw [e]
      exact S.sub_mem (f 1).2 (maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS _ h1)
    refine ⟨⟨L, hLS⟩, fun n => ?_⟩
    rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem, residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff O R S hS]
    have := hL n
    rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem] at this
    simpa using this

theorem UpperTheory.V.CA.cl_sub :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsLocalRing R], IsCL O R → ∃ S : Subalgebra O R, (∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) ∧ Module.Finite S R ∧ ∃ _ : IsCO O S, ∀ (N : ℕ) (c : S), c ∈ maximalIdeal S ^ N ↔ (c : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ N := by
  intro O _ _ _ _ R _ _ _ hR
  haveI := hR.noeth
  haveI := hR.complete
  haveI := hR.isLocalHom
  haveI := hR.res_fin
  obtain ⟨S, hS⟩ := exists_subalgebra_of_residue_constants O R
  haveI : IsLocalRing S := residue_constant_subalgebra_isLocalRing O R S hS
  haveI : Module.Finite S R := residue_constant_subalgebra_module_finite O R S hS
  haveI : IsNoetherian S R := residue_constant_subalgebra_isNoetherian O R S hS
  haveI : IsNoetherian S S :=
    isNoetherian_of_injective (Algebra.linearMap S R) Subtype.val_injective
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal S) S := isAdicComplete_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS
  refine ⟨S, hS, inferInstance,
