    { complete := inferInstance, isLocalHom := ?_, res_surj := ?_ },
    fun N c => residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff O R S hS N c⟩
  · refine ⟨fun a ha => ?_⟩
    have h1 : IsUnit (algebraMap O R a) := by
      have := ha.map S.val
      simpa using this
    exact IsUnit.of_map _ _ h1
  · intro x
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := (hS x).1 x.2
    exact ⟨a, (residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS _).2 (by simpa using ha)⟩


[APD_SRC_TRI_BAR] Broader IsBar triangularity in both ranks and vanishing of mixed weight-zero cycles
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PointLoc.lean#L42-L269

private theorem or_zero {G K : Type} [Field K] {a b : G → K} (h : ∀ g g', a g * b g' = 0) :
    (∀ g, a g = 0) ∨ ∀ g, b g = 0 := by
  by_contra hc
  push_neg at hc
  obtain ⟨⟨g, hg⟩, ⟨g', hg'⟩⟩ := hc
  exact mul_ne_zero hg hg' (h g g')

private theorem tri_of_bar {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}
    (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) {K : Type} [Field K] (f : k →+* K) (ρ : NRep F n K) (hρ : ρ.IsBar f) :
    (∀ g i j, j < i → ρ.r g i j = 0) ∨ ∀ g i j, i < j → ρ.r g i j = 0 := by
  have hst := UpperTheory.V.Fr.star hF
  have hd : ∀ g h : F.G0, ∀ i, ρ.r (g * h) i i = ρ.r g i i * ρ.r h i i := by
    intro g h i
    rw [hρ.1, hρ.1, hρ.1]
    simp [mul_zpow, map_mul]
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl
  · have hE : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 * ρ.r h 1 0 = 0 := by
      intro g h
      have e := hd g h 0
      rw [map_mul, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two] at e
      linear_combination e
    rcases or_zero hE with h01 | h10
    · right
      intro g i j hij
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij
      exact h01 g
    · left
      intro g i j hij
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij
      exact h10 g
  · set X := ρ.r F.g0 - ρ.r (F.star F.g0) with hXdef
    have hX : ∀ a b : Fin 3, a ≠ b → X a b = 0 := fun a b hab => ρ.diag a b hab
    have hXm : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r (g * F.g0 * h) - ρ.r (g * F.star F.g0 * h) = ρ.r g * X * ρ.r h := by
      intro g h
      simp only [map_mul, hXdef, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul]
    have hT : ∀ g h : F.G0, ∀ i : Fin 3, (ρ.r g * X * ρ.r h) i i = ρ.r g i i * X i i * ρ.r h i i := by
      intro g h i
      rw [← hXm, Matrix.sub_apply, hd, hd, hd, hd, hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply]
      ring
    have hE : ∀ g h : F.G0, ∀ i : Fin 3, (ρ.r g * ρ.r h) i i = ρ.r g i i * ρ.r h i i := by
      intro g h i
      rw [← map_mul, hd]
    have hp : (F.Ψ ↑F.g0 : kˣ) ^ 2 ≠ 1 := hF.baseSubgroup_spec
    set p := F.Ψ ↑F.g0 with hpdef
    have hw0 : w 3 0 = 1 := by simp [w]
    have hw1 : w 3 1 = 0 := by simp [w]
    have hw2 : w 3 2 = -1 := by simp [w]
    have hs : F.Ψ ↑(F.star F.g0) = p⁻¹ := hst.2.2.1 F.g0
    have hX0 : X 0 0 = f ↑p - f ↑p⁻¹ := by
      rw [hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply, hρ.1, hρ.1, hw0, hs]
      simp [← hpdef]
    have hX1 : X 1 1 = 0 := by
      rw [hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply, hρ.1, hρ.1, hw1]
      simp
    have hX2 : X 2 2 = f ↑p⁻¹ - f ↑p := by
      rw [hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply, hρ.1, hρ.1, hw2, hs]
      simp [← hpdef]
    have hq : f ↑p - f ↑p⁻¹ ≠ 0 := by
      intro e
      apply hp
      have e2 : f ((p : k) * p) = f ((p : k) * ↑p⁻¹) := by
        rw [map_mul, map_mul, sub_eq_zero.1 e]
      rw [Units.mul_inv] at e2
      have e3 : (p : k) * p = 1 := f.injective (by rw [e2, map_one])
      ext
      simpa [pow_two] using e3
    have h2 : (2 : K) ≠ 0 := by
      have hk2 : (2 : k) ≠ 0 := by
        intro e
        have : (l : ℕ) ∣ 2 := by
          have := (CharP.cast_eq_zero_iff k l 2).1 (by exact_mod_cast e)
          exact this
        have hl := (Fact.out : l.Prime)
        rcases (Nat.le_of_dvd (by norm_num) this).lt_or_eq with h | h
        · have := hl.two_le
          omega
        · exact hF.l_odd h
      intro e
      apply hk2
      apply f.injective
      rw [map_ofNat, e, map_zero]
    have hA : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 * ρ.r h 1 0 = 0 ∧ ρ.r g 0 2 * ρ.r h 2 0 = 0 := by
      intro g h
      have e1 := hE g h 0
      have e2 := hT g h 0
      simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, hX 0 1 (by decide), hX 0 2 (by decide),
        hX 1 0 (by decide), hX 1 2 (by decide), hX 2 0 (by decide), hX 2 1 (by decide)] at e1 e2
      refine two_zero (s := X 1 1) (t := X 2 2) ?_ ?_ ?_
      · rw [hX1, hX2]
        intro e
        exact hq (by linear_combination e)
      · linear_combination e1
      · linear_combination e2
    have hB : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 0 * ρ.r h 0 1 = 0 ∧ ρ.r g 1 2 * ρ.r h 2 1 = 0 := by
      intro g h
      have e1 := hE g h 1
      have e2 := hT g h 1
      simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, hX 0 1 (by decide), hX 0 2 (by decide),
        hX 1 0 (by decide), hX 1 2 (by decide), hX 2 0 (by decide), hX 2 1 (by decide)] at e1 e2
      refine two_zero (s := X 0 0) (t := X 2 2) ?_ ?_ ?_
      · rw [hX0, hX2]
        intro e
        apply h2
        have : (2 : K) * (f ↑p - f ↑p⁻¹) = 0 := by linear_combination e
        exact (mul_eq_zero.1 this).resolve_right hq
      · linear_combination e1
      · linear_combination e2
    have s01 : ∀ g, ρ.r (F.star g) 0 1 = ρ.r g 1 2 := fun g => hst.2.2.2.2 ρ g 0 1
    have s10 : ∀ g, ρ.r (F.star g) 1 0 = ρ.r g 2 1 := fun g => hst.2.2.2.2 ρ g 1 0
    have t12 : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 = 0) → ∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 2 = 0 := fun h g => by
      rw [← s01]; exact h _
    have t21 : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 0 = 0) → ∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 1 = 0 := fun h g => by
      rw [← s10]; exact h _
    have t10 : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 1 = 0) → ∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 0 = 0 := fun h g => by
      rw [← hst.1 g, s10]; exact h _
    have hP : ∀ g h : F.G0, (ρ.r g * ρ.r h) = ρ.r (g * h) := fun g h => (map_mul _ _ _).symm
    have hL : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 0 = 0) → (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 0 = 0) → (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 1 = 0) →
        (∀ (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin 3), j < i → ρ.r g i j = 0) := by
      intro a b c g i j hij
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij
      · exact a g
      · exact b g
      · exact c g
    have hR : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 = 0) → (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 2 = 0) → (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 2 = 0) →
        (∀ (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin 3), i < j → ρ.r g i j = 0) := by
      intro a b c g i j hij
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij
      · exact a g
      · exact b g
      · exact c g
    rcases or_zero (fun g h => (hA g h).1) with a01 | a10
    · have a12 := t12 a01
      rcases or_zero (fun g h => (hA g h).2) with a02 | a20
      · exact Or.inr (hR a01 a02 a12)
      · have hC : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 2 * ρ.r h 2 1 = 0 := by
          intro g h
          have e := congrFun (congrFun (hP g h) 0) 1
          simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, a01, a01 h] at e
          linear_combination e
        rcases or_zero hC with a02 | a21
        · exact Or.inr (hR a01 a02 a12)
        · exact Or.inl (hL (t10 a21) a20 a21)
    · have a21 := t21 a10
      rcases or_zero (fun g h => (hA g h).2) with a02 | a20
      · have hC : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 0 * ρ.r h 0 1 = 0 := by
          intro g h
          have e := congrFun (congrFun (hP g h) 2) 1
          simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, a21, a21 h] at e
          linear_combination e
        rcases or_zero hC with a20 | a01
        · exact Or.inl (hL a10 a20 a21)
        · exact Or.inr (hR a01 a02 (t12 a01))
      · exact Or.inl (hL a10 a20 a21)

private theorem wsum {G : Type} {n : ℕ} (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) (L : List (G × Fin n × Fin n))
    (h : ∀ s ∈ L, s.2.1 ≤ s.2.2) (h0 : (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0) : ∀ s ∈ L, s.2.1 = s.2.2 := by
  have hw : ∀ i j : Fin n, i ≤ j → 0 ≤ w n i - w n j ∧ (w n i - w n j = 0 → i = j) := by
    intro i j hij
    have hij' : (i : ℕ) ≤ j := hij
    rcases hn with rfl | rfl
    · simp only [w]
      refine ⟨by norm_num; omega, fun e => Fin.ext ?_⟩
      norm_num at e
      omega
    · simp only [w]
      refine ⟨by norm_num; omega, fun e => Fin.ext ?_⟩
      norm_num at e
      omega
  induction L with
  | nil => simp
  | cons s L ih =>
    intro s' hs'
    have hs := hw s.2.1 s.2.2 (h s (List.mem_cons_self ..))
    have hL : ∀ t ∈ L, t.2.1 ≤ t.2.2 := fun t ht => h t (List.mem_cons_of_mem _ ht)
    have hnn : 0 ≤ (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum := by
      apply List.sum_nonneg
      intro x hx
      obtain ⟨t, ht, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hx
      exact (hw _ _ (hL t ht)).1
    rw [List.map_cons, List.sum_cons] at h0
    have e1 : w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2 = 0 := by linarith [hs.1]
    have e2 : (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0 := by linarith [hs.1]
    rcases List.mem_cons.1 hs' with rfl | hs'
    · exact hs.2 e1
    · exact ih hL e2 s' hs'

private theorem cyc_mem {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}
    (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) {C : Type} [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] (f : k →+* ResidueField C)
    (ρ : NRep F n C) (hρ : (ρ.map (residue C)).IsBar f) (L : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n))
    (h0 : (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0) (h1 : ∃ s ∈ L, s.2.1 ≠ s.2.2) :
    (L.map fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod ∈ maximalIdeal C := by
  have hr : ∀ g i j, (ρ.map (residue C)).r g i j = residue C (ρ.r g i j) := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, map_list_prod, List.map_map]
  apply List.prod_eq_zero
  rcases tri_of_bar hF hn f (ρ.map (residue C)) hρ with ht | ht
  · by_cases hex : ∃ s ∈ L, s.2.2 < s.2.1
    · obtain ⟨s, hs, hlt⟩ := hex
      refine List.mem_map.2 ⟨s, hs, ?_⟩
      simp only [Function.comp_apply]
      rw [← hr]
      exact ht _ _ _ hlt
    · push_neg at hex
      exfalso
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hne⟩ := h1
      exact hne (wsum hn L hex h0 s hs)
  · by_cases hex : ∃ s ∈ L, s.2.1 < s.2.2
    · obtain ⟨s, hs, hlt⟩ := hex
      refine List.mem_map.2 ⟨s, hs, ?_⟩
      simp only [Function.comp_apply]
      rw [← hr]
      exact ht _ _ _ hlt
    · push_neg at hex
      exfalso
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hne⟩ := h1
      have hL' : ∀ t ∈ L.map (fun s => (s.1, s.2.2, s.2.1)), t.2.1 ≤ t.2.2 := by
        intro t ht
        obtain ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 ht
        exact hex s hs
      have h0' : ((L.map (fun s => (s.1, s.2.2, s.2.1))).map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0 := by
        have e : ((L.map (fun s => (s.1, s.2.2, s.2.1))).map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2) =
            (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).map Neg.neg := by
          simp [List.map_map, Function.comp_def]
        have hneg : ∀ m : List ℤ, (m.map Neg.neg).sum = -m.sum := by
          intro m
          induction m with
          | nil => simp
          | cons a m ih => simp only [List.map_cons, List.sum_cons, ih]; ring


[APD_SRC_POINT_LOC] Full point_loc for all original IsCL targets and compatible residue maps
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PointLoc.lean#L286-L349
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.point_loc :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] [Algebra O C], UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCL O C → ∀ (f : ResidueField O →+* ResidueField C), IsResMap O C f → ∀ ρ : NRep F n C, ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal C) → (ρ.map (residue C)).IsBar f → ∃ ξ : S.A →ₐ[O] C, S.u.map (ξ : S.A →+* C) = ρ ∧ S.x0 ≤ (maximalIdeal C).comap (ξ : S.A →+* C) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S C _ _ _ hCL f hf ρ hρc hρ
  obtain ⟨S', hmem, hfin, hCO, hpow⟩ := UpperTheory.V.CA.cl_sub O C hCL
  obtain ⟨hBco, hmB⟩ := co_B hF hn S
  have hJ : ∀ c ∈ maximalIdeal C, c ∈ S' := fun c hc => (hmem c).2 ⟨0, by simpa using hc⟩
  have hmax : ∀ c : S', c ∈ maximalIdeal S' ↔ (c : C) ∈ maximalIdeal C := by
    intro c
    simpa using hpow 1 c
  have hin : ∀ (c : C) (t : ResidueField O), residue C c = f t → c ∈ S' := by
    intro c t h
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective t
    refine (hmem c).2 ⟨a, ?_⟩
    rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sub, h, hf a, sub_self]
  have hres : ∀ (c : S') (t : ResidueField O), residue C (c : C) = f t → res O S' c = t := by
    intro c t h
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective t
    have d : c - algebraMap O S' a ∈ maximalIdeal S' := by
      rw [hmax]
      rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff]
      simp only [Subalgebra.coe_sub, Subalgebra.coe_algebraMap, map_sub, h, hf a, sub_self]
    have d2 := (res_eq_zero_iff O S' _).2 d
    rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at d2
    rw [d2, IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq]
  have hJN : ∀ N, ∀ c ∈ maximalIdeal C ^ N, c ∈ ((maximalIdeal S' ^ N • ⊤ : Submodule S' C)) := by
    intro N c hc
    have hle : maximalIdeal C ≤ Ideal.map (algebraMap S' C) (maximalIdeal S') := by
      intro c hc
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem (algebraMap S' C) ((hmax ⟨c, hJ c hc⟩).2 hc)
    have hc' := Ideal.pow_right_mono hle N hc
    rw [← Ideal.map_pow] at hc'
    rw [Ideal.smul_top_eq_map]
    exact hc'
  have cone : Cone O S' ρ := by
    refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro g i
      exact ⟨⟨ρ.r g i i, hin _ _ (hρ.1 g i)⟩, rfl, hres ⟨ρ.r g i i, hin _ _ (hρ.1 g i)⟩ _ (hρ.1 g i)⟩
    · intro L h0 h1
      have hp := cyc_mem hF hn f ρ hρ L h0 h1
      exact ⟨⟨_, hJ _ hp⟩, (hmax _).2 hp, rfl⟩
    · intro i j
      haveI : IsNoetherian S' C := isNoetherian_of_isNoetherianRing_of_finite S' C
      exact IsNoetherian.noetherian _
    · intro i j N
      exact cont_sub (maximalIdeal S') (maximalIdeal C) hJN (fun g => ρ.r g i j) (hρc.1 i j) N
    · refine ⟨fun v i g => ⟨((ρ.ψ v i g : Cˣ) : C), hin _ _ (hρ.2 v i g)⟩, ?_, fun v i g => rfl⟩
      intro v i
      refine ⟨?_, fun g => hres _ _ (hρ.2 v i g)⟩
      intro N
      refine lc_lemma _ _ (hρc.2 v i N) ?_
      intro x y hxy
      apply Ideal.Quotient.eq.2
      rw [hpow]
      exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 hxy
  obtain ⟨ξ, β, h1, h2⟩ := alg hF hn S S' C Subtype.val_injective ρ cone
  refine ⟨ξ, h1, ?_⟩
  rw [Space.x0, Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
  intro b hb
  rw [← hmB] at hb
  show ξ (S.inc b) ∈ maximalIdeal C
  rw [h2]
  apply (hmax _).1
  rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O S', res_map]
  exact (res_eq_zero_iff O S.B b).2 hb


[APD_SRC_ALG_DEFS] Actual Laurent subalgebra, HasVertex, vertex ideal, IsVertexRing and IsModel
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgDefs.lean#L9-L121
section Lau

variable {C K : Type} [CommRing C] [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] (P : ℤ → Submodule C K) [SetLike.GradedMonoid P]

theorem coeff_mul_mem {p q : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ} (j : ℤ) (M : Submodule C K)
    (h : ∀ a b, a + b = j → p.coeff a * q.coeff b ∈ M) : (p * q).coeff j ∈ M := by
  classical
  rw [AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_mul]
  refine Submodule.sum_mem _ fun a _ => Submodule.sum_mem _ fun b _ => ?_
  dsimp only
  split_ifs with e
  · exact h a b e
  · exact zero_mem _

def lauSub : Subalgebra C (AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) where
  carrier := {p | ∀ j, p.coeff j ∈ P j}
  mul_mem' {p q} hp hq j := coeff_mul_mem j _ fun a b e => e ▸ SetLike.mul_mem_graded (hp a) (hq b)
  add_mem' {p q} hp hq j := add_mem (hp j) (hq j)
  algebraMap_mem' c j := by
    classical
    rw [AddMonoidAlgebra.coe_algebraMap, Function.comp_apply, AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_single, Finsupp.single_apply]
    split_ifs with e
    · subst e
      rw [Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one]
      exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (SetLike.one_mem_graded P)
    · exact zero_mem _

theorem mem_lauSub {p : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ} : p ∈ lauSub P ↔ ∀ j, p.coeff j ∈ P j := Iff.rfl

theorem single_mem_lauSub {j : ℤ} {x : K} (hx : x ∈ P j) : AddMonoidAlgebra.single j x ∈ lauSub P := by
  classical
  intro i
  rw [AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_single, Finsupp.single_apply]
  split_ifs with e
  · subst e
    exact hx
  · exact zero_mem _

variable [IsLocalRing C]

structure HasVertex : Prop where
  zero : ∀ x ∈ P 0, ∃ c : C, algebraMap C K c = x
  cross : ∀ j, j ≠ 0 → ∀ x ∈ P j, ∀ y ∈ P (-j), ∃ c ∈ maximalIdeal C, algebraMap C K c = x * y

variable {P} in

def vertex (h : HasVertex P) : Ideal (lauSub P) where
  carrier := {p | ∃ c ∈ maximalIdeal C, algebraMap C K c = p.1.coeff 0}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro p q ⟨c, h1, h2⟩ ⟨d, h3, h4⟩
    exact ⟨c + d, add_mem h1 h3, by rw [map_add, h2, h4]; rfl⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, zero_mem _, map_zero _⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro q p ⟨c, h1, h2⟩
    refine Submodule.mem_map.1 (coeff_mul_mem (p := q.1) (q := p.1) 0
      (Submodule.map (Algebra.linearMap C K) (maximalIdeal C)) fun a b e => Submodule.mem_map.2 ?_)
    by_cases h0 : a = 0
    · subst h0
      rw [zero_add] at e
      subst e
      obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := h.zero _ (q.2 0)
      exact ⟨d * c, Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ h1, by rw [← hd, ← h2, ← map_mul]; rfl⟩
    · obtain rfl : b = -a := by omega
      exact h.cross a h0 _ (q.2 a) _ (p.2 _)

variable {P} in
theorem mem_vertex (h : HasVertex P) {p : lauSub P} :
    p ∈ vertex h ↔ ∃ c ∈ maximalIdeal C, algebraMap C K c = p.1.coeff 0 := Iff.rfl

end Lau

section Part

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ} (C : Type) [CommRing C] {K : Type}
  [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] (ρ : NRep F n K)

def NRep.part (j : ℤ) : Submodule C K := Submodule.span C (ρ.mon j)

instance : SetLike.GradedMonoid (ρ.part C) where
  one_mem := Submodule.subset_span ⟨[], by simp, by simp⟩
  mul_mem i j x y hx hy := by
    have := Submodule.mul_mem_mul hx hy
    rw [NRep.part, NRep.part, Submodule.span_mul_span] at this
    refine Submodule.span_mono ?_ this
    rintro _ ⟨_, ⟨L, h1, rfl⟩, _, ⟨M, h2, rfl⟩, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨L ++ M, by simp only [List.map_append, List.sum_append, h1, h2], by simp⟩

theorem NRep.r_mem_part (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin n) : ρ.r g i j ∈ ρ.part C (w n i - w n j) :=
  Submodule.subset_span ⟨[(g, i, j)], by simp, by simp⟩

end Part

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ}

structure IsVertexRing (C : Type) [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] {D : Type} [CommRing D] [Algebra C D] (𝔪 : Ideal D) :
    Prop where
  noeth : IsNoetherianRing D
  inj : Function.Injective (algebraMap C D)
  sep : ⨅ N : ℕ, 𝔪 ^ N = ⊥
  comap : 𝔪.comap (algebraMap C D) = maximalIdeal C
  surj : ∀ d : D, ∃ c : C, d - algebraMap C D c ∈ 𝔪

structure IsModel (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {D : Type} [CommRing D] [Algebra C D]
    (𝔪 : Ideal D) (ρ : NRep F n D) : Prop where
  cont : ρ.Cont 𝔪
  off : ∀ g i j, i ≠ j → ρ.r g i j ∈ 𝔪
  dg : ∀ g i, ∃ c : C, algebraMap C D c = ρ.r g i i ∧
    res O C c = ((F.Ψ g ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)
  psi : ∀ v i g, ∃ c : C, algebraMap C D c = ρ.ψ v i g ∧
    res O C c = ((F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)
  mon : ∀ x ∈ ρ.mon 0, ∃ c : C, algebraMap C D c = x

end UpperTheory.V.Fr


[APD_SRC_VERTEX] The actual Cone-to-HasVertex construction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgVertex.lean#L5-L53
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_vertex :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ} (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {K : Type} [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] (ρ : NRep F n K), Cone O C ρ → HasVertex (ρ.part C) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ F n C _ _ _ K _ _ ρ hC
  have off : ∀ (L : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n)) (j : ℤ),
      (L.map (fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2)).sum = j → j ≠ 0 → ∃ s ∈ L, s.2.1 ≠ s.2.2 := by
    intro L j hL hj
    by_contra h
    push_neg at h
    apply hj
    rw [← hL]
    apply List.sum_eq_zero
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hx
    rw [h s hs, sub_self]
  constructor
  · intro x hx
    have hle : ρ.mon 0 ⊆ (LinearMap.range (Algebra.linearMap C K) : Set K) := by
      rintro _ ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩
      by_cases hd : ∃ s ∈ L, s.2.1 ≠ s.2.2
      · obtain ⟨c, -, hc⟩ := hC.cyc L hL hd
        exact ⟨c, hc⟩
      · push_neg at hd
        have hm : (L.map (fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod ∈ (algebraMap C K).rangeS := by
          apply list_prod_mem
          intro y hy
          obtain ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hy
          rw [← hd s hs]
          obtain ⟨c, hc, -⟩ := hC.dg s.1 s.2.1
          exact ⟨c, hc⟩
        obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hm
        exact ⟨c, hc⟩
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := Submodule.span_le.2 hle hx
    exact ⟨c, hc⟩
  · intro j hj x hx y hy
    open Pointwise in
    have hle : ρ.mon j * ρ.mon (-j) ⊆
        (Submodule.map (Algebra.linearMap C K) (maximalIdeal C) : Set K) := by
      rintro _ ⟨a, ⟨L1, h1, rfl⟩, b, ⟨L2, h2, rfl⟩, rfl⟩
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hne⟩ := off L1 j h1 hj
      obtain ⟨c, hc, hc'⟩ := hC.cyc (L1 ++ L2)
        (by rw [List.map_append, List.sum_append, h1, h2]; ring) ⟨s, List.mem_append_left _ hs, hne⟩
      refine Submodule.mem_map.2 ⟨c, hc, ?_⟩
      rw [Algebra.linearMap_apply, hc', List.map_append, List.prod_append]
    open Pointwise in
    have hxy : x * y ∈ Submodule.span C (ρ.mon j * ρ.mon (-j)) := by
      rw [← Submodule.span_mul_span]
      exact Submodule.mul_mem_mul hx hy
    obtain ⟨c, hc, hc'⟩ := Submodule.mem_map.1 (Submodule.span_le.2 hle hxy)
    exact ⟨c, hc, hc'⟩


[APD_SRC_ALG_FT] Finite generation of the Laurent subalgebra from entry spans
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/SingleMemOfSpan.lean#L15-L70
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_ft :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ} (C : Type) [CommRing C] {K : Type} [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] (ρ : NRep F n K), (∀ i j, (Submodule.span C (Set.range fun g => ρ.r g i j)).FG) → Algebra.FiniteType C (lauSub (ρ.part C)) := by
  intro l _ k _ F n C _ K _ _ ρ h
  choose S hS using h
  set G : Set (AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) :=
    ⋃ i, ⋃ j, (fun x => AddMonoidAlgebra.single (w n i - w n j) x) '' (S i j : Set K) with hG
  have hGfin : G.Finite :=
    Set.finite_iUnion fun i => Set.finite_iUnion fun j => (S i j).finite_toSet.image _
  have hGmem : ∀ i j x, x ∈ (S i j : Set K) →
      AddMonoidAlgebra.single (w n i - w n j) x ∈ Algebra.adjoin C G := fun i j x hx =>
    Algebra.subset_adjoin (Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨i, Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨j, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩⟩⟩)
  have hent : ∀ g i j, AddMonoidAlgebra.single (w n i - w n j) (ρ.r g i j) ∈ Algebra.adjoin C G :=
    fun g i j => single_mem_of_span (Algebra.adjoin C G) _ (S i j) (hGmem i j)
      (by rw [hS i j]; exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨g, rfl⟩)
  have hlist : ∀ L : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n),
      AddMonoidAlgebra.single ((L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum)
        ((L.map fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod) ∈ Algebra.adjoin C G := by
    intro L
    induction L with
    | nil =>
      simp only [List.map_nil, List.sum_nil, List.prod_nil]
      rw [← AddMonoidAlgebra.one_def]
      exact (Algebra.adjoin C G).one_mem
    | cons s L ih =>
      simp only [List.map_cons, List.sum_cons, List.prod_cons]
      rw [← AddMonoidAlgebra.single_mul_single]
      exact mul_mem (hent _ _ _) ih
  have hmon : ∀ j, ∀ x ∈ ρ.mon j, AddMonoidAlgebra.single j x ∈ Algebra.adjoin C G := by
    rintro j x ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩
    rw [← hL]
    exact hlist L
  have hpart : ∀ j x, x ∈ ρ.part C j → AddMonoidAlgebra.single j x ∈ Algebra.adjoin C G :=
    fun j x hx => single_mem_of_span _ j _ (hmon j) hx
  have heq : Algebra.adjoin C G = lauSub (ρ.part C) := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · rw [Algebra.adjoin_le_iff]
      intro y hy
      obtain ⟨i, hy⟩ := Set.mem_iUnion.1 hy
      obtain ⟨j, hy⟩ := Set.mem_iUnion.1 hy
      obtain ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩ := hy
      apply single_mem_lauSub
      have hx' : x ∈ Submodule.span C (Set.range fun g => ρ.r g i j) := by
        rw [← hS i j]
        exact Submodule.subset_span hx
      refine Submodule.span_le.mpr ?_ hx'
      rintro _ ⟨g, rfl⟩
      exact NRep.r_mem_part C ρ g i j
    · intro p hp
      have hp' : ∀ j, p.coeff j ∈ ρ.part C j := hp
      have hsum : p = p.coeff.sum fun j x => AddMonoidAlgebra.single j x := by
        apply AddMonoidAlgebra.ext
        rw [Finsupp.sum, AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_sum]
        exact (Finsupp.sum_single _).symm
      rw [hsum]
      exact Subalgebra.sum_mem _ fun j _ => hpart j _ (hp' j)
  exact ⟨(Subalgebra.fg_top _).mpr ⟨hGfin.toFinset, by rw [Set.Finite.coe_toFinset]; exact heq⟩⟩


[APD_SRC_HOMOG] Homogeneous components of every power of the actual vertex ideal
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgHomog.lean#L1-L72
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.AlgDefs

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.single_coeff_mul {K : Type} [CommRing K] (p q : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) (j : ℤ) :
    AddMonoidAlgebra.single j ((p * q).coeff j) = ∑ a ∈ p.coeff.support,
      AddMonoidAlgebra.single a (p.coeff a) * AddMonoidAlgebra.single (-a + j) (q.coeff (-a + j)) := by
  classical
  have key : ∀ a : ℤ, AddMonoidAlgebra.single a (p.coeff a) *
      AddMonoidAlgebra.single (-a + j) (q.coeff (-a + j)) =
        AddMonoidAlgebra.single j (p.coeff a * q.coeff (-a + j)) := by
    intro a
    rw [AddMonoidAlgebra.single_mul_single, add_neg_cancel_left]
  simp_rw [key]
  have hc : (p * q).coeff j = ∑ a ∈ p.coeff.support, p.coeff a * q.coeff (-a + j) := by
    rw [AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_mul, Finsupp.sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
    rw [Finsupp.sum]
    have hcond : ∀ b : ℤ, (a + b = j) ↔ (b = -a + j) := fun b => by omega
    simp_rw [hcond]
    rw [Finset.sum_ite_eq']
    split_ifs with hm
    · rfl
    · rw [Finsupp.notMem_support_iff.mp hm, mul_zero]
  rw [hc]
  ext k
  simp only [AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_sum, AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_single, Finsupp.finset_sum_apply,
    Finsupp.single_apply]
  split_ifs with hk
  · rfl
  · simp

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_homog :
    ∀ {C K : Type} [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] {P : ℤ → Submodule C K} [SetLike.GradedMonoid P] (h : HasVertex P) (N : ℕ) (p : lauSub P), p ∈ vertex h ^ N → ∀ j : ℤ, (⟨AddMonoidAlgebra.single j (p.1.coeff j), single_mem_lauSub P (p.2 j)⟩ : lauSub P) ∈ vertex h ^ N := by
  intro C K _ _ _ _ P _ h N
  let comp : lauSub P → ℤ → lauSub P := fun q k =>
    ⟨AddMonoidAlgebra.single k (q.1.coeff k), single_mem_lauSub P (q.2 k)⟩
  have hcomp : ∀ q k, (comp q k).1 = AddMonoidAlgebra.single k (q.1.coeff k) := fun _ _ => rfl
  have base : ∀ q : lauSub P, q ∈ vertex h → ∀ k : ℤ, comp q k ∈ vertex h := by
    intro q hq k
    rw [mem_vertex] at hq ⊢
    obtain ⟨c, hc, hce⟩ := hq
    rw [hcomp, AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_single]
    by_cases hk : k = 0
    · subst hk
      exact ⟨c, hc, by rw [Finsupp.single_eq_same, hce]⟩
    · exact ⟨0, Ideal.zero_mem _, by rw [Finsupp.single_eq_of_ne' hk, map_zero]⟩
  show ∀ p : lauSub P, p ∈ vertex h ^ N → ∀ j, comp p j ∈ vertex h ^ N
  induction N with
  | zero =>
    intro p _ j
    rw [pow_zero, Ideal.one_eq_top]
    exact Submodule.mem_top
  | succ n ih =>
    intro p hp
    rw [pow_succ] at hp
    refine Submodule.mul_induction_on (C := fun r => ∀ j, comp r j ∈ vertex h ^ (n + 1)) hp ?_ ?_
    · intro x hx y hy j
      have e : comp (x * y) j =
          ∑ a ∈ x.1.coeff.support, comp x a * comp y (-a + j) := by
        apply Subtype.ext
        rw [AddSubmonoidClass.coe_finset_sum, hcomp, Subalgebra.coe_mul, single_coeff_mul]
        rfl
      rw [e, pow_succ]
      exact Ideal.sum_mem _ fun a _ => Ideal.mul_mem_mul (ih x hx a) (base y hy _)
    · intro x y hx hy j
      have e : comp (x + y) j = comp x j + comp y j := by
        apply Subtype.ext
        simp only [hcomp, Subalgebra.coe_add, AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_add, Finsupp.add_apply,
          AddMonoidAlgebra.single_add]
      rw [e]
      exact add_mem (hx j) (hy j)


[APD_SRC_ALG_SEP] Separation of the actual vertex ideal, without a global Jacobson hypothesis
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgSep.lean#L6-L44
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_sep :
    ∀ {C K : Type} [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] {P : ℤ → Submodule C K} [SetLike.GradedMonoid P] (h : HasVertex P), IsNoetherianRing (lauSub P) → ⨅ N : ℕ, vertex h ^ N = ⊥ := by
  intro C K _ _ _ _ P _ h hN
  classical
  rw [eq_bot_iff]
  intro p hp
  rw [Submodule.mem_iInf] at hp
  have hcomp : ∀ j : ℤ, (p : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ).coeff j = 0 := by
    intro j
    set a := (p : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ).coeff j with ha
    have hpj : a ∈ P j := (show ∀ i, (p : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ).coeff i ∈ P i from p.2) j
    let q : lauSub P := ⟨AddMonoidAlgebra.single j a, single_mem_lauSub P hpj⟩
    have hq : ∀ N : ℕ, q ∈ vertex h ^ N := fun N => alg_homog h N p (hp N) j
    have hq' : q ∈ ⨅ i : ℕ, (vertex h) ^ i • (⊤ : Submodule (lauSub P) (lauSub P)) := by
      rw [Submodule.mem_iInf]
      intro i
      simp only [Ideal.smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top]
      exact hq i
    obtain ⟨⟨r, hr⟩, hrq⟩ := (Ideal.mem_iInf_smul_pow_eq_bot_iff (vertex h) q).1 hq'
    have hrq' : r * q = q := hrq
    obtain ⟨c, hc, hcr⟩ := hr
    have h1 : ((r * q : lauSub P) : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ).coeff j =
        (r : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ).coeff 0 * a := by
      have h0 := AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_mul_single_add (r : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) a j 0
      rw [zero_add] at h0
      exact h0
    have h2 : ((r * q : lauSub P) : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ).coeff j = a := by
      rw [hrq']
      show (AddMonoidAlgebra.single j a).coeff j = a
      rw [AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_single, Finsupp.single_eq_same]
    rw [h2, ← hcr] at h1
    have hu : IsUnit (algebraMap C K (1 - c)) :=
      (IsLocalRing.isUnit_one_sub_self_of_mem_nonunits c hc).map _
    have h3 : algebraMap C K (1 - c) * a = 0 := by
      rw [map_sub, map_one, sub_mul, one_mul, ← h1, sub_self]
    exact hu.mul_right_eq_zero.1 h3
  apply Subtype.ext
  ext j
  simpa using hcomp j


[APD_SRC_ALG_LAU] Weighted Laurent representation and full ordinary characters
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgLau.lean#L15-L42
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_lau :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ} (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {K : Type} [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] (ρ : NRep F n K), Cone O C ρ → ∃ ρD : NRep F n (lauSub (ρ.part C)), (∀ g i j, (ρD.r g i j).1 = AddMonoidAlgebra.single (w n i - w n j) (ρ.r g i j)) ∧ (∀ v i g, (ρD.ψ v i g : lauSub (ρ.part C)).1 = AddMonoidAlgebra.single 0 (ρ.ψ v i g : K)) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ F n C _ _ _ K _ _ ρ hc
  obtain ⟨χ, -, hχ⟩ := hc.psi
  obtain ⟨ρ', hr, hψ⟩ := alg_twist (lauT K (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1))
    (NRep.map (AddMonoidAlgebra.singleZeroRingHom : K →+* AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) ρ)
  have hr' : ∀ g i j, ρ'.r g i j = AddMonoidAlgebra.single (w n i - w n j) (ρ.r g i j) := by
    intro g i j
    rw [hr, lauT_zpow, NRep.map_r]
    simp [AddMonoidAlgebra.single_mul_single]
  have hψ' : ∀ v i g, ((ρ'.ψ v i g : (AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ)ˣ) : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) =
      AddMonoidAlgebra.single 0 (ρ.ψ v i g : K) := by
    intro v i g
    rw [hψ]
    rfl
  have h1 : ∀ g i j, ρ'.r g i j ∈ lauSub (ρ.part C) := by
    intro g i j
    rw [hr']
    exact single_mem_lauSub _ (NRep.r_mem_part C ρ g i j)
  have h2 : ∀ v i g, ((ρ'.ψ v i g : (AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ)ˣ) : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) ∈
      lauSub (ρ.part C) := by
    intro v i g
    rw [hψ']
    apply single_mem_lauSub
    rw [← hχ, Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one]
    exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem
  exact ⟨NRep.restrict ρ' (lauSub (ρ.part C)) h1 h2, fun g i j => hr' g i j,
    fun v i g => hψ' v i g⟩


[APD_SRC_ALG_CONT] Exact quotient-module continuity transported to vertex powers
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgCont.lean#L49-L77
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_cont :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ} (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {K : Type} [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] (ρ : NRep F n K), Cone O C ρ → ∀ (h : HasVertex (ρ.part C)) (ρD : NRep F n (lauSub (ρ.part C))), (∀ g i j, (ρD.r g i j).1 = AddMonoidAlgebra.single (w n i - w n j) (ρ.r g i j)) → (∀ v i g, (ρD.ψ v i g : lauSub (ρ.part C)).1 = AddMonoidAlgebra.single 0 (ρ.ψ v i g : K)) → ρD.Cont (vertex h) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ F n C _ _ _ K _ _ ρ hc h ρD hr hψ
  refine ⟨fun i j N => ?_, fun v i N => ?_⟩
  · refine locConst_of_factor (hc.cont i j N) _ fun g g' hgg => ?_
    rw [Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.eq] at hgg
    have hS : Submodule.span C (Set.range fun g => ρ.r g i j) ≤ ρ.part C (w n i - w n j) := by
      rw [Submodule.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨g0, rfl⟩
      exact NRep.r_mem_part C ρ g0 i j
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hpe⟩ := single_mem_vertex_pow h N (w n i - w n j) _ hS hgg
    rw [Ideal.Quotient.eq]
    have e : ρD.r g i j - ρD.r g' i j = p := by
      apply Subtype.ext
      rw [hpe, AddSubgroupClass.coe_sub, hr, hr, AddMonoidAlgebra.single_sub]
    rw [e]
    exact hp
  · obtain ⟨χ, hχ, hχe⟩ := hc.psi
    refine locConst_of_factor ((hχ v i).1 N) _ fun g g' hgg => ?_
    rw [Ideal.Quotient.eq] at hgg ⊢
    have hm := algebraMap_mem_vertex_pow h N hgg
    have e : (↑(ρD.ψ v i g) : lauSub (ρ.part C)) - ↑(ρD.ψ v i g') =
        algebraMap C (lauSub (ρ.part C)) (χ v i g - χ v i g') := by
      apply Subtype.ext
      rw [AddSubgroupClass.coe_sub, hψ, hψ]
      show _ = AddMonoidAlgebra.single 0 (algebraMap C K (χ v i g - χ v i g'))
      rw [map_sub, hχe, hχe, AddMonoidAlgebra.single_sub]
    rw [e]
    exact hm


[APD_SRC_ALG_MODEL] All original model fields after weighting
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgModel.lean#L26-L65
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_model :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ} (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {K : Type} [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] (ρ : NRep F n K), Cone O C ρ → ∀ (h : HasVertex (ρ.part C)) (ρD : NRep F n (lauSub (ρ.part C))), (∀ g i j, (ρD.r g i j).1 = AddMonoidAlgebra.single (w n i - w n j) (ρ.r g i j)) → (∀ v i g, (ρD.ψ v i g : lauSub (ρ.part C)).1 = AddMonoidAlgebra.single 0 (ρ.ψ v i g : K)) → IsModel O C (vertex h) ρD := by
  intro l _ O _ _ F n C _ _ _ K _ _ ρ hc h ρD hr hψ
  have key : ∀ (c : C) (y : K), algebraMap C K c = y → ∀ p : lauSub (ρ.part C),
      p.1 = AddMonoidAlgebra.single 0 y → algebraMap C (lauSub (ρ.part C)) c = p := by
    intro c y hcy p hp
    apply Subtype.ext
    rw [alg_val, hp, hcy]
  refine ⟨alg_cont C ρ hc h ρD hr hψ, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro g i j hij
    rw [mem_vertex]
    refine ⟨0, Ideal.zero_mem _, ?_⟩
    rw [hr, map_zero]
    have hne : (0 : ℤ) ≠ w n i - w n j := by
      intro h0
      exact w_ne_w_of_ne n hij (by omega)
    exact (Finsupp.single_eq_of_ne hne).symm
  · intro g i
    obtain ⟨c, hc1, hc2⟩ := hc.dg g i
    refine ⟨c, key c _ hc1 _ ?_, hc2⟩
    rw [hr, sub_self]
  · intro v i g
    obtain ⟨χ, hχ, hχψ⟩ := hc.psi
    exact ⟨χ v i g, key _ _ (hχψ v i g) _ (hψ v i g), (hχ v i).2 g⟩
  · intro x hx
    obtain ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩ := hx
    have hmem : (L.map fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod ∈ ρ.part C 0 :=
      Submodule.subset_span ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨c, hc'⟩ := h.zero _ hmem
    refine ⟨c, key c _ hc' _ ?_⟩
    have e1 : ∀ L' : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n),
        ((L'.map fun s => ρD.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod : lauSub (ρ.part C)).1 =
          (L'.map fun s => AddMonoidAlgebra.single (w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2) (ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod := by
      intro L'
      induction L' with
      | nil => rfl
      | cons s L' ih =>
        rw [List.map_cons, List.prod_cons, Subalgebra.coe_mul, ih, hr, List.map_cons, List.prod_cons]
    rw [e1, prod_single (fun s : F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2)
      (fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2) L, hL]


[APD_SRC_ALG_QUOT] Every vertex quotient is an original coefficient algebra
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgQuot.lean#L16-L95
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_quot :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {D : Type} [CommRing D] [Algebra O D] [Algebra C D] [IsScalarTower O C D] (𝔪 : Ideal D), IsVertexRing C 𝔪 → ∀ N : ℕ, ∃ h : IsCO O (D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)), @maximalIdeal _ _ h.toIsLocalRing = 𝔪.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1))) := by
  intro O _ _ C _ _ _ D _ _ _ _ 𝔪 hV N
  have hle : 𝔪 ^ (N + 1) ≤ 𝔪 := Ideal.pow_le_self (Nat.succ_ne_zero N)
  have hmax : 𝔪.IsMaximal := by
    rw [Ideal.isMaximal_iff]
    constructor
    · intro h1
      have h1' : (1 : C) ∈ 𝔪.comap (algebraMap C D) := by
        rw [Ideal.mem_comap, map_one]
        exact h1
      rw [hV.comap] at h1'
      exact (maximalIdeal.isMaximal C).ne_top ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).mpr h1')
    · intro J x hJ hx hxJ
      obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hV.surj x
      have hcu : IsUnit c := by
        by_contra hnu
        have hcm : c ∈ maximalIdeal C := hnu
        rw [← hV.comap, Ideal.mem_comap] at hcm
        apply hx
        have := 𝔪.add_mem hc hcm
        rwa [sub_add_cancel] at this
      have hcJ : algebraMap C D c ∈ J := by
        have := J.sub_mem hxJ (hJ hc)
        rwa [sub_sub_cancel] at this
      rw [Ideal.eq_top_of_isUnit_mem J hcJ (hcu.map _)]
      exact Submodule.mem_top
  set M : Ideal (D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) := 𝔪.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1))) with hM
  have hmem : ∀ d : D, Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) d ∈ M ↔ d ∈ 𝔪 :=
    fun d => Ideal.mem_quotient_iff_mem hle
  have hMmax : M.IsMaximal := by
    rcases Ideal.map_eq_top_or_isMaximal_of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)))
      Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective hmax with h | h
    · exfalso
      have h1 : Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) 1 ∈ M := by
        rw [← hM] at h
        rw [h]
        exact Submodule.mem_top
      exact hmax.ne_top ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).mpr ((hmem 1).mp h1))
    · exact h
  have hMpow : M ^ (N + 1) = ⊥ := by
    rw [hM, ← Ideal.map_pow, Ideal.map_quotient_self]
  have hnil : nilradical (D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) = M := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · exact nilradical_le_prime M
    · intro x hx
      refine ⟨N + 1, ?_⟩
      have := Ideal.pow_mem_pow hx (N + 1)
      rw [hMpow] at this
      exact (Submodule.mem_bot _).mp this
  haveI : (nilradical (D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1))).IsMaximal := hnil ▸ hMmax
  haveI hloc : IsLocalRing (D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) := IsLocalRing.of_isMaximal_nilradical _
  have hMeq : maximalIdeal (D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) = M := (IsLocalRing.eq_maximalIdeal hMmax).symm
  have halg : ∀ a : O, algebraMap O (D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) a
      = Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (algebraMap C D (algebraMap O C a)) := by
    intro a
    rw [← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply]
    rfl
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing D := hV.noeth
  refine ⟨@IsCO.mk O (D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) _ _ _ _ hloc inferInstance ?_ ?_ ?_, hMeq⟩
  · rw [hMeq]
    exact adicComplete_of_pow_eq_bot M (N + 1) hMpow
  · refine ⟨fun a ha => ?_⟩
    have hC : IsUnit (algebraMap O C a) := by
      by_contra hnu
      have hcm : algebraMap O C a ∈ maximalIdeal C := hnu
      rw [← hV.comap, Ideal.mem_comap, ← hmem, ← halg, ← hMeq] at hcm
      exact hcm ha
    exact (IsCO.isLocalHom (O := O) (R := C)).map_nonunit a hC
  · intro x
    obtain ⟨d, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective x
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hV.surj d
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) c
    refine ⟨a, ?_⟩
    rw [hMeq, halg, ← map_sub, hmem]
    have hca : algebraMap C D (c - algebraMap O C a) ∈ 𝔪 := by
      rw [← Ideal.mem_comap, hV.comap]
      exact ha
    have := 𝔪.add_mem hc hca
    rwa [map_sub, sub_add_sub_cancel] at this


[APD_SRC_ALG_FAMILY] Compatible maps from the supplied Space to all vertex quotients
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgFamily.lean#L7-L47
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_family :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ} (S : Space O F n) (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {D : Type} [CommRing D] [Algebra O D] [Algebra C D] [IsScalarTower O C D] (𝔪 : Ideal D) (ρ : NRep F n D), IsVertexRing C 𝔪 → IsModel O C 𝔪 ρ → ∃ f : ∀ N : ℕ, S.R →ₐ[O] D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1), (∀ N, S.ρ.map (f N : S.R →+* D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) = ρ.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)))) ∧ (∀ N x d, Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1 + 1)) d = f (N + 1) x → Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) d = f N x) ∧ ∀ N, ∀ x ∈ maximalIdeal S.R ^ (N + 1), f N x = 0 := by
  intro l _ O _ _ F n S C _ _ _ D _ _ _ _ 𝔪 ρ hV hM
  choose hco hmax using alg_quot O C 𝔪 hV
  have hex : ∀ N, ∃! f : S.R →ₐ[O] D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1),
      S.ρ.map (f : S.R →+* D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) = ρ.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1))) := by
    intro N
    letI := hco N
    exact S.univ _ _ (alg_lift C 𝔪 ρ hM _ (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O (𝔪 ^ (N + 1))) (fun x hx => by
      rw [hmax N]
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ hx))
  choose f hf huniq using hex
  refine ⟨f, hf, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro N x d hd
    have hle : 𝔪 ^ (N + 1 + 1) ≤ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1) := Ideal.pow_le_pow_right (Nat.le_succ _)
    have hcomp : (Ideal.Quotient.factorₐ O hle).comp (f (N + 1)) = f N := by
      apply huniq N
      have e : (((Ideal.Quotient.factorₐ O hle).comp (f (N + 1)) : S.R →ₐ[O] D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) :
          S.R →+* D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) =
          ((Ideal.Quotient.factorₐ O hle : D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1 + 1) →ₐ[O] D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) :
            D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1 + 1) →+* D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)).comp (f (N + 1) : S.R →+* D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1 + 1)) := rfl
      rw [e, ← NRep.map_map, hf (N + 1), NRep.map_map]
      rfl
    rw [← hcomp, AlgHom.comp_apply, ← hd]
    rfl
  · intro N x hx
    letI := hco N
    have hm : ∀ y ∈ maximalIdeal S.R,
        f N y ∈ Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1))) 𝔪 := by
      intro y hy
      rw [← hmax N, ← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]
      exact hy
    have hle : Ideal.map (f N : S.R →+* D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (maximalIdeal S.R) ≤
        Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1))) 𝔪 := by
      rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
      exact hm
    have hx' := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem (f N : S.R →+* D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) hx
    rw [Ideal.map_pow] at hx'
    have h2 := Ideal.pow_right_mono hle (N + 1) hx'
    rw [← Ideal.map_pow, Ideal.map_quotient_self, Ideal.mem_bot] at h2
    exact h2


[APD_SRC_ALG_CLOSED] Source coefficient-closedness step; APD uses finite-residue compactness at full alg's actual scope
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgClosed.lean#L6-L44
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_closed :
    ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] [IsNoetherianRing C] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal C) C] {D : Type} [CommRing D] [Algebra C D] (𝔪 : Ideal D), IsVertexRing C 𝔪 → ∀ c : ℕ → C, (∀ N, algebraMap C D (c (N + 1) - c N) ∈ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) → ∃ x : C, ∀ N, algebraMap C D (x - c N) ∈ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1) := by
  intro C _ _ _ _ D _ _ 𝔪 h c hc
  let J : ℕ → Ideal C := fun n => (𝔪 ^ n).comap (algebraMap C D)
  have hJ : Antitone J := fun _ _ hab => Ideal.comap_mono (Ideal.pow_le_pow_right hab)
  have h0 : ⨅ n, J n = ⊥ := by
    simp only [J]
    rw [← Ideal.comap_iInf, h.sep, ← RingHom.ker_eq_comap_bot, ← RingHom.injective_iff_ker_eq_bot]
    exact h.inj
  choose s hs using GlobalTheory.exists_le_maximalIdeal_pow_of_iInf_eq_bot J hJ h0
  have hd : ∀ N d, c (N + d) - c N ∈ J (N + 1) := fun N d => by
    induction d with
    | zero => simp
    | succ d ih =>
      have h1 : c (N + d + 1) - c (N + d) ∈ J (N + 1) := hJ (show N + 1 ≤ N + d + 1 by omega) (hc (N + d))
      have := (J (N + 1)).add_mem h1 ih
      rwa [sub_add_sub_cancel] at this
  have hle : ∀ {a b}, a ≤ b → c b - c a ∈ J (a + 1) := fun {a b} hab => by
    obtain ⟨d, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_add_of_le hab
    exact hd a d
  let t : ℕ → ℕ := fun k => k + ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (k + 1), s i
  have ht : ∀ k, s k ≤ t k := fun k => by
    simp only [t, Finset.sum_range_succ]
    omega
  have ht' : ∀ k, k ≤ t k := fun k => Nat.le_add_right _ _
  have htm : Monotone t := fun a b hab =>
    Nat.add_le_add hab (Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset (Finset.range_mono (by omega)))
  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := IsPrecomplete.prec' (I := maximalIdeal C) (fun k => c (t k)) fun {m n} hmn => by
    rw [SModEq.sub_mem, smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top, ← neg_mem_iff, neg_sub]
    exact hs m (hJ (by have := ht m; omega) (hle (htm hmn)))
  simp only [SModEq.sub_mem, smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top] at hL
  refine ⟨L, fun N => ?_⟩
  have h1 : c (t (N + 1)) - L ∈ J (N + 1) := by
    have := hL (N + 1)
    rw [← h.comap] at this
    exact Ideal.le_comap_pow _ (N + 1) this
  have h2 : c (t (N + 1)) - c N ∈ J (N + 1) := hle (by have := ht' (N + 1); omega)
  have h3 := (J (N + 1)).sub_mem h2 h1
  rwa [sub_sub_sub_cancel_left] at h3


[APD_SRC_ALG_LIMIT] Original recovery of A and B from quotient maps; broader alg_limit is not claimed
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgLimit.lean#L7-L126
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg_limit :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ} (S : Space O F n) (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] {D : Type} [CommRing D] [Algebra O D] [Algebra C D] [IsScalarTower O C D] (𝔪 : Ideal D) (ρ : NRep F n D), IsVertexRing C 𝔪 → IsModel O C 𝔪 ρ → ∃ (ξ : S.A →ₐ[O] D) (β : S.B →ₐ[O] C), S.u.map (ξ : S.A →+* D) = ρ ∧ ∀ b, ξ (S.inc b) = algebraMap C D (β b) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ F n S C _ _ _ D _ _ _ _ 𝔪 ρ hV hM
  obtain ⟨f, hf1, hf2, hf3⟩ := alg_family S C 𝔪 ρ hV hM
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal C) C := IsCO.complete (O := O)
  have hsep : ∀ d d' : D, (∀ N : ℕ, Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) d = Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) d') → d = d' := by
    intro d d' h
    have hmem : d - d' ∈ ⨅ N, 𝔪 ^ N := by
      refine (Submodule.mem_iInf _).2 fun N => ?_
      cases N with
      | zero => simp
      | succ N => exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 (h N)
    rw [hV.sep] at hmem
    exact sub_eq_zero.1 ((Submodule.mem_bot _).1 hmem)
  have hr : ∀ N g i j, f N (S.ρ.r g i j) = Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (ρ.r g i j) := by
    intro N g i j
    have := congrArg (fun σ => σ.r g i j) (hf1 N)
    simpa only [NRep.map_r, AlgHom.coe_toRingHom] using this
  have hψ : ∀ N v i g, f N ((S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R)) = Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (ρ.ψ v i g : D) := by
    intro N v i g
    have := congrArg (fun σ => ((σ.ψ v i g : Units _) : D ⧸ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1))) (hf1 N)
    simpa only [NRep.map_ψ, AlgHom.coe_toRingHom] using this
  let T' : Subalgebra O S.R :=
    { carrier := {x | ∃ c : C, ∀ N, Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (algebraMap C D c) = f N x}
      mul_mem' := by
        rintro a b ⟨c, hc⟩ ⟨c', hc'⟩
        exact ⟨c * c', fun N => by simp only [map_mul, hc, hc']⟩
      add_mem' := by
        rintro a b ⟨c, hc⟩ ⟨c', hc'⟩
        exact ⟨c + c', fun N => by simp only [map_add, hc, hc']⟩
      algebraMap_mem' := fun a => ⟨algebraMap O C a, fun N => by
        rw [AlgHom.commutes, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply]
        rfl⟩ }
  let T : Subalgebra O S.R :=
    { carrier := {x | ∃ d : D, ∀ N, Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) d = f N x}
      mul_mem' := by
        rintro a b ⟨c, hc⟩ ⟨c', hc'⟩
        exact ⟨c * c', fun N => by simp only [map_mul, hc, hc']⟩
      add_mem' := by
        rintro a b ⟨c, hc⟩ ⟨c', hc'⟩
        exact ⟨c + c', fun N => by simp only [map_add, hc, hc']⟩
      algebraMap_mem' := fun a => ⟨algebraMap O D a, fun N => by
        rw [AlgHom.commutes]
        rfl⟩ }
  have hgen : Algebra.adjoin O (S.ρ.mon 0 ∪ {x | ∃ v i g, x = ↑((S.ρ.ψ v i) g)}) ≤ T' := by
    refine Algebra.adjoin_le ?_
    rintro x (hx | ⟨v, i, g, rfl⟩)
    · obtain ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩ := hx
      obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hM.mon _ ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩
      refine ⟨c, fun N => ?_⟩
      rw [hc, map_list_prod, map_list_prod, List.map_map, List.map_map]
      congr 1
      refine List.map_congr_left ?_
      intro s _
      simp only [Function.comp, hr]
    · obtain ⟨c, hc, -⟩ := hM.psi v i g
      exact ⟨c, fun N => by rw [hc, hψ]⟩
  have hB : S.B ≤ T' := by
    intro x hx
    have hx' : ∀ N : ℕ, ∃ y ∈ Algebra.adjoin O (S.ρ.mon 0 ∪ {x | ∃ v i g, x = ↑((S.ρ.ψ v i) g)}), x - y ∈ maximalIdeal S.R ^ N := hx
    choose y hy hxy using hx'
    choose c hc using fun N => hgen (hy (N + 1))
    have hfx : ∀ N M, N + 1 ≤ M → f N x = f N (y M) := by
      intro N M hNM
      have h0 := hf3 N _ (Ideal.pow_le_pow_right hNM (hxy M))
      rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h0
      exact h0
    have hcau : ∀ N : ℕ, algebraMap C D (c (N + 1) - c N) ∈ 𝔪 ^ (N + 1) := by
      intro N
      rw [map_sub]
      refine Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 ?_
      rw [hc, hc, ← hfx N (N + 1 + 1) (by omega), ← hfx N (N + 1) le_rfl]
    obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := alg_closed C 𝔪 hV c hcau
    refine ⟨z, fun N => ?_⟩
    have h1 : Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (algebraMap C D z) = Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (algebraMap C D (c N)) := by
      refine Ideal.Quotient.eq.2 ?_
      rw [← map_sub]
      exact hz N
    rw [h1, hc, ← hfx N (N + 1) le_rfl]
  have hA : S.A ≤ T := by
    show Algebra.adjoin O ((S.B : Set S.R) ∪ {x | ∃ g i j, x = S.ρ.r g i j}) ≤ T
    refine Algebra.adjoin_le ?_
    rintro x (hx | ⟨g, i, j, rfl⟩)
    · obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hB hx
      exact ⟨algebraMap C D c, hc⟩
    · exact ⟨ρ.r g i j, fun N => (hr N g i j).symm⟩
  obtain ⟨ξ, hξ⟩ := alg_glue (A := S.A) 𝔪 hV.sep (fun N => (f N).comp S.A.val) (fun a => by
    obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := hA a.2
    exact ⟨d, fun N => by simpa using hd N⟩)
  have hξ' : ∀ (a : S.A) N, Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (ξ a) = f N (a : S.R) := by
    intro a N
    simpa using hξ a N
  let φ := IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O C D
  have hmem : ∀ b, (ξ.comp S.inc) b ∈ φ.range := by
    intro b
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hB b.2
    refine (AlgHom.mem_range φ).2 ⟨c, ?_⟩
    refine hsep _ _ fun N => ?_
    show Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (algebraMap C D c) = Ideal.Quotient.mk (𝔪 ^ (N + 1)) (ξ (S.inc b))
    rw [hc, hξ']
    rfl
  let e := AlgEquiv.ofInjective φ hV.inj
  refine ⟨ξ, e.symm.toAlgHom.comp ((ξ.comp S.inc).codRestrict φ.range hmem), ?_, ?_⟩
  · refine NRep.ext ?_ ?_
    · refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => Matrix.ext fun i j => ?_
      rw [NRep.map_r]
      refine hsep _ _ fun N => ?_
      rw [← hr]
      exact hξ' _ N
    · funext v i
      refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => Units.ext ?_
      rw [NRep.map_ψ]
      refine hsep _ _ fun N => ?_
      rw [← hψ]
      exact hξ' _ N
  · intro b
    have h1 : ((e (e.symm ⟨ξ (S.inc b), hmem b⟩) : φ.range) : D) = φ (e.symm ⟨ξ (S.inc b), hmem b⟩) :=
      AlgEquiv.ofInjective_apply _ _ _
    rw [AlgEquiv.apply_symm_apply] at h1
    exact h1


[APD_SRC_ALG] Full original alg, including injective coefficient structure and finite Laurent evaluation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/AlgVring.lean#L6-L74
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.AlgModel
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.AlgLimit

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem alg_vring {C K : Type} [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] [CommRing K] [Algebra C K]
    {P : ℤ → Submodule C K} [SetLike.GradedMonoid P] (h : HasVertex P) (hi : Function.Injective (algebraMap C K))
    (hn : IsNoetherianRing (lauSub P)) : IsVertexRing C (vertex h) := by
  have hcoeff : ∀ c : C, ((algebraMap C (lauSub P) c : lauSub P) : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ).coeff 0 = algebraMap C K c := by
    intro c
    simp
  refine ⟨hn, ?_, alg_sep h hn, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro c c' hcc
    apply hi
    rw [← hcoeff c, ← hcoeff c', hcc]
  · ext c
    rw [Ideal.mem_comap, mem_vertex, hcoeff]
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨c', hc', he⟩
      rwa [← hi he]
    · intro hc
      exact ⟨c, hc, rfl⟩
  · intro d
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := h.zero _ (d.2 0)
    refine ⟨c, ?_⟩
    rw [mem_vertex]
    refine ⟨0, (maximalIdeal C).zero_mem, ?_⟩
    simp [hc]

private theorem alg_ev (C K : Type) [CommRing C] [CommRing K] [Algebra C K] :
    ∃ e : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ →ₐ[C] K, ∀ j x, e (AddMonoidAlgebra.single j x) = x := by
  refine ⟨AddMonoidAlgebra.liftNCAlgHom (AlgHom.id C K) 1 (fun _ _ => Commute.one_right _), fun j x => ?_⟩
  exact (AddMonoidAlgebra.liftNC_single _ _ _ _).trans (by simp)

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.alg :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (K : Type) [CommRing K] [Algebra O K] [Algebra C K] [IsScalarTower O C K], Function.Injective (algebraMap C K) → ∀ ρ : NRep F n K, Cone O C ρ → ∃ (ξ : S.A →ₐ[O] K) (β : S.B →ₐ[O] C), S.u.map (ξ : S.A →+* K) = ρ ∧ ∀ b, ξ (S.inc b) = algebraMap C K (β b) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S C _ _ _ K _ _ _ _ hi ρ hcone
  have hv := alg_vertex C ρ hcone
  haveI := alg_ft C ρ hcone.fg
  have hN : IsNoetherianRing (lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) := Algebra.FiniteType.isNoetherianRing C _
  have hvr := alg_vring hv hi hN
  obtain ⟨ρD, h1, h2⟩ := alg_lau C ρ hcone
  have hmod := alg_model C ρ hcone hv ρD h1 h2
  letI : Algebra O (lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) :=
    ((algebraMap C (lauSub (NRep.part C ρ))).comp (algebraMap O C)).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower O C (lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq' rfl
  obtain ⟨ξ, β, hξ, hβ⟩ := alg_limit S C (vertex hv) ρD hvr hmod
  obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := alg_ev C K
  let ev : lauSub (NRep.part C ρ) →ₐ[O] K := (e.comp (lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)).val).restrictScalars O
  refine ⟨ev.comp ξ, β, ?_, fun b => ?_⟩
  · apply NRep.ext
    · ext g i j
      rw [NRep.map_r]
      have hr := NRep.map_r (ξ : S.A →+* lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) S.u g i j
      rw [hξ] at hr
      show e ((ξ (S.u.r g i j) : lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) = _
      have hr' : ξ (S.u.r g i j) = ρD.r g i j := hr.symm
      rw [hr', h1, he]
    · funext v i
      ext g
      rw [NRep.map_ψ]
      have hp := NRep.map_ψ (ξ : S.A →+* lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) S.u v i g
      rw [hξ] at hp
      show e ((ξ ((S.u.ψ v i g : S.Aˣ) : S.A) : lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) = _
      have hp' : ξ ((S.u.ψ v i g : S.Aˣ) : S.A) = ((ρD.ψ v i g : (lauSub (NRep.part C ρ))ˣ) : lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) := hp.symm
      rw [hp', h2, he]
  · show e ((ξ (S.inc b) : lauSub (NRep.part C ρ)) : AddMonoidAlgebra K ℤ) = _
    rw [hβ b]
    exact e.commutes (β b)


[APD_SRC_EXT_A] Full ext_A with arbitrary Noetherian local, possibly incomplete target
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ExtA.lean#L6-L63
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.ext_A :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] [IsNoetherianRing C] [Algebra O C] (ξ ξ' : S.A →ₐ[O] C), S.u.map (ξ : S.A →+* C) = S.u.map (ξ' : S.A →+* C) → S.x0 ≤ (maximalIdeal C).comap (ξ : S.A →+* C) → S.x0 ≤ (maximalIdeal C).comap (ξ' : S.A →+* C) → ξ = ξ' := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S C _ _ _ _ ξ ξ' hu h1 h2
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, hc⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Fr.cont_u hF hn S
  let E : Subalgebra O S.R := (AlgHom.equalizer ξ ξ').map S.A.val
  have hE : ∀ a : S.A, (a : S.R) ∈ E ↔ ξ a = ξ' a := fun a =>
    ⟨fun ⟨b, hb, h⟩ => (Subtype.ext h : b = a) ▸ hb, fun h => ⟨a, h, rfl⟩⟩
  have hr : ∀ g i j, S.ρ.r g i j ∈ E := fun g i j =>
    (hE (S.u.r g i j)).2 (congrArg (fun ρ : NRep F n C => ρ.r g i j) hu)
  have hψ : ∀ v i g, (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R) ∈ E := fun v i g =>
    (hE (S.u.ψ v i g : S.A)).2 (congrArg (fun ρ : NRep F n C => (ρ.ψ v i g : C)) hu)
  have h0 : Algebra.adjoin O (S.ρ.mon 0 ∪ {x | ∃ v i g, x = (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R)}) ≤ E := by
    refine Algebra.adjoin_le ?_
    rintro x (⟨L, -, rfl⟩ | ⟨v, i, g, rfl⟩)
    · refine Subalgebra.list_prod_mem E fun y hy => ?_
      obtain ⟨s, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hy
      exact hr _ _ _
    · exact hψ v i g
  have hpow : ∀ (f : S.A →ₐ[O] C), S.x0 ≤ (maximalIdeal C).comap (f : S.A →+* C) → ∀ (N : ℕ) (a : S.A),
      a ∈ S.mB ^ N • S.gr 0 → f a ∈ maximalIdeal C ^ N := by
    intro f hf N a ha
    refine Submodule.smul_induction_on ha (fun b hb y _ => ?_) fun x y hx hy => ?_
    · rw [Algebra.smul_def, map_mul]
      refine Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ ?_
      have h3 : algebraMap S.B S.A b ∈ S.x0 ^ N := by
        have := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A) hb
        rw [Ideal.map_pow] at this
        exact this
      have h4 := Ideal.pow_right_mono hf N h3
      exact Ideal.le_comap_pow (f : S.A →+* C) N h4
    · rw [map_add]
      exact add_mem hx hy
  have hB : S.B ≤ E := by
    intro b hb
    refine (hE ⟨b, S.B_le_A hb⟩).2 ?_
    rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← Ideal.mem_bot,
      ← Ideal.iInf_pow_eq_bot_of_isLocalRing (maximalIdeal C) (maximalIdeal.isMaximal C).ne_top, Ideal.mem_iInf]
    intro N
    obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := hc 0 N
    obtain ⟨y, hy, hby⟩ := hb M
    have hyB : y ∈ S.B := le_adicClosure _ hy
    have hg : S.inc ⟨b - y, sub_mem hb hyB⟩ ∈ S.gr 0 := by
      have h5 : (1 : S.A) ∈ S.gr 0 := Submodule.subset_span ⟨[], by simp, by simp⟩
      have h6 := Submodule.smul_mem _ (⟨b - y, sub_mem hb hyB⟩ : S.B) h5
      rw [Algebra.smul_def, mul_one] at h6
      exact h6
    have hm := hM _ hg hby
    have he : ξ ⟨y, S.B_le_A hyB⟩ = ξ' ⟨y, S.B_le_A hyB⟩ := (hE _).1 (h0 hy)
    have hs : (⟨b, S.B_le_A hb⟩ : S.A) = S.inc ⟨b - y, sub_mem hb hyB⟩ + ⟨y, S.B_le_A hyB⟩ :=
      Subtype.ext (sub_add_cancel b y).symm
    rw [hs, map_add, map_add, he, add_sub_add_right_eq_sub]
    exact sub_mem (hpow ξ h1 N _ hm) (hpow ξ' h2 N _ hm)
  have hA : S.A ≤ E := by
    refine Algebra.adjoin_le ?_
    rintro x (hx | ⟨g, i, j, rfl⟩)
    · exact hB hx
    · exact hr g i j
  exact AlgHom.ext fun a => (hE a).1 (hA a.2)


[APD_SRC_DEF_ISO] Full original def_iso and actual local normalized universality
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/DefIso.lean#L34-L126
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.def_iso :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra L.O' C] [IsCO L.O' C] (ρ : NRep F n C), ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal C) → ρ.map (res L.O' C : C →+* ResidueField L.O') = L.ρ.map (res L.O' L.R : L.R →+* ResidueField L.O') → ∃! g : L.R →ₐ[L.O'] C, L.ρ.map (g : L.R →+* C) = ρ := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L C _ _ _ ρ hc hres
  have hOloc : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O L.O') := by
    refine ⟨fun a ha => ?_⟩
    by_contra h
    have hm : algebraMap O L.O' a ∈ maximalIdeal L.O' := by
      rw [← L.unr]
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ((IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal a).mpr h)
    exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).mp hm ha
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap L.O' C) := IsCO.isLocalHom
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap L.O' L.R) := IsCO.isLocalHom
  letI : Algebra O C := ((algebraMap L.O' C).comp (algebraMap O L.O')).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower O L.O' C := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq (fun _ => rfl)
  have hCloc : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O C) :=
    inferInstanceAs (IsLocalHom ((algebraMap L.O' C).comp (algebraMap O L.O')))
  have hCL : UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCL O C := by
    refine ⟨inferInstance, IsCO.complete (O := L.O'), hCloc, ?_⟩
    let φ : L.O' →ₐ[O] ResidueField C :=
      (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O C (ResidueField C)).comp (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O L.O' C)
    refine Module.Finite.of_surjective φ.toLinearMap ?_
    intro y
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective y
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := L.O') z
    refine ⟨a, ?_⟩
    show IsLocalRing.residue C (algebraMap L.O' C a) = IsLocalRing.residue C z
    rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, ← neg_sub]
    exact neg_mem ha
  let f0 : ResidueField O →+* ResidueField L.O' := IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map (algebraMap O L.O')
  let jC : ResidueField L.O' →+* ResidueField C := IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map (algebraMap L.O' C)
  let jR : ResidueField L.O' →+* ResidueField L.R :=
    IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map (algebraMap L.O' L.R)
  have hfC : IsResMap O C (jC.comp f0) := by
    intro a
    show jC (f0 (IsLocalRing.residue O a)) = _
    rw [IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue, IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue]
    rfl
  have hfR : IsResMap O L.R (jR.comp f0) := by
    intro a
    show jR (f0 (IsLocalRing.residue O a)) = _
    rw [IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue, IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue,
      IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O L.O' L.R a]
  have hbR := (loc_rho hF hn S x L).2.1 _ hfR
  rw [residue_eq_residueField_map_comp_res (O' := L.O') (C := L.R)] at hbR
  have hσ : NRep.IsBar f0 (NRep.map (res L.O' L.R : L.R →+* ResidueField L.O') L.ρ) :=
    (nRep_isBar_comp_map_iff f0 jR _).mp hbR
  have hbar : NRep.IsBar (jC.comp f0) (NRep.map (IsLocalRing.residue C) ρ) := by
    rw [residue_eq_residueField_map_comp_res (O' := L.O') (C := C)]
    show NRep.IsBar (jC.comp f0) (NRep.map jC (NRep.map (res L.O' C : C →+* ResidueField L.O') ρ))
    rw [hres]
    exact (nRep_isBar_comp_map_iff f0 jC _).mpr hσ
  obtain ⟨ξ, hξ, hξx⟩ := point_loc hF hn S C hCL (jC.comp f0) hfC ρ hc hbar
  have hx0 : S.x0 ≤ x := by
    unfold Space.x0
    exact Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.mpr (loc_flat hF hn S x L).2.2
  let ξ1 : S.A →ₐ[O] ResidueField L.O' := ((res L.O' C).restrictScalars O).comp ξ
  let ξ2 : S.A →ₐ[O] ResidueField L.O' := ((res L.O' L.R).restrictScalars O).comp L.ι
  have h12 : ξ1 = ξ2 := by
    refine ext_A hF hn S (ResidueField L.O') ξ1 ξ2 ?_ ?_ ?_
    · exact (congrArg (NRep.map (res L.O' C : C →+* ResidueField L.O')) hξ).trans hres
    · intro a ha
      refine Ideal.mem_comap.mpr ?_
      show res L.O' C (ξ a) ∈ _
      rw [(res_eq_zero_iff L.O' C (ξ a)).mpr (hξx ha)]
      exact zero_mem _
    · intro a ha
      have hm : a ∈ Ideal.comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) (maximalIdeal L.R) := by
        rw [L.lies]
        exact hx0 ha
      refine Ideal.mem_comap.mpr ?_
      show res L.O' L.R (L.ι a) ∈ _
      rw [(res_eq_zero_iff L.O' L.R (L.ι a)).mpr hm]
      exact zero_mem _
  obtain ⟨g, hg, huniq⟩ := L.univ C ξ (fun a => AlgHom.congr_fun h12 a)
  have hgι : (g : L.R →+* C).comp (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) = (ξ : S.A →+* C) := RingHom.ext hg
  refine ⟨g, ?_, fun g' hg' => huniq g' ?_⟩
  · show NRep.map ((g : L.R →+* C).comp (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R)) S.u = ρ
    rw [hgι]
    exact hξ
  · let ξ' : S.A →ₐ[O] C := (g'.restrictScalars O).comp L.ι
    have h' : ξ' = ξ := by
      refine ext_A hF hn S C ξ' ξ ?_ ?_ hξx
      · exact hg'.trans hξ.symm
      · intro a ha
        have hm : a ∈ Ideal.comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) (maximalIdeal L.R) := by
          rw [L.lies]
          exact hx0 ha
        refine Ideal.mem_comap.mpr ?_
        show g' (L.ι a) ∈ maximalIdeal C
        rw [← res_eq_zero_iff L.O' C, res_map]
        exact (res_eq_zero_iff L.O' L.R _).mpr hm
    intro a
    exact AlgHom.congr_fun h' a


[APD_SRC_BOX] Explicit semidirect extension and full pr_box
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ConjVal.lean#L8-L99

private noncomputable def ee (F : Frame l k) (u : ℤˣ) : F.Γ := if u = 1 then 1 else F.c

private theorem ee_one : ee F 1 = 1 := if_pos rfl

private theorem ee_neg : ee F (-1) = F.c := if_neg (by decide)

private noncomputable def pr (hc : F.del F.c = -1) (s : F.Γ) : F.G0 :=
  ⟨s * ee F (F.del s), by
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul]
    rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (F.del s) with h | h
    · rw [h, ee_one, map_one, mul_one]
    · rw [h, ee_neg, hc]; simp⟩

private theorem pr_val (hc : F.del F.c = -1) (s : F.Γ) : (pr hc s : F.Γ) = s * ee F (F.del s) := rfl

private theorem pr_coe (hc : F.del F.c = -1) (s : F.G0) : pr hc s = s := by
  apply Subtype.ext
  have h : F.del s = 1 := s.2
  rw [pr_val, h, ee_one, mul_one]

private theorem conj_val (s : F.G0) : (F.conj s : F.Γ) = F.c * s * F.c⁻¹ := rfl

private theorem del_lc (ho : IsOpen (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)) : IsLocallyConstant F.del := by
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro x
  have h1 : IsOpen ((fun y => x⁻¹ * y) ⁻¹' (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)) := ho.preimage (by fun_prop)
  filter_upwards [h1.mem_nhds (show x⁻¹ * x ∈ F.del.ker by simp)] with y hy
  have h2 : F.del (x⁻¹ * y) = 1 := hy
  rw [map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at h2
  exact h2.symm

private theorem pr_cont (hc : F.del F.c = -1) (ho : IsOpen (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)) : Continuous (pr hc) :=
  Continuous.subtype_mk (continuous_id.mul ((del_lc ho).comp (ee F)).continuous) _

private theorem theta_r {n : ℕ} {C : Type} [CommRing C] (ρ : NRep F n C) (g : F.G0) :
    theta (ρ.r.toHomUnits g) = ρ.r.toHomUnits (F.conj g) := by
  apply Units.ext
  rw [theta_val, ← map_inv, MonoidHom.coe_toHomUnits, MonoidHom.coe_toHomUnits, ← ρ.pol, Frame.star, inv_inv]

private noncomputable def rp (hc : F.del F.c = -1) (hs : F.c * F.c = 1) {n : ℕ} {C : Type} [CommRing C]
    (ρ : NRep F n C) : F.Γ →* GG n C where
  toFun s := ⟨ρ.r.toHomUnits (pr hc s), F.del s⟩
  map_one' := by
    have h : pr hc (1 : F.Γ) = 1 := pr_coe hc 1
    ext1
    · simp [h]
    · simp
  map_mul' s t := by
    have hi : F.c⁻¹ = F.c := inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_right hs
    have hs' : ∀ x, F.c * (F.c * x) = x := fun x => by rw [← mul_assoc, hs, one_mul]
    ext1
    · rw [SemidirectProduct.mul_left]
      dsimp only
      rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (F.del s) with h | h
      · rw [h, sgnAct_one, ← map_mul]
        congr 1
        apply Subtype.ext
        rw [Subgroup.coe_mul, pr_val, pr_val, pr_val, map_mul, h, ee_one, one_mul, mul_one, mul_assoc]
      · rw [h, sgnAct_neg_one, theta_r, ← map_mul]
        congr 1
        apply Subtype.ext
        rw [Subgroup.coe_mul, conj_val, pr_val, pr_val, pr_val, map_mul, h, ee_neg, hi]
        rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (F.del t) with h' | h'
        · rw [h', mul_one, ee_neg, ee_one]
          simp only [mul_assoc, mul_one, hs']
        · rw [h', neg_mul_neg, mul_one, ee_neg, ee_one]
          simp only [mul_assoc, mul_one, hs', hs]
    · simp

end UpperTheory.V.Fr

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_box :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] (I : Ideal C) (ρ : NRep F n C), ρ.Cont I → (∀ v, UpperTheory.V.W.HasFlag (ρ.r.comp (F.loc0 v)).toHomUnits (ρ.ψ v)) → ∃ B : Box F n C, B.Of ρ ∧ B.Cont I := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF C _ I ρ hc hf
  choose g hg using hf
  have hm : ∀ s, (rp hF.del_c hF.c_sq ρ s).mat = ρ.r (pr hF.del_c s) := fun s => rfl
  refine ⟨⟨rp hF.del_c hF.c_sq ρ, fun s => rfl, g, fun v s i j h => ?_⟩, ⟨?_, fun s => ?_, fun v i s => ?_⟩,
    fun i j N => ?_⟩
  · have e : pr hF.del_c (F.loc v s) = F.loc0 v s := pr_coe hF.del_c (F.loc0 v s)
    rw [hm, e]
    exact (hg v s).1 i j h
  · have e : pr hF.del_c F.c = 1 := by
      apply Subtype.ext
      rw [pr_val, hF.del_c, ee_neg, hF.c_sq]
      rfl
    show (rp hF.del_c hF.c_sq ρ F.c).mat = 1
    rw [hm, e, map_one]
  · show (rp hF.del_c hF.c_sq ρ s).mat = _
    rw [hm, pr_coe]
  · exact (hg v s).2 i
  · exact (hc.1 i j N).comp_continuous (pr_cont hF.del_c hF.del_open)


[APD_SRC_OFBOX] Full pr_ofbox retaining exact ordered ordinary characters
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrOfbox.lean#L7-L66
    (h : ∀ s, UpT (m s)) (i : Fin n) : H →* C where
  toFun s := m s i i
  map_one' := by simp
  map_mul' s t := by
    rw [map_mul, Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single i]
    · intro b _ hb
      rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hb with h' | h'
      · rw [h s i b h', zero_mul]
      · rw [h t b i h', mul_zero]
    · simp

private def conjHom {H R : Type} [Group H] [Monoid R] (u : Rˣ) (m : H →* R) : H →* R where
  toFun s := (u : R) * m s * ((u⁻¹ : Rˣ) : R)
  map_one' := by simp
  map_mul' s t := by simp [mul_assoc]

private theorem cont_conj {X C : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] [CommRing C] {n : ℕ} (I : Ideal C)
    (m : X → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) (hm : ∀ i j, AdicCont I fun s => m s i j) (a b : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)
    (i j : Fin n) : AdicCont I fun s => (a * m s * b) i j := by
  intro N
  have e : (fun s => Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) ((a * m s * b) i j)) = ∑ q, ∑ p, fun s => Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (a i p) * Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (m s p q) * Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (b q j) := by
    funext s
    simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_mul, map_sum, map_mul, Finset.sum_apply]
  rw [e]
  refine Finset.sum_induction _ IsLocallyConstant (fun _ _ hf hg => hf.add hg) (IsLocallyConstant.const 0) (fun q _ => ?_)
  refine Finset.sum_induction _ IsLocallyConstant (fun _ _ hf hg => hf.add hg) (IsLocallyConstant.const 0) (fun p _ => ?_)
  exact ((IsLocallyConstant.const _).mul (hm p q N)).mul (IsLocallyConstant.const _)

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_ofbox :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] (I : Ideal C) (B : Box F n C), (B.r F.c).mat = 1 → (∀ i j, i ≠ j → ((B.r F.g0).mat - (B.r (F.star F.g0)).mat) i j = 0) → B.Cont I → ∃ ρ : NRep F n C, B.Of ρ ∧ ρ.Cont I := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF C _ I B hc hdg hcont
  let rr : F.G0 →* Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C := (Units.coeHom _).comp (glPart F.del B.r B.nu)
  let m : ∀ v, F.Gv v →* Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C := fun v => conjHom (B.g v) (rr.comp (F.loc0 v))
  have hup : ∀ v s, UpT (m v s) := fun v s => B.tri v s
  let ψ : (v : F.V) → Fin n → F.Gv v →* Cˣ := fun v i => (diagHom (m v) (hup v) i).toHomUnits
  have hpol : ∀ g : F.G0, rr (F.star g) = Jmat C n * (rr g)ᵀ * Jmat C n := by
    intro g
    have hcc : F.c⁻¹ = F.c := inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_right hF.c_sq
    have hs : ((F.star g : F.G0) : F.Γ) = F.c * (↑g)⁻¹ * F.c := by
      show ((F.conj g⁻¹ : F.G0) : F.Γ) = _
      rw [Frame.conj, MulEquiv.coe_toMonoidHom, MulAut.conjNormal_apply, hcc]
      rfl
    have hPl : (B.r F.c).left = 1 := Units.ext hc
    have hPr : (B.r F.c).right = -1 := by rw [B.nu, hF.del_c]
    have hX : (B.r (↑g)⁻¹).left = (glPart F.del B.r B.nu g)⁻¹ := by
      rw [← map_inv]
      rfl
    show ((B.r ↑(F.star g)).left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) = _
    rw [hs, map_mul, map_mul, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_left]
    simp only [hPl, hPr, map_one, mul_one, one_mul, sgnAct_neg_one, hX, theta_val, inv_inv]
    rfl
  have hord : ∀ v (g : Fin n → F.Gv v), ordProd (fun s => rr (F.loc0 v s)) (fun i s => ((ψ v i s : Cˣ) : C)) g = 0 := by
    intro v g
    have hf : UpperTheory.V.W.HasFlag (rr.comp (F.loc0 v)).toHomUnits (ψ v) := ⟨B.g v, fun s => ⟨fun i j hij => hup v s i j hij, fun i => rfl⟩⟩
    exact (ord_of_flag (rr.comp (F.loc0 v)) (ψ v) hf).1 g
  refine ⟨⟨rr, ψ, hpol, fun i j hij => hdg i j hij, hord⟩, ⟨hc, fun s => rfl, fun v i s => rfl⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro i j N
    exact (hcont i j N).comp_continuous continuous_subtype_val
  · intro v i
    exact cont_conj I (fun s => (B.r (F.loc v s)).mat) (fun p q N => (hcont p q N).comp_continuous (continuous_subtype_val.comp (hF.loc_cont v))) ↑(B.g v) ↑(B.g v)⁻¹ i i


[APD_SRC_SEC] Full pr_sec, arbitrary topological domain, and discrete small kernel
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/InfPowBot.lean#L5-L81
private theorem inf_pow_bot (R : Type) [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [IsNoetherianRing R] (I : Ideal R)
    (h : maximalIdeal R * I = ⊥) : ∃ N : ℕ, ∀ x ∈ I, x ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ N → x = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Ideal.exists_pow_inf_eq_pow_smul (maximalIdeal R) (I : Submodule R R)
  refine ⟨k + 1, fun x hxI hxm => ?_⟩
  have hx : x ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ (k + 1) • (⊤ : Submodule R R) ⊓ (I : Submodule R R) := by
    refine ⟨?_, hxI⟩
    rw [Ideal.smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top]
    exact hxm
  rw [hk (k + 1) (by omega), Nat.add_sub_cancel_left, pow_one] at hx
  have hle : maximalIdeal R • (maximalIdeal R ^ k • (⊤ : Submodule R R) ⊓ (I : Submodule R R))
      ≤ maximalIdeal R • (I : Submodule R R) :=
    Submodule.smul_mono le_rfl inf_le_right
  have hx' := hle hx
  rw [Ideal.smul_eq_mul, h] at hx'
  exact (Submodule.mem_bot R).1 hx'

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_sec :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C), Function.Surjective p → maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → ∀ (Γ : Type) [TopologicalSpace Γ] (x : Γ → C), AdicCont (maximalIdeal C) x → ∃ y : Γ → C', AdicCont (maximalIdeal C') y ∧ ∀ g, p (y g) = x g := by
  intro O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ _ p hp hker Γ _ x hx
  have hmem : ∀ z : C', p z ∈ maximalIdeal C ↔ z ∈ maximalIdeal C' := by
    intro z
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O C, ← res_eq_zero_iff O C', res_map]
  have hmap : Ideal.map p (maximalIdeal C') = maximalIdeal C := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
      intro z hz
      exact (hmem z).2 hz
    · intro c hc
      obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := hp c
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem p ((hmem z).1 hc)
  have hpow : ∀ n : ℕ, ∀ c ∈ maximalIdeal C ^ n, ∃ z ∈ maximalIdeal C' ^ n, p z = c := by
    intro n c hc
    rw [← hmap, ← Ideal.map_pow] at hc
    exact (Ideal.mem_map_iff_of_surjective p hp).1 hc
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing C' := IsCO.toIsNoetherian O
  obtain ⟨N₀, hN₀⟩ := inf_pow_bot C' (RingHom.ker p) hker
  have hq : Function.Surjective (fun z : C' => Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C ^ N₀) (p z)) :=
    Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective.comp hp
  let σ := Function.surjInv hq
  have hσ : ∀ c : C, c - p (σ (Ideal.Quotient.mk _ c)) ∈ maximalIdeal C ^ N₀ := by
    intro c
    apply Ideal.Quotient.eq.1
    exact (Function.surjInv_eq hq (Ideal.Quotient.mk _ c)).symm
  have hw : ∀ c : C, ∃ w ∈ maximalIdeal C' ^ N₀, p w = c - p (σ (Ideal.Quotient.mk _ c)) :=
    fun c => hpow N₀ _ (hσ c)
  choose w hwm hwp using hw
  let s : C → C' := fun c => σ (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C ^ N₀) c) + w c
  have hs : ∀ c, p (s c) = c := by
    intro c
    simp only [s, map_add, hwp]
    ring
  have hsd : ∀ n ≥ N₀, ∀ c₁ c₂ : C, c₁ - c₂ ∈ maximalIdeal C ^ n →
      s c₁ - s c₂ ∈ maximalIdeal C' ^ n := by
    intro n hn c₁ c₂ h
    have hcl : Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C ^ N₀) c₁ = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ c₂ :=
      Ideal.Quotient.eq.2 (Ideal.pow_le_pow_right hn h)
    obtain ⟨z, hz, hpz⟩ := hpow n _ h
    have hdiff : s c₁ - s c₂ = w c₁ - w c₂ := by
      simp only [s, hcl]
      ring
    have hz0 : (s c₁ - s c₂) - z = 0 := by
      apply hN₀
      · rw [RingHom.mem_ker]
        simp [map_sub, hs, hpz]
      · rw [hdiff]
        exact sub_mem (sub_mem (hwm c₁) (hwm c₂)) (Ideal.pow_le_pow_right hn hz)
    rw [sub_eq_zero.1 hz0]
    exact hz
  refine ⟨fun g => s (x g), ?_, fun g => hs (x g)⟩
  intro n
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro g
  have hxl := (IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq _).1 (hx (max n N₀)) g
  filter_upwards [hxl] with g' hg'
  apply Ideal.Quotient.eq.2
  exact Ideal.pow_le_pow_right (le_max_left n N₀)
    (hsd _ (le_max_right _ _) _ _ (Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 hg'))


[APD_SRC_SETLIFT] Continuous invertible and upper-triangular set lifts; full pr_setlift
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrSetlift.lean#L23-L106
private theorem gl_lift {n : ℕ} (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C']
    [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (hp : Function.Surjective p)
    (hk : maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥) (Γ : Type) [TopologicalSpace Γ] (x : Γ → GL (Fin n) C)
    (hx : ∀ i j, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C) fun s => x s i j) :
    ∃ y : Γ → GL (Fin n) C', (∀ i j, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C') fun s => y s i j) ∧
      (∀ s, GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (y s) = x s) ∧
      ∀ i j, (∀ s, x s i j = 0) → ∀ s, y s i j = 0 := by
  classical
  have hloc : ∀ a : C', IsUnit (p a) → IsUnit a := by
    intro a ha
    by_contra hna
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hp (↑ha.unit⁻¹ : C)
    have hK : a * b - 1 ∈ RingHom.ker (p : C' →+* C) := by
      rw [RingHom.mem_ker]
      show p (a * b - 1) = 0
      rw [map_sub, map_mul, map_one, hb, IsUnit.mul_val_inv, sub_self]
    have hKm : RingHom.ker (p : C' →+* C) ≤ maximalIdeal C' := IsLocalRing.le_maximalIdeal (RingHom.ker_ne_top _)
    have ham : a ∈ maximalIdeal C' := by
      rw [IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff]
      exact hna
    have h1 : (1 : C') ∈ maximalIdeal C' := by
      have h2 := sub_mem (Ideal.mul_mem_right b _ ham) (hKm hK)
      simpa using h2
    exact (maximalIdeal.isMaximal C').ne_top ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).2 h1)
  let Y : Fin n → Fin n → Γ → C' := fun i j =>
    if ∀ s, x s i j = 0 then fun _ => 0 else Classical.choose (pr_sec O C' C p hp hk Γ (fun s => x s i j) (hx i j))
  have hYc : ∀ i j, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C') (Y i j) := by
    intro i j
    by_cases h : ∀ s, x s i j = 0
    · simp only [Y, if_pos h]
      exact fun _ => IsLocallyConstant.const _
    · simp only [Y, if_neg h]
      exact (Classical.choose_spec (pr_sec O C' C p hp hk Γ (fun s => x s i j) (hx i j))).1
  have hYp : ∀ i j s, p (Y i j s) = x s i j := by
    intro i j s
    by_cases h : ∀ s, x s i j = 0
    · simp only [Y, if_pos h, map_zero]
      exact (h s).symm
    · simp only [Y, if_neg h]
      exact (Classical.choose_spec (pr_sec O C' C p hp hk Γ (fun s => x s i j) (hx i j))).2 s
  have hY0 : ∀ i j, (∀ s, x s i j = 0) → ∀ s, Y i j s = 0 := by
    intro i j h s
    simp only [Y, if_pos h]
  let M : Γ → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' := fun s => Matrix.of fun i j => Y i j s
  have hMp : ∀ s, (M s).map p = (x s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) := by
    intro s
    ext i j
    exact hYp i j s
  have hdet : ∀ s, IsUnit (M s).det := by
    intro s
    apply hloc
    have e : p (M s).det = ((M s).map p).det := RingHom.map_det (p : C' →+* C) (M s)
    rw [e, hMp]
    exact Matrix.isUnits_det_units (x s)
  refine ⟨fun s => Matrix.nonsingInvUnit (M s) (hdet s), fun i j => hYc i j, fun s => ?_, fun i j h s => hY0 i j h s⟩
  apply Units.ext
  exact hMp s

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_setlift :
