    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (B : Box F n C), Function.Surjective p → maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → B.Cont (maximalIdeal C) → Nonempty (SetLift p B) := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ _ p B hp hk hcont
  obtain ⟨r, hrc, hrm, -⟩ := gl_lift O C' C p hp hk F.Γ (fun s => (B.r s).left) (fun i j => hcont i j)
  have hg : ∀ v, ∃ a : GL (Fin n) C', GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) a = B.g v := by
    intro v
    obtain ⟨y, -, hy, -⟩ := gl_lift O C' C p hp hk Unit (fun _ => B.g v)
      (fun i j N => IsLocallyConstant.of_constant _ (fun _ _ => rfl))
    exact ⟨y (), hy ()⟩
  have ht : ∀ v, ∃ y : F.Gv v → GL (Fin n) C', (∀ i j, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C') fun s => y s i j) ∧
      (∀ s, GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (y s) = B.g v * (B.r (F.loc v s)).left * (B.g v)⁻¹) ∧
      ∀ s, UpT (y s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') := by
    intro v
    have hM : ∀ a b, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C) fun s : F.Gv v => (B.r (F.loc v s)).mat a b :=
      fun a b N => (hcont a b N).comp_continuous (continuous_subtype_val.comp (hF.loc_cont v))
    have hx : ∀ i j, AdicCont (maximalIdeal C) fun s : F.Gv v => ((B.g v * (B.r (F.loc v s)).left * (B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i j := by
      intro i j
      simp only [Units.val_mul, Matrix.mul_apply]
      exact ac_sum _ (f := fun q s => (∑ a, (B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i a * (B.r (F.loc v s)).mat a q) * ((B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) q j)
        fun q => ac_mul (ac_sum _ (f := fun a s => (B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i a * (B.r (F.loc v s)).mat a q)
          fun a => ac_mul (ac_const _ _) (hM a q)) (ac_const _ _)
    obtain ⟨y, hyc, hym, hy0⟩ := gl_lift O C' C p hp hk (F.Gv v) _ hx
    refine ⟨y, hyc, hym, fun s i j hij => hy0 i j (fun s' => B.tri v s' i j hij) s⟩
  choose g hgm using hg
  choose t htc htm htt using ht
  exact ⟨⟨r, hrc, hrm, g, hgm, t, htc, htt, htm⟩⟩


[APD_SRC_OMK] Full pr_omk, actual kernel-valued defects and local constancy
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/LcMatrix.lean#L7-L142
variable {T A C : Type} [TopologicalSpace T] [CommRing A] [CommRing C] {n : ℕ}

private theorem lc_matrix {B : Type} {f : T → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) B}
    (h : ∀ a b, IsLocallyConstant fun s => f s a b) : IsLocallyConstant f := by
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro x
  have hall : ∀ᶠ y in nhds x, ∀ a b, f y a b = f x a b :=
    Filter.eventually_all.2 fun a => Filter.eventually_all.2 fun b =>
      (IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq _).1 (h a b) x
  filter_upwards [hall] with y hy
  ext a b
  exact hy a b

private theorem lc_gl {I : Ideal A} {f : T → GL (Fin n) A} (h : ∀ a b, AdicCont I fun s => f s a b) (N : ℕ) :
    IsLocallyConstant fun s => GeneralLinearGroup.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N)) (f s) := by
  apply IsLocallyConstant.desc _ (fun u : GL (Fin n) (A ⧸ I ^ N) => (u : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (A ⧸ I ^ N)))
  · apply lc_matrix
    intro a b
    simpa [Function.comp_def] using h a b N
  · exact fun u v huv => Units.ext huv

private theorem mem_ker (p : A →+* C) {u : GL (Fin n) A} (h : GeneralLinearGroup.map p u = 1) (i j : Fin n) :
    ((u : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) A) - 1) i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := by
  rw [RingHom.mem_ker]
  have he := congrArg (fun v : GL (Fin n) C => (v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i j) h
  simp only [GeneralLinearGroup.map_apply, Units.val_one] at he
  rw [Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub, he, Matrix.one_apply, Matrix.one_apply]
  split_ifs <;> simp

private theorem lc_ker [IsNoetherianRing A] {I K : Ideal A} (hs : I * K = ⊥) {f : T → GL (Fin n) A}
    (hf : ∀ s a b, ((f s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) A) - 1) a b ∈ K)
    (h : ∀ N, IsLocallyConstant fun s => GeneralLinearGroup.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N)) (f s)) :
    IsLocallyConstant fun s => (f s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) A) - 1 := by
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Ideal.exists_pow_inf_eq_pow_smul I (K : Submodule A A)
  have h0 : ∀ x ∈ I ^ (k + 1), x ∈ K → x = 0 := by
    intro x hxm hxK
    have hx : x ∈ I ^ (k + 1) • (⊤ : Submodule A A) ⊓ (K : Submodule A A) := by
      refine ⟨?_, hxK⟩
      rw [Ideal.smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top]
      exact hxm
    rw [hk (k + 1) (by omega), Nat.add_sub_cancel_left, pow_one] at hx
    have hle : I • (I ^ k • (⊤ : Submodule A A) ⊓ (K : Submodule A A)) ≤ I • (K : Submodule A A) :=
      Submodule.smul_mono le_rfl inf_le_right
    have hx' := hle hx
    rw [Ideal.smul_eq_mul, hs] at hx'
    exact (Submodule.mem_bot A).1 hx'
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro s
  filter_upwards [(IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq _).1 (h (k + 1)) s] with s' hs'
  ext a b
  have he := congrArg
    (fun v : GL (Fin n) (A ⧸ I ^ (k + 1)) => (v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (A ⧸ I ^ (k + 1))) a b) hs'
  simp only [GeneralLinearGroup.map_apply] at he
  have hd := Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 he
  have hK : (f s' : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) A) a b - (f s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) A) a b ∈ K := by
    have := sub_mem (hf s' a b) (hf s a b)
    simpa [Matrix.sub_apply] using this
  rw [Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.sub_apply, sub_eq_zero.1 (h0 _ hd hK)]

private theorem map_sgn (f : A →+* C) (s : ℤˣ) (g : GL (Fin n) A) :
    GeneralLinearGroup.map f (sgnAct n A s g) = sgnAct n C s (GeneralLinearGroup.map f g) := by
  by_cases hs : s = 1
  · subst hs
    simp
  · have hA : sgnAct n A s = thetaAut n A := by
      simp [sgnAct, hs]
    have hC : sgnAct n C s = thetaAut n C := by
      simp [sgnAct, hs]
    rw [hA, hC]
    exact map_theta f g

private theorem lc_hom {G H : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] [Group H]
    (φ : G →* H) (h : IsOpen (φ.ker : Set G)) : IsLocallyConstant φ := by
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro x
  have h1 : ∀ᶠ z in nhds (1 : G), z ∈ (φ.ker : Set G) := h.mem_nhds (one_mem _)
  have hc : Filter.Tendsto (fun y => x⁻¹ * y) (nhds x) (nhds 1) := by
    have := (continuous_mul_left x⁻¹).tendsto x
    rwa [inv_mul_cancel] at this
  filter_upwards [hc.eventually h1] with y hy
  rw [SetLike.mem_coe, MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at hy
  exact hy.symm

end LC

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_omk :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (B : Box F n C) (D : SetLift p B), maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → (∀ x i j, D.om x i j ∈ RingHom.ker p) ∧ (∀ v s i j, D.a v s i j ∈ RingHom.ker p) ∧ IsLocallyConstant D.om ∧ ∀ v, IsLocallyConstant (D.a v) := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ _ p B D hker
  have hgrp : ∀ s t, (B.r (s * t)).left = (B.r s).left * sgnAct n C (F.del s) (B.r t).left := by
    intro s t
    rw [map_mul, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, B.nu]
  have hom1 : ∀ x : F.Γ × F.Γ, GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C)
      (D.r x.1 * sgnAct n C' (F.del x.1) (D.r x.2) * (D.r (x.1 * x.2))⁻¹) = 1 := by
    intro x
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, map_sgn, D.r_map, D.r_map, D.r_map, hgrp, mul_inv_cancel]
  have ha1 : ∀ v s, GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C)
      (D.r (F.loc v s) * ((D.g v)⁻¹ * D.t v s * D.g v)⁻¹) = 1 := by
    intro v s
    rw [map_mul, map_inv, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, D.r_map, D.g_map, D.t_map]
    group
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing C' := IsCO.toIsNoetherian O
  have hrc : ∀ N, IsLocallyConstant fun s : F.Γ =>
      GeneralLinearGroup.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C' ^ N)) (D.r s) :=
    fun N => lc_gl (fun a b => D.r_cont a b) N
  have hdel : IsLocallyConstant F.del := lc_hom F.del hF.del_open
  refine ⟨fun x i j => mem_ker _ (hom1 x) i j, fun v s i j => mem_ker _ (ha1 v s) i j, ?_, ?_⟩
  · have := lc_ker hker
      (f := fun x : F.Γ × F.Γ => D.r x.1 * sgnAct n C' (F.del x.1) (D.r x.2) * (D.r (x.1 * x.2))⁻¹)
      (fun x a b => mem_ker _ (hom1 x) a b) (by
        intro N
        have e1 := (hrc N).comp_continuous (continuous_fst (X := F.Γ) (Y := F.Γ))
        have e2 := (hrc N).comp_continuous (continuous_snd (X := F.Γ) (Y := F.Γ))
        have e3 := (hrc N).comp_continuous (continuous_mul (M := F.Γ))
        have e4 := hdel.comp_continuous (continuous_fst (X := F.Γ) (Y := F.Γ))
        have := IsLocallyConstant.comp₂
          (IsLocallyConstant.comp₂ e1 (IsLocallyConstant.comp₂ e4 e2
            fun s g => sgnAct n (C' ⧸ maximalIdeal C' ^ N) s g) (· * ·)) e3 (fun a b => a * b⁻¹)
        convert this using 1
        funext x
        simp [Function.comp, map_sgn])
    exact this
  · intro v
    have hloc : Continuous (F.loc v) := continuous_subtype_val.comp (hF.loc_cont v)
    have := lc_ker hker
      (f := fun s : F.Gv v => D.r (F.loc v s) * ((D.g v)⁻¹ * D.t v s * D.g v)⁻¹)
      (fun s a b => mem_ker _ (ha1 v s) a b) (by
        intro N
        have e1 := (hrc N).comp_continuous hloc
        have e2 := lc_gl (fun a b => D.t_cont v a b) N
        have := IsLocallyConstant.comp₂ e1 e2 (fun a b => a *
          ((GeneralLinearGroup.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C' ^ N)) (D.g v))⁻¹ * b *
            GeneralLinearGroup.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C' ^ N)) (D.g v))⁻¹)
        convert this using 1
        funext s
        simp [Function.comp])
    exact this


[APD_SRC_EVK] Entrywise kernel evaluation and signed adjoint identities
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/MapThetaLie.lean#L1-L145
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.PresDefs

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

variable {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C]
  {K : Ideal C} (u : K →ₗ[O] ResidueField O)

private theorem evk_add {x y : C} (hx : x ∈ K) (hy : y ∈ K) :
    evK K u (x + y) = evK K u x + evK K u y := by
  rw [evK_of_mem u hx, evK_of_mem u hy, evK_of_mem u (K.add_mem hx hy), ← map_add]
  rfl

private theorem evk_sub {x y : C} (hx : x ∈ K) (hy : y ∈ K) :
    evK K u (x - y) = evK K u x - evK K u y := by
  rw [evK_of_mem u hx, evK_of_mem u hy, evK_of_mem u (K.sub_mem hx hy), ← map_sub]
  rfl

private theorem evk_neg {x : C} (hx : x ∈ K) : evK K u (-x) = - evK K u x := by
  rw [evK_of_mem u hx, evK_of_mem u (K.neg_mem hx), ← map_neg]
  rfl

private theorem evk_sum {ι : Type} (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → C) (hf : ∀ i, f i ∈ K) :
    evK K u (∑ i ∈ s, f i) = ∑ i ∈ s, evK K u (f i) := by
  classical
  induction s using Finset.induction_on with
  | empty =>
    rw [Finset.sum_empty, Finset.sum_empty, evK_of_mem u K.zero_mem]
    exact map_zero u
  | insert a s ha ih =>
    rw [Finset.sum_insert ha, Finset.sum_insert ha, evk_add u (hf a) (K.sum_mem fun i _ => hf i), ih]

private theorem evk_mul [IsCO O C] (hK : maximalIdeal C * K = ⊥) (a : C) {x : C} (hx : x ∈ K) :
    evK K u (a * x) = res O C a * evK K u x := by
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) a
  have h0 : (a - algebraMap O C b) * x = 0 := by
    have := Ideal.mul_mem_mul hb hx
    rwa [hK, Ideal.mem_bot] at this
  have h1 : res O C a = algebraMap O _ b := by
    have := (res_eq_zero_iff O C _).2 hb
    rwa [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at this
  rw [evK_of_mem u hx, evK_of_mem u (K.mul_mem_left a hx), h1, ← Algebra.smul_def, ← map_smul]
  congr 1
  apply Subtype.ext
  show a * x = b • x
  rw [Algebra.smul_def, ← sub_eq_zero, ← sub_mul, h0]

variable {n : ℕ}

private theorem mem_left {Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C} (hZ : ∀ i j, Z i j ∈ K)
    (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) (i j : Fin n) : (A * Z) i j ∈ K := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  exact K.sum_mem fun k _ => K.mul_mem_left _ (hZ k j)

private theorem mem_right {Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C} (hZ : ∀ i j, Z i j ∈ K)
    (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) (i j : Fin n) : (Z * A) i j ∈ K := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  exact K.sum_mem fun k _ => K.mul_mem_right _ (hZ i k)

private theorem map_left [IsCO O C] (hK : maximalIdeal C * K = ⊥) {Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C}
    (hZ : ∀ i j, Z i j ∈ K) (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) :
    (A * Z).map (evK K u) = A.map (res O C) * Z.map (evK K u) := by
  ext i j
  rw [Matrix.map_apply, Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.mul_apply,
    evk_sum u _ _ fun k => K.mul_mem_left _ (hZ k j)]
  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => evk_mul u hK _ (hZ k j)

private theorem map_right [IsCO O C] (hK : maximalIdeal C * K = ⊥) {Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C}
    (hZ : ∀ i j, Z i j ∈ K) (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) :
    (Z * A).map (evK K u) = Z.map (evK K u) * A.map (res O C) := by
  ext i j
  rw [Matrix.map_apply, Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.mul_apply,
    evk_sum u _ _ fun k => K.mul_mem_right _ (hZ i k)]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
  rw [mul_comm, evk_mul u hK _ (hZ i k), mul_comm]
  rfl

private theorem mem_theta {Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C} (hZ : ∀ i j, Z i j ∈ K) (i j : Fin n) :
    thetaLie Z i j ∈ K :=
  K.neg_mem (mem_right (mem_left (Z := Zᵀ) (fun i j => hZ j i) _) _ i j)

private theorem map_thetaLie [IsCO O C] (hK : maximalIdeal C * K = ⊥) {Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C}
    (hZ : ∀ i j, Z i j ∈ K) : (thetaLie Z).map (evK K u) = thetaLie (Z.map (evK K u)) := by
  have hT : ∀ i j, Zᵀ i j ∈ K := fun i j => hZ j i
  have hJ : (Jmat C n).map (res O C) = Jmat (ResidueField O) n :=
    Jmat_map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O) n
  have e : (Jmat C n * Zᵀ * Jmat C n).map (evK K u) =
      Jmat (ResidueField O) n * (Z.map (evK K u))ᵀ * Jmat (ResidueField O) n := by
    rw [map_right u hK (mem_left hT _), map_left u hK hT, hJ]
    rfl
  unfold thetaLie
  rw [← e]
  ext i j
  exact evk_neg u (mem_right (mem_left hT _) _ i j)

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_evk :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (n : ℕ) (K : Ideal C) (u : K →ₗ[O] ResidueField O), maximalIdeal C * K = ⊥ → K ≤ maximalIdeal C → ∀ Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C, (∀ i j, Z i j ∈ K) → (∀ Z' : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C, (∀ i j, Z' i j ∈ K) → Z * Z' = 0 ∧ (Z + Z').map (evK K u) = Z.map (evK K u) + Z'.map (evK K u) ∧ (Z - Z').map (evK K u) = Z.map (evK K u) - Z'.map (evK K u)) ∧ (∀ A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C, (∀ i j, (A * Z) i j ∈ K ∧ (Z * A) i j ∈ K) ∧ (A * Z).map (evK K u) = A.map (res O C) * Z.map (evK K u) ∧ (Z * A).map (evK K u) = Z.map (evK K u) * A.map (res O C)) ∧ ∀ x : GG n C, (∀ i j, x.adFun Z i j ∈ K) ∧ (x.adFun Z).map (evK K u) = (GG.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O) x).adFun (Z.map (evK K u)) ∧ ∀ z : GL (Fin n) C, (z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) = 1 + Z → (x * GG.ofGL z * x⁻¹).mat = 1 + x.adFun Z := by
  intro O _ _ C _ _ _ n K u hK hKm Z hZ
  have hsq : ∀ Z' : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C, (∀ i j, Z' i j ∈ K) → Z * Z' = 0 := fun Z' hZ' => by
    ext i j
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
    refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun k _ => ?_
    have := Ideal.mul_mem_mul (hKm (hZ i k)) (hZ' k j)
    rwa [hK, Ideal.mem_bot] at this
  refine ⟨fun Z' hZ' => ⟨hsq Z' hZ', ?_, ?_⟩,
    fun A => ⟨fun i j => ⟨mem_left hZ A i j, mem_right hZ A i j⟩, map_left u hK hZ A, map_right u hK hZ A⟩,
    fun x => ?_⟩
  · ext i j
    exact evk_add u (hZ i j) (hZ' i j)
  · ext i j
    exact evk_sub u (hZ i j) (hZ' i j)
  have hY : ∀ i j, (if x.right = 1 then Z else thetaLie Z) i j ∈ K := fun i j => by
    split_ifs
    · exact hZ i j
    · exact mem_theta hZ i j
  refine ⟨mem_right (mem_left hY _) _, ?_, fun z hz => ?_⟩
  · have hY' : (if x.right = 1 then Z else thetaLie Z).map (evK K u) =
        if x.right = 1 then Z.map (evK K u) else thetaLie (Z.map (evK K u)) := by
      split_ifs
      · rfl
      · exact map_thetaLie u hK hZ
    unfold GG.adFun
    rw [map_right u hK (mem_left hY _), map_left u hK hY, hY']
    rfl
  · have hinv : ((z⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) = 1 - Z := by
      have : (z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * (1 - Z) = 1 := by
        rw [hz, add_mul, mul_sub, mul_sub, hsq Z hZ]
        simp
      exact Units.inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_right this
    have hl : (x * GG.ofGL z * x⁻¹).left = x.left * sgnAct n C x.right z * x.left⁻¹ := by
      rw [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_right,
        SemidirectProduct.inv_left, SemidirectProduct.left_inl, SemidirectProduct.right_inl, mul_one,
        ← MulAut.mul_apply, ← map_mul, mul_inv_cancel, map_one, MulAut.one_apply]
    have hs : ((sgnAct n C x.right z : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) =
        1 + (if x.right = 1 then Z else thetaLie Z) := by
      rcases Int.units_eq_one_or x.right with h | h
      · rw [h, sgnAct_one, if_pos rfl, hz]
      · rw [h, sgnAct_neg_one, if_neg (by decide), theta_val, hinv, thetaLie, Matrix.transpose_sub,
          Matrix.transpose_one, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.mul_one, Jmat_mul_Jmat,
          sub_eq_add_neg]
    unfold GG.adFun GG.mat
    rw [hl, Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, hs, mul_add, add_mul, mul_one, Units.mul_inv]


[APD_SRC_COCYCLE] Full original global two-cocycle equation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/BorelProjectionIsTwoCocycle.lean#L7-L76
private theorem grp_id {H : Type*} [Group H] (a b c ab bc abc : H) :
    (a * b * ab⁻¹) * (ab * c * abc⁻¹) = (a * (b * c * bc⁻¹) * a⁻¹) * (a * bc * abc⁻¹) := by
  group

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.borelProjection_isTwoCocycle :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (B : Box F n C) (D : SetLift p B) (u : RingHom.ker p →ₗ[O] ResidueField O), maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → ∀ s t r, (B.bar O).ad s ((D.co u).1 (t, r)) + (D.co u).1 (s, t * r) = (D.co u).1 (s * t, r) + (D.co u).1 (s, t) := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ _ p B D u hK s t r
  have hKm : RingHom.ker p ≤ maximalIdeal C' := by
    intro y hy
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O C', ← res_map p y, RingHom.mem_ker.mp hy, map_zero]
  obtain ⟨hom, -, -, -⟩ := pr_omk hF O C' C p B D hK
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ : ∃ x : F.Γ → GG n C', ∀ a, x a = ⟨D.r a, F.del a⟩ := ⟨_, fun _ => rfl⟩
  have hW : ∀ a b, x a * x b * (x (a * b))⁻¹ =
      GG.ofGL (D.r a * sgnAct n C' (F.del a) (D.r b) * (D.r (a * b))⁻¹) := by
    intro a b
    rw [hx, hx, hx]
    apply SemidirectProduct.ext
    · simp only [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_right, SemidirectProduct.inv_left,
        SemidirectProduct.inv_right, SemidirectProduct.left_inl, GG.ofGL]
      rw [← MulAut.mul_apply, ← map_mul, map_mul F.del a b, mul_inv_cancel, map_one, MulAut.one_apply]
    · simp only [SemidirectProduct.mul_right, SemidirectProduct.inv_right, SemidirectProduct.right_inl,
        GG.ofGL, map_mul, mul_inv_cancel, mul_one]
  have hz : ∀ a b, ((D.r a * sgnAct n C' (F.del a) (D.r b) * (D.r (a * b))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') :
      Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') = 1 + D.om (a, b) := by
    intro a b
    simp [SetLift.om]
  have hbar : (B.bar O).r s = GG.map (res O C' : C' →+* ResidueField O) (x s) := by
    rw [hx]
    apply SemidirectProduct.ext
    · ext i j
      simp [Box.map, GG.map, ← D.r_map s, res_map]
    · simp [Box.map, GG.map, B.nu]
  have key : ∀ a b : GG n C', a.right = 1 → (a * b).mat = a.mat * b.mat := by
    intro a b ha
    simp only [GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, ha, map_one, MulAut.one_apply, Units.val_mul]
  have hofr : ∀ w : GL (Fin n) C', (GG.ofGL w).right = 1 := fun w => rfl
  have hmat : ∀ w : GL (Fin n) C', (GG.ofGL w).mat = (w : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') := fun w => rfl
  have hW' : x s * x (t * r) * (x (s * t * r))⁻¹ =
      GG.ofGL (D.r s * sgnAct n C' (F.del s) (D.r (t * r)) * (D.r (s * (t * r)))⁻¹) := by
    rw [mul_assoc s t r]
    exact hW s (t * r)
  have h := grp_id (x s) (x t) (x r) (x (s * t)) (x (t * r)) (x (s * t * r))
  rw [hW s t, hW (s * t) r, hW t r, hW'] at h
  have hyr : (x s * GG.ofGL (D.r t * sgnAct n C' (F.del t) (D.r r) * (D.r (t * r))⁻¹) * (x s)⁻¹).right
      = 1 := by
    simp only [SemidirectProduct.mul_right, SemidirectProduct.inv_right, GG.ofGL,
      SemidirectProduct.right_inl, mul_one, mul_inv_cancel]
  have hM := congrArg GG.mat h
  rw [key _ _ (hofr _), key _ _ hyr, hmat, hmat, hmat] at hM
  obtain ⟨-, -, hE3⟩ := pr_evk O C' n (RingHom.ker p) u hK hKm (D.om (t, r)) (hom (t, r))
  obtain ⟨hAK, hAmap, hconj⟩ := hE3 (x s)
  rw [hconj _ (hz t r), hz s t, hz (s * t) r, hz s (t * r)] at hM
  obtain ⟨h1, -, -⟩ := pr_evk O C' n (RingHom.ker p) u hK hKm (D.om (s, t)) (hom (s, t))
  obtain ⟨h12, h12add, -⟩ := h1 (D.om (s * t, r)) (hom _)
  obtain ⟨hA, -, -⟩ := pr_evk O C' n (RingHom.ker p) u hK hKm ((x s).adFun (D.om (t, r))) hAK
  obtain ⟨hA4, hA4add, -⟩ := hA (D.om (s, t * r)) (hom _)
  have hsum : D.om (s, t) + D.om (s * t, r) = (x s).adFun (D.om (t, r)) + D.om (s, t * r) := by
    have e1 : (1 + D.om (s, t)) * (1 + D.om (s * t, r)) = 1 + (D.om (s, t) + D.om (s * t, r)) := by
      rw [add_mul, one_mul, mul_add, mul_one, h12]
      abel
    have e2 : (1 + (x s).adFun (D.om (t, r))) * (1 + D.om (s, t * r)) =
        1 + ((x s).adFun (D.om (t, r)) + D.om (s, t * r)) := by
      rw [add_mul, one_mul, mul_add, mul_one, hA4]
      abel
    rw [e1, e2] at hM
    exact add_left_cancel hM
  change ((B.bar O).r s).adFun ((D.om (t, r)).map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u)) +
      (D.om (s, t * r)).map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u) =
      (D.om (s * t, r)).map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u) + (D.om (s, t)).map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u)
  rw [hbar, ← hAmap, ← hA4add, add_comm ((D.om (s * t, r)).map _), ← h12add, hsum]


[APD_SRC_COCV] Full local cone two-cocycle equation and Borel projection
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrCocv.lean#L7-L106
private theorem upT_gl {C : Type} [CommRing C] {n : ℕ} (a b c : GL (Fin n) C)
    (ha : UpT (a : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)) (hb : UpT (b : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C))
    (hc : UpT (c : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)) :
    UpT (((a * b * c⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) - 1) := by
  have ha' : (a : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C).BlockTriangular id := fun i j h => ha i j h
  have hb' : (b : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C).BlockTriangular id := fun i j h => hb i j h
  have hc' : (c : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C).BlockTriangular id := fun i j h => hc i j h
  letI : Invertible (c : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) := c.invertible
  have hi := Matrix.blockTriangular_inv_of_blockTriangular hc'
  rw [← Matrix.coe_units_inv] at hi
  intro i j h
  have h1 := ((ha'.mul hb').mul hi) (show id j < id i from h)
  rw [Matrix.sub_apply, Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, h1, Matrix.one_apply_ne (ne_of_gt h), sub_zero]

private theorem ring_id {R : Type} [Ring R] (o c x a w : R) (h : (1 + o) * (1 + c) = (1 + x) * (1 + a) * (1 + w))
    (h1 : o * c = 0) (h2 : x * a = 0) (h3 : x * w = 0) (h4 : a * w = 0) : o - (x - c + a) = w := by
  have e1 : (1 + o) * (1 + c) = 1 + o + c + o * c := by noncomm_ring
  have e2 : (1 + x) * (1 + a) * (1 + w) = 1 + x + a + w + x * a + x * w + a * w + x * a * w := by noncomm_ring
  rw [e1, e2, h1, h2, h3, h4] at h
  simp only [add_zero, zero_mul] at h
  have : o = 1 + x + a + w - c - 1 := by rw [← h]; abel
  rw [this]; abel

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_cocv :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (B : Box F n C) (D : SetLift p B) (u : RingHom.ker p →ₗ[O] ResidueField O), maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → ∀ v s t, (D.co u).1 (F.loc v s, F.loc v t) - dOne ((B.bar O).ad.comp (F.loc v)) ((D.co u).2 v) (s, t) ∈ (B.bar O).b v := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ _ p B D u hK v s t
  obtain ⟨hom, ha, -, -⟩ := pr_omk hF O C' C p B D hK
  have hle : RingHom.ker p ≤ maximalIdeal C' := fun x hx => by
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, ← res_map p, (RingHom.mem_ker).1 hx, map_zero]
  have ev := pr_evk O C' n (RingHom.ker p) u hK hle
  have hdel : ∀ s, F.del (F.loc v s) = 1 := fun s => (F.loc0 v s).2
  have hright : ∀ s, (B.r (F.loc v s)).right = 1 := fun s => (B.nu _).trans (hdel s)
  have hres : ∀ x : GL (Fin n) C', GeneralLinearGroup.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O)
      (GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) x) = GeneralLinearGroup.map (res O C' : C' →+* ResidueField O) x := by
    intro x
    ext i j
    exact res_map p _
  have hval : ∀ x : GL (Fin n) C', (x : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') =
      ((GeneralLinearGroup.map (res O C' : C' →+* ResidueField O) x : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) :
        Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) := fun x => rfl

  set T : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' :=
    ((D.t v s * D.t v t * (D.t v (s * t))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') - 1 with hT
  have hTup : UpT T := upT_gl _ _ _ (D.t_tri v s) (D.t_tri v t) (D.t_tri v _)
  have hTK : ∀ i j, T i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := by
    have e : GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (D.t v s * D.t v t * (D.t v (s * t))⁻¹) = 1 := by
      rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, D.t_map, D.t_map, D.t_map, map_mul, map_mul, SemidirectProduct.mul_left,
        hright, sgnAct_one]
      group
    intro i j
    have e2 := congrArg (fun x : GL (Fin n) C => (x : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i j) e
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, hT, Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub]
    refine sub_eq_zero.2 (Eq.trans e2 ?_)
    by_cases h : i = j <;> simp [h, Matrix.one_apply]
  set gi : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' := (((D.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') with hgi
  set g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' := ((D.g v : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') with hg
  set ri : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' := (((D.r (F.loc v s))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') with hri
  set r : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' := ((D.r (F.loc v s) : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') with hr
  have hW1 : ∀ i j, (gi * T) i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := fun i j => (((ev T hTK).2.1 gi).1 i j).1
  have hW : ∀ i j, (gi * T * g) i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := fun i j => (((ev _ hW1).2.1 g).1 i j).2
  have hX1 : ∀ i j, (r * D.a v t) i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := fun i j => (((ev _ (ha v t)).2.1 r).1 i j).1
  have hX : ∀ i j, (r * D.a v t * ri) i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := fun i j => (((ev _ hX1).2.1 ri).1 i j).2
  have key : D.om (F.loc v s, F.loc v t) - (r * D.a v t * ri - D.a v (s * t) + D.a v s) = gi * T * g := by
    refine ring_id _ _ _ _ _ ?_ ((ev _ (hom _)).1 _ (ha v _)).1 ((ev _ hX).1 _ (ha v _)).1
      ((ev _ hX).1 _ hW).1 ((ev _ (ha v _)).1 _ hW).1
    have e1 : 1 + r * D.a v t * ri = ((D.r (F.loc v s) * (D.r (F.loc v t) * ((D.g v)⁻¹ * D.t v t * D.g v)⁻¹) *
        (D.r (F.loc v s))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') := by
      rw [SetLift.a, mul_sub, sub_mul, mul_one, hr, hri, Units.mul_inv, add_sub_cancel]
      rfl
    have e2 : 1 + gi * T * g = (((D.g v)⁻¹ * (D.t v s * D.t v t * (D.t v (s * t))⁻¹) * D.g v : GL (Fin n) C') :
        Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') := by
      rw [hT, mul_sub, sub_mul, mul_one, hg, hgi, Units.inv_mul, add_sub_cancel]
      rfl
    rw [e1, e2]
    simp only [SetLift.om, SetLift.a, add_sub_cancel, hdel, sgnAct_one, ← Units.val_mul, map_mul]
    congr 1
    group
  have hXc : ∀ i j, (r * D.a v t * ri - D.a v (s * t)) i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := fun i j =>
    sub_mem (hX i j) (ha v _ i j)
  have hXca : ∀ i j, (r * D.a v t * ri - D.a v (s * t) + D.a v s) i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := fun i j =>
    add_mem (hXc i j) (ha v _ i j)
  have k2 := congrArg (fun M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' => M.map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u)) key
  rw [((ev _ (hom _)).1 _ hXca).2.2, ((ev _ hXc).1 _ (ha v _)).2.1, ((ev _ hX).1 _ (ha v _)).2.2,
    ((ev _ hX1).2.1 ri).2.2, ((ev _ (ha v t)).2.1 r).2.1, ((ev _ hW1).2.1 g).2.2, ((ev _ hTK).2.1 gi).2.1,
    hr, hri, hg, hgi, hval, hval, hval, hval, map_inv, map_inv, ← hres, ← hres (D.g v), D.r_map, D.g_map] at k2
  have k3 : (D.co u).1 (F.loc v s, F.loc v t) - dOne ((B.bar O).ad.comp (F.loc v)) ((D.co u).2 v) (s, t) =
      ((((B.bar O).g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) *
        T.map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u) * ((B.bar O).g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) := by
    refine Eq.trans ?_ k2
    have e : ((B.bar O).ad.comp (F.loc v)) s ((D.co u).2 v t) =
        ((B.bar O).r (F.loc v s)).adFun ((D.a v t).map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u)) := rfl
    have e' : ((B.bar O).r (F.loc v s)).right = 1 := hright s
    simp only [dOne]
    rw [e, GG.adFun, if_pos e', add_sub_assoc, add_comm]
    rfl
  rw [k3]
  intro i j h
  rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv,
    Matrix.mul_one, Matrix.map_apply, hTup i j h, evK_of_mem u (zero_mem _)]
  exact map_zero u


[APD_SRC_SQZ] All original pr_sqz exponential and coefficient multiplication identities
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrSqz.lean#L5-L90
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_sqz :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (n : ℕ) (μ : ResidueField O →+ C), IsSq O μ → ∃ e : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O) → GL (Fin n) C, (∀ X, (e X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) = 1 + X.map μ) ∧ (∀ X Y, e (X + Y) = e X * e Y) ∧ (∀ X, (e X)⁻¹ = e (-X)) ∧ (∀ (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) (X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)), A * X.map μ = (A.map (res O C) * X).map μ ∧ X.map μ * A = (X * A.map (res O C)).map μ) ∧ ∀ (x : GG n C) (X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)), x * GG.ofGL (e X) = GG.ofGL (e ((GG.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O) x).adFun X)) * x := by
  intro O _ _ C _ _ _ n μ hμ
  have hsq : ∀ c d, μ c * μ d = 0 := by
    intro c d
    have h := hμ.mul (μ c) d
    rw [(res_eq_zero_iff O C _).2 (hμ.mem c), zero_mul, map_zero] at h
    exact h.symm
  have hL : ∀ (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) (X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)),
      A * X.map μ = (A.map (res O C) * X).map μ := by
    intro A X
    ext i j
    simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.map_apply, map_sum, hμ.mul]
  have hR : ∀ (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) (X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)),
      X.map μ * A = (X * A.map (res O C)).map μ := by
    intro A X
    ext i j
    simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.map_apply, map_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
    rw [mul_comm (X i k), hμ.mul, mul_comm]
  have hsqM : ∀ X Y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O), X.map μ * Y.map μ = 0 := by
    intro X Y
    ext i j
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply, hsq]
  have hadd : ∀ X Y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O), (X + Y).map μ = X.map μ + Y.map μ := by
    intro X Y
    ext i j
    simp
  have hzero : (0 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)).map μ = 0 := by
    ext i j
    simp
  have hu : ∀ X Y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O),
      (1 + X.map μ) * (1 + Y.map μ) = 1 + (X + Y).map μ := by
    intro X Y
    rw [hadd, show (1 + X.map μ) * (1 + Y.map μ) = 1 + (X.map μ + Y.map μ) + X.map μ * Y.map μ by
      noncomm_ring, hsqM, add_zero]
  let e : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O) → GL (Fin n) C := fun X =>
    ⟨1 + X.map μ, 1 + (-X).map μ, by rw [hu, add_neg_cancel, hzero, add_zero],
      by rw [hu, neg_add_cancel, hzero, add_zero]⟩
  have he : ∀ X, (e X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) = 1 + X.map μ := fun X => rfl
  have hemul : ∀ X Y, e (X + Y) = e X * e Y := by
    intro X Y
    apply Units.ext
    rw [Units.val_mul, he, he, he, hu]
  have heinv : ∀ X, (e X)⁻¹ = e (-X) := fun X => Units.ext rfl
  have hconj : ∀ (g : GL (Fin n) C) (h : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O))
      (Y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)),
      (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C).map (res O C) = (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) →
      g * e Y = e ((h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) * Y *
        (↑h⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O))) * g := by
    intro g h Y hh
    apply Units.ext
    rw [Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, he, he, Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_one,
      Matrix.one_mul, hL, hR, hh, Matrix.mul_assoc ((h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) * Y),
      Units.inv_mul, Matrix.mul_one]
  have hJ : (Jmat C n).map (res O C) = Jmat (ResidueField O) n :=
    Jmat_map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O) n
  have htheta : ∀ Y, theta (e Y) = e (thetaLie Y) := by
    intro Y
    apply Units.ext
    rw [theta_val, heinv, he, he, Matrix.transpose_add, Matrix.transpose_one, Matrix.mul_add,
      Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_one, Jmat_mul_Jmat, ← Matrix.transpose_map, hL, hJ, hR, hJ]
    rw [show thetaLie Y = Jmat (ResidueField O) n * (-Y)ᵀ * Jmat (ResidueField O) n by
      simp [thetaLie, Matrix.transpose_neg]]
  refine ⟨e, he, hemul, heinv, fun A X => ⟨hL A X, hR A X⟩, ?_⟩
  intro x X
  obtain ⟨g, s⟩ := x
  have hh : (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C).map (res O C) =
      ((GG.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O) (⟨g, s⟩ : GG n C)).left :
        Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) := rfl
  have had : GG.adFun (GG.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O) (⟨g, s⟩ : GG n C)) X =
      ((GG.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O) (⟨g, s⟩ : GG n C)).left :
        Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) *
        (if s = 1 then X else thetaLie X) *
        (↑(GG.map (res O C : C →+* ResidueField O) (⟨g, s⟩ : GG n C)).left⁻¹ :
          Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) := rfl
  rw [had]
  refine SemidirectProduct.ext ?_ ?_
  · simp only [GG.ofGL, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.left_inl,
      SemidirectProduct.right_inl, map_one, MulAut.one_apply]
    rcases Int.units_eq_one_or s with rfl | rfl
    · rw [sgnAct_one, if_pos rfl]
      exact hconj _ _ X hh
    · rw [sgnAct_neg_one, if_neg (by decide), htheta]
      exact hconj _ _ _ hh
  · simp only [GG.ofGL, SemidirectProduct.mul_right, SemidirectProduct.right_inl, mul_one, one_mul]


[APD_SRC_CORRECT] Full pr_correct with the exact signs of both matrix corrections
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrCorrect.lean#L6-L189
private theorem upT_mul {C : Type} [CommRing C] {n : ℕ} {M N : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C} (hM : UpT M) (hN : UpT N) :
    UpT (M * N) := fun i j h => by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun c _ => ?_
  by_cases hc : c < i
  · rw [hM i c hc, zero_mul]
  · rw [hN c j (lt_of_lt_of_le h (not_lt.1 hc)), mul_zero]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_correct :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (B : Box F n C) (D : SetLift p B) (μ : ResidueField O →+ C'), IsSq O μ → (∀ c, p (μ c) = 0) → ∀ (β : F.Γ → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) (Y : F.V → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) (a : ∀ v, F.Gv v → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)), IsLocallyConstant β → (∀ x, D.om x = (dOne (B.bar O).ad β x).map μ) → (∀ v s, D.a v s = (a v s).map μ) → (∀ v s, a v s - (β (F.loc v s) - ((B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) (Y v) - Y v)) ∈ (B.bar O).b v) → ∃ B' : Box F n C', B'.Cont (maximalIdeal C') ∧ B'.map (p : C' →+* C) = B ∧ ∀ s, (B'.r s).mat = (1 - (β s).map μ) * (D.r s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ _ p B D μ hμ hpμ β Y a hβ hom ha hb
  obtain ⟨e, he1, he2, he3, he4, he5⟩ := pr_sqz O C' n μ hμ
  have hres : ∀ z : C', res O C (p z) = res O C' z := fun z => res_map p z
  let x : F.Γ → GG n C' := fun s => SemidirectProduct.mk (D.r s) (F.del s)
  have hK1 : ∀ s, GG.map (res O C' : C' →+* ResidueField O) (x s) = (B.bar O).r s := by
    intro s
    apply SemidirectProduct.ext
    · ext i j
      simp [x, GG.map, Box.map, hres, ← D.r_map s]
    · simp [x, GG.map, Box.map, B.nu]
  have hdl : ∀ v (s : F.Gv v), F.del (F.loc v s) = 1 := fun v s => MonoidHom.mem_ker.1 (F.loc0 v s).2
  have hAd : ∀ v (s : F.Gv v) M, (B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) M =
      (D.r (F.loc v s) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') * M *
        (((D.r (F.loc v s))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') := by
    intro v s M
    show GG.adFun ((B.bar O).r (F.loc v s)) M = _
    rw [← hK1]
    clear hom ha hb he5 he4 he3 he2 he1 hβ hpμ hμ
    simp [GG.adFun, GG.mat, GG.map, x, hdl]
    congr 1
    apply Matrix.inv_eq_left_inv
    rw [← Matrix.coe_units_inv]
    calc (((D.r (F.loc v s))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') *
          (D.r (F.loc v s) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C')
        = ((((D.r (F.loc v s))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') *
            (D.r (F.loc v s) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C')).map (res O C') :=
          (Matrix.map_mul (f := (res O C').toRingHom)).symm
      _ = 1 := by
          rw [Units.inv_mul]
          exact Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero _) (map_one _)
  have hxmul : ∀ s t, x s * x t = GG.ofGL (e (dOne (B.bar O).ad β (s, t))) * x (s * t) := by
    intro s t
    have hu : D.r s * sgnAct n C' (F.del s) (D.r t) * (D.r (s * t))⁻¹ = e (dOne (B.bar O).ad β (s, t)) := by
      apply Units.ext
      rw [he1, ← hom (s, t)]
      simp [SetLift.om]
    apply SemidirectProduct.ext
    · rw [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, ← hu]
      simp [x]
    · simp [x]
  have hcomm : ∀ s X, x s * GG.ofGL (e X) = GG.ofGL (e ((B.bar O).ad s X)) * x s := by
    intro s X
    rw [he5 (x s) X, hK1 s]
    rfl
  let R' : F.Γ → GG n C' := fun s => GG.ofGL (e (-β s)) * x s
  have hmul : ∀ s t, R' (s * t) = R' s * R' t := by
    intro s t
    symm
    calc R' s * R' t = GG.ofGL (e (-β s)) * (x s * GG.ofGL (e (-β t))) * x t := by
          simp only [R', mul_assoc]
      _ = GG.ofGL (e (-β s)) * GG.ofGL (e ((B.bar O).ad s (-β t))) * (x s * x t) := by
          rw [hcomm]
          simp only [mul_assoc]
      _ = GG.ofGL (e (-β s)) * GG.ofGL (e ((B.bar O).ad s (-β t))) *
            GG.ofGL (e (dOne (B.bar O).ad β (s, t))) * x (s * t) := by
          rw [hxmul]
          simp only [mul_assoc]
      _ = GG.ofGL (e (-β s + (B.bar O).ad s (-β t) + dOne (B.bar O).ad β (s, t))) * x (s * t) := by
          rw [he2, he2, map_mul, map_mul]
      _ = R' (s * t) := by
          simp only [R']
          congr 3
          simp only [dOne, map_neg]
          abel
  let rr : F.Γ →* GG n C' := MonoidHom.mk' R' hmul
  have hneg : ∀ Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O), (-Z).map μ = -(Z.map μ) := by
    intro Z
    ext i j
    simp
  have hpe : ∀ X, GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (e X) = 1 := by
    intro X
    ext i j
    simp only [GeneralLinearGroup.map_apply, he1, Matrix.add_apply, Matrix.map_apply, Units.val_one, map_add]
    have h0 : (p : C' →+* C) (μ (X i j)) = 0 := hpμ _
    rw [h0, add_zero, Matrix.one_apply, Matrix.one_apply]
    split_ifs <;> simp
  have hconj : ∀ (A : GL (Fin n) C') X, A * e X * A⁻¹ =
      e ((A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') * X * ((A⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C')) := by
    intro A X
    apply Units.ext
    rw [Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, he1, he1, mul_add, mul_one, add_mul, Units.mul_inv, (he4 _ X).1, (he4 _ _).2]
  have hGbar : ∀ v, ((B.bar O).g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) =
        (D.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') ∧
      (((B.bar O).g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) =
        (((D.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') := by
    intro v
    constructor
    · ext i j
      simp [Box.map, ← D.g_map v, hres]
    · ext i j
      simp [Box.map, ← D.g_map v, hres, ← map_inv]
  refine ⟨{ r := rr, nu := ?_, g := fun v => D.g v * e (-(Y v)), tri := ?_ }, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro s
    show (GG.ofGL (e (-β s)) * x s).right = F.del s
    simp [x]
  · intro v s
    show UpT ((↑(D.g v * e (-(Y v))) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') * (R' (F.loc v s)).mat *
      (((D.g v * e (-(Y v)))⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C'))
    have hr : D.r (F.loc v s) = e (a v s) * ((D.g v)⁻¹ * D.t v s * D.g v) := by
      rw [← mul_inv_eq_iff_eq_mul]
      apply Units.ext
      rw [he1, ← ha v s]
      simp [SetLift.a]
    have hmat : (R' (F.loc v s)).mat = ↑(e (-β (F.loc v s)) * D.r (F.loc v s)) := by
      simp [R', GG.mat, x]
    have h1 : D.r (F.loc v s) * e (Y v) * (D.r (F.loc v s))⁻¹ = e ((B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) (Y v)) := by
      rw [hconj, hAd]
    have hX : e (a v s - (β (F.loc v s) - ((B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) (Y v) - Y v))) =
        e (-(Y v)) * e (-β (F.loc v s)) * e ((B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) (Y v)) * e (a v s) := by
      rw [← he2, ← he2, ← he2]
      congr 1
      abel
    have key : D.g v * e (-(Y v)) * (e (-β (F.loc v s)) * D.r (F.loc v s)) * (D.g v * e (-(Y v)))⁻¹ =
        e ((D.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') *
          (a v s - (β (F.loc v s) - ((B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) (Y v) - Y v))) *
          (((D.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C')) * D.t v s := by
      rw [← hconj, hX]
      calc D.g v * e (-(Y v)) * (e (-β (F.loc v s)) * D.r (F.loc v s)) * (D.g v * e (-(Y v)))⁻¹
          = D.g v * e (-(Y v)) * e (-β (F.loc v s)) *
              (D.r (F.loc v s) * e (Y v) * (D.r (F.loc v s))⁻¹) * D.r (F.loc v s) * (D.g v)⁻¹ := by
            rw [_root_.mul_inv_rev, he3, neg_neg]
            group
        _ = D.g v * e (-(Y v)) * e (-β (F.loc v s)) * e ((B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) (Y v)) *
              (e (a v s) * ((D.g v)⁻¹ * D.t v s * D.g v)) * (D.g v)⁻¹ := by
            rw [h1, ← hr]
        _ = D.g v * (e (-(Y v)) * e (-β (F.loc v s)) * e ((B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) (Y v)) * e (a v s)) *
              (D.g v)⁻¹ * D.t v s := by
            group
    have hW : UpT ((D.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C') *
          (a v s - (β (F.loc v s) - ((B.bar O).ad (F.loc v s) (Y v) - Y v))) *
          (((D.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (res O C')) := by
      rw [← (hGbar v).1, ← (hGbar v).2]
      exact hb v s
    rw [hmat, ← Units.val_mul, ← Units.val_mul, key, Units.val_mul, he1]
    apply upT_mul _ (D.t_tri v s)
    intro i j hij
    rw [Matrix.add_apply, Matrix.one_apply_ne (ne_of_gt hij), Matrix.map_apply, hW i j hij, map_zero, add_zero]
  · intro i j N
    have hB : IsLocallyConstant fun s =>
        (D.r s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C' ^ N)) := by
      rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
      intro s0
      have hall : ∀ᶠ s in nhds s0, ∀ a b,
          Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C' ^ N) ((D.r s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') a b) =
            Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C' ^ N) ((D.r s0 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') a b) :=
        Filter.eventually_all.2 fun a => Filter.eventually_all.2 fun b =>
          (IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq _).1 (D.r_cont a b N) s0
      filter_upwards [hall] with s hs
      ext a b
      exact hs a b
    have hA := hβ.comp (fun Z =>
      ((1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') + (-Z).map μ).map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal C' ^ N)))
    have hM := (IsLocallyConstant.comp₂ hA hB (· * ·)).comp (fun M => M i j)
    convert hM using 1
    funext s
    simp only [Function.comp_apply]
    rw [← Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.map_apply]
    congr 1
    show (R' s).mat i j = _
    simp [R', GG.mat, x, he1]
  · apply Box.ext
    · apply MonoidHom.ext
      intro s
      apply SemidirectProduct.ext
      · show GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (R' s).left = (B.r s).left
        simp [R', x, hpe, D.r_map]
      · show (R' s).right = (B.r s).right
        simp [R', x, B.nu]
    · funext v
      show GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : C' →+* C) (D.g v * e (-(Y v))) = B.g v
      rw [map_mul, hpe, mul_one, D.g_map]
  · intro s
    show (R' s).mat = _
    simp [R', GG.mat, x, he1, hneg, sub_eq_add_neg]


[APD_SRC_NORM1] Full first normalization over an arbitrary target O-algebra
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrNorm1.lean#L5-L75
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_norm1 :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (n : ℕ) (x : GG n C'), maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → RingHom.ker p ≤ maximalIdeal C' → (2 : ResidueField O) ≠ 0 → x * x = 1 → x.right = -1 → x.mat.map p = 1 → ∃ h : GL (Fin n) C', (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map p = 1 ∧ (GG.ofGL h * x * (GG.ofGL h)⁻¹).mat = 1 := by
  intro O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ p n x hmI hle h2R hxx hr hmap
  have hgt : x.left * theta x.left = 1 := by
    have hl := congrArg SemidirectProduct.left hxx
    simpa [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hr] using hl
  have hθ : ∀ u : GL (Fin n) C', ((theta u⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') =
      Jmat C' n * (u : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C')ᵀ * Jmat C' n := by
    intro u
    rw [show theta u⁻¹ = (theta u)⁻¹ from map_inv (thetaAut n C') u, theta_inv_val]
  have hgg : (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') =
      Jmat C' n * (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C')ᵀ * Jmat C' n := by
    have h1 : (theta x.left)⁻¹ = x.left := inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_left hgt
    rw [← theta_inv_val x.left, h1]
  have hsq : ∀ a b : C', a ∈ RingHom.ker p → b ∈ RingHom.ker p → a * b = 0 := by
    intro a b ha hb
    have hab : a * b ∈ maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p := Ideal.mul_mem_mul (hle ha) hb
    rw [hmI] at hab
    exact (Ideal.mem_bot).mp hab
  have hp1 : ∀ i j : Fin n,
      p ((1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') i j) = (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i j := by
    intro i j
    rw [Matrix.one_apply, Matrix.one_apply]
    split_ifs <;> simp
  set X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' := (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') - 1 with hXdef
  have hX : ∀ i j, X i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := by
    intro i j
    have hgij : p ((x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') i j) = (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i j :=
      congrFun (congrFun hmap i) j
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub, hp1, hgij, sub_self]
  have hXX : X * X = 0 := by
    ext i j
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.zero_apply]
    exact Finset.sum_eq_zero fun k _ => hsq _ _ (hX i k) (hX k j)
  have hXJ : Jmat C' n * Xᵀ * Jmat C' n = X := by
    rw [hXdef, Matrix.transpose_sub, Matrix.transpose_one, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul,
      Matrix.mul_one, Jmat_mul_Jmat, ← hgg]
  have hO : IsUnit (2 : O) := by
    rw [← IsLocalRing.notMem_maximalIdeal, ← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, map_ofNat]
    exact h2R
  have h2 : IsUnit (2 : C') := by
    have h2' := hO.map (algebraMap O C')
    rwa [map_ofNat] at h2'
  have hc2 : (↑h2.unit⁻¹ : C') * 2 = 1 := h2.val_inv_mul
  set Y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C' := (↑h2.unit⁻¹ : C') • X with hYdef
  have hYX : Y * X = 0 := by rw [hYdef, Matrix.smul_mul, hXX, smul_zero]
  have hXY : X * Y = 0 := by rw [hYdef, Matrix.mul_smul, hXX, smul_zero]
  have hYY : Y * Y = 0 := by rw [hYdef, Matrix.mul_smul, hYX, smul_zero]
  have hY2 : Y + Y = X := by rw [hYdef, ← add_smul, ← mul_two, hc2, one_smul]
  have e1 : (1 - Y) * (1 + Y) = 1 := by
    rw [sub_mul, one_mul, mul_add, mul_one, hYY, add_zero, add_sub_cancel_right]
  have e2 : (1 + Y) * (1 - Y) = 1 := by
    rw [add_mul, one_mul, mul_sub, mul_one, hYY, sub_zero, sub_add_cancel]
  have hpY : ∀ i j, p (Y i j) = 0 := by
    intro i j
    rw [hYdef, Matrix.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, map_mul, (RingHom.mem_ker).mp (hX i j), mul_zero]
  obtain ⟨U, hU⟩ : ∃ U : GL (Fin n) C', (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') = 1 - Y :=
    ⟨⟨1 - Y, 1 + Y, e1, e2⟩, rfl⟩
  have hJ : Jmat C' n * (1 - Y)ᵀ * Jmat C' n = 1 - Y := by
    rw [Matrix.transpose_sub, Matrix.transpose_one, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.mul_one,
      Jmat_mul_Jmat, hYdef, Matrix.transpose_smul, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.smul_mul, hXJ]
  have hg1 : (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') = 1 + X := by rw [hXdef, add_sub_cancel]
  have key : (1 - Y) * (1 + X) * (1 - Y) =
      1 + X - (Y + Y) - Y * X - X * Y + Y * Y + Y * X * Y := by noncomm_ring
  refine ⟨U, ?_, ?_⟩
  · ext i j
    show p ((U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') i j) = (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i j
    rw [hU, Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub, hp1, hpY, sub_zero]
  · simp [GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hr]
    rw [hθ, hU, hJ, hg1, key, hYX, hXY, hYY, hY2, zero_mul]
    simp


[APD_SRC_NORM2] Full second normalization and anti-diagonal symmetry
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/JconjApply.lean#L5-L113
private theorem jconj_apply {R : Type} [CommRing R] {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) R) (i j : Fin n) :
    (Jmat R n * Mᵀ * Jmat R n) i j = M j.rev i.rev := by
  have h1 : ∀ k, (Jmat R n * Mᵀ) i k = M k i.rev := by
    intro k
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single i.rev]
    · simp [Jmat]
    · intro b _ hb
      simp [Jmat, hb]
    · simp
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single j.rev]
  · rw [h1]
    simp [Jmat]
  · intro b _ hb
    have : j ≠ b.rev := by
      intro h
      apply hb
      rw [h, Fin.rev_rev]
    simp [Jmat, this]
  · simp

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_norm2 :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (n : ℕ) (Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C'), maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → RingHom.ker p ≤ maximalIdeal C' → thetaLie Z = Z → (∀ i j, i ≠ j → p (Z i j) = 0) → (∀ i j, i ≠ j → res O C' (Z i i) ≠ res O C' (Z j j)) → ∃ h : GL (Fin n) C', (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map p = 1 ∧ theta h = h ∧ ∀ i j, i ≠ j → ((h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') * Z * ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C') : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C')) i j = 0 := by
  intro O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ p n Z hmI hIm hZ hoff hdiag
  classical
  have hII : ∀ a b : C', a ∈ RingHom.ker p → b ∈ RingHom.ker p → a * b = 0 := by
    intro a b ha hb
    have hab : a * b ∈ maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p := Ideal.mul_mem_mul (hIm ha) hb
    rw [hmI] at hab
    simpa using hab
  have hZI : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → Z i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := by
    intro i j hij
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker]
    exact hoff i j hij
  have hunit : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → IsUnit (Z i i - Z j j) := by
    intro i j hij
    by_contra hu
    have hm : Z i i - Z j j ∈ maximalIdeal C' := (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).2 hu
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O C', map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at hm
    exact hdiag i j hij hm
  have hZr : ∀ i j, Z j.rev i.rev = -Z i j := by
    intro i j
    have hij := congrFun (congrFun hZ i) j
    simp only [thetaLie, Matrix.neg_apply, jconj_apply] at hij
    rw [← hij, neg_neg]
  obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ : ∃ A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C', ∀ i j,
      A i j = if i = j then 0 else Z i j * Ring.inverse (Z i i - Z j j) :=
    ⟨Matrix.of fun i j => if i = j then 0 else Z i j * Ring.inverse (Z i i - Z j j), fun i j => rfl⟩
  have hAI : ∀ i j, A i j ∈ RingHom.ker p := by
    intro i j
    rw [hA]
    split_ifs with h
    · exact Ideal.zero_mem _
    · exact Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (hZI i j h)
  have hAA : A * A = 0 := by
    ext i j
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.zero_apply]
    exact Finset.sum_eq_zero fun k _ => hII _ _ (hAI i k) (hAI k j)
  have hAr : ∀ i j, A j.rev i.rev = -A i j := by
    intro i j
    rw [hA, hA]
    by_cases hij : i = j
    · subst hij
      simp
    · have hrev : j.rev ≠ i.rev := fun h => hij (Fin.rev_injective h).symm
      rw [if_neg hrev, if_neg hij, hZr i j, hZr j j, hZr i i]
      have hd : -Z j j - -Z i i = Z i i - Z j j := by ring
      rw [hd]
      ring
  have h1 : (1 + A) * (1 - A) = 1 := by
    rw [show (1 + A) * (1 - A) = 1 - A * A by noncomm_ring, hAA, sub_zero]
  have h2 : (1 - A) * (1 + A) = 1 := by
    rw [show (1 - A) * (1 + A) = 1 - A * A by noncomm_ring, hAA, sub_zero]
  refine ⟨⟨1 + A, 1 - A, h1, h2⟩, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · ext i j
    have h0 : p (A i j) = 0 := by
      have := hAI i j
      rwa [RingHom.mem_ker] at this
    simp only [Units.val_mk, Matrix.map_apply, Matrix.add_apply, map_add, h0, add_zero,
      Matrix.one_apply]
    split_ifs <;> simp
  · apply Units.ext
    rw [theta_val]
    show Jmat C' n * (1 - A)ᵀ * Jmat C' n = 1 + A
    rw [Matrix.transpose_sub, Matrix.transpose_one, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.mul_one,
      Jmat_mul_Jmat, sub_eq_add_neg]
    congr 1
    ext i j
    rw [Matrix.neg_apply, jconj_apply, hAr, neg_neg]
  · intro i j hij
    show ((1 + A) * Z * (1 - A)) i j = 0
    rw [show (1 + A) * Z * (1 - A) = Z + A * Z - Z * A - A * Z * A by noncomm_ring]
    have hAZ : (A * Z) i j = A i j * Z j j := by
      rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single j]
      · intro k _ hk
        exact hII _ _ (hAI i k) (hZI k j hk)
      · simp
    have hZA : (Z * A) i j = Z i i * A i j := by
      rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single i]
      · intro k _ hk
        exact hII _ _ (hZI i k (Ne.symm hk)) (hAI k j)
      · simp
    have hAZA : (A * Z * A) i j = 0 := by
      rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
      refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun k _ => hII _ _ ?_ (hAI k j)
      rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
      exact Ideal.sum_mem _ fun l _ => Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (hAI i l)
    rw [Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.add_apply, hAZ, hZA, hAZA, hA, if_neg hij]
    have hu := Ring.inverse_mul_cancel _ (hunit i j hij)
    linear_combination (-(Z i j)) * hu


[APD_SRC_NORM] Full pr_norm; no surjectivity premise on p
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrNorm.lean#L7-L182

namespace UpperTheory.V.Fr

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ} {C C' : Type} [CommRing C] [CommRing C']

private theorem conj_left {G : Type} [Group G] (h : GL (Fin n) C) (r : G →* GG n C) (s : G) :
    (conjBy h r s).left = h * (r s).left * sgnAct n C (r s).right h⁻¹ := by
  simp [conjBy, GG.ofGL, MulAut.conj_apply, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_right, mul_assoc]

private theorem conj_right {G : Type} [Group G] (h : GL (Fin n) C) (r : G →* GG n C) (s : G) :
    (conjBy h r s).right = (r s).right := by
  simp [conjBy, GG.ofGL, MulAut.conj_apply, SemidirectProduct.mul_right]

private theorem conj_mat {G : Type} [Group G] (h : GL (Fin n) C) (r : G →* GG n C) (s : G) (hs : (r s).right = 1) :
    (conjBy h r s).mat = (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * (r s).mat *
      ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) := by
  unfold GG.mat
  rw [conj_left, hs, sgnAct_one, Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul]

private theorem conj_mul {G : Type} [Group G] (h h' : GL (Fin n) C) (r : G →* GG n C) :
    conjBy (h * h') r = conjBy h (conjBy h' r) := by
  apply MonoidHom.ext
  intro s
  simp [conjBy, MulAut.conj_apply, _root_.mul_inv_rev, mul_assoc]

private theorem frame_right_eq_one_of_G0 (B : Box F n C) (s : F.G0) : (B.r s).right = 1 := by
  exact (B.nu s).trans (MonoidHom.mem_ker.mp s.2)

private noncomputable def cj (h : GL (Fin n) C) (B : Box F n C) : Box F n C where
  r := conjBy h B.r
  nu s := (conj_right h B.r s).trans (B.nu s)
  g v := B.g v * h⁻¹
  tri v s := by
    have e : B.g v * h⁻¹ * (conjBy h B.r (F.loc v s)).left * (B.g v * h⁻¹)⁻¹ =
        B.g v * (B.r (F.loc v s)).left * (B.g v)⁻¹ := by
      have : (B.r (F.loc v s)).right = 1 := frame_right_eq_one_of_G0 B (F.loc0 v s)
      rw [conj_left, this, sgnAct_one]
      group
    have e' := congrArg Units.val e
    simp only [Units.val_mul] at e'
    have := B.tri v s
    unfold GG.mat at this ⊢
    rw [← e'] at this
    simpa only [Units.val_mul] using this

private theorem cj_map (f : C →+* C') (h : GL (Fin n) C) (B : Box F n C)
    (hh : (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C).map f = 1) : (cj h B).map f = B.map f := by
  have hh' : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f h = 1 := Units.ext (by simpa using hh)
  have hg : GG.map f (GG.ofGL h) = 1 := by
    simp [GG.map, GG.ofGL, hh']
  apply Box.ext
  · apply MonoidHom.ext
    intro s
    show GG.map f (conjBy h B.r s) = GG.map f (B.r s)
    simp [conjBy, MulAut.conj_apply, map_mul, map_inv, hg]
  · funext v
    show Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f (B.g v * h⁻¹) = Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f (B.g v)
    simp [map_mul, map_inv, hh']

private theorem cj_cont (hd : IsOpen (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)) (I : Ideal C) (h : GL (Fin n) C) (B : Box F n C)
    (hB : B.Cont I) : (cj h B).Cont I := by
  intro i j N
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro x
  have hdel : ∀ᶠ y in nhds x, F.del y = F.del x := by
    have hopen : IsOpen ((fun y => x⁻¹ * y) ⁻¹' (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)) :=
      hd.preimage (continuous_const.mul continuous_id)
    filter_upwards [hopen.mem_nhds (by simp)] with y hy
    have e : F.del (x⁻¹ * y) = 1 := hy
    rw [map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at e
    exact e.symm
  have hM : ∀ᶠ y in nhds x, ∀ a b : Fin n,
      Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) ((B.r y).mat a b) = Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) ((B.r x).mat a b) := by
    rw [Filter.eventually_all]
    intro a
    rw [Filter.eventually_all]
    intro b
    exact (hB a b N).eventually_eq x
  filter_upwards [hdel, hM] with y hy hMy
  show Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) ((conjBy h B.r y).mat i j) = Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) ((conjBy h B.r x).mat i j)
  simp only [GG.mat] at hMy ⊢
  simp only [conj_left, B.nu, hy, Units.val_mul, Matrix.mul_apply, map_sum, map_mul, hMy]

private theorem of_unique {B : Box F n C} {ρ ρ' : NRep F n C} (h : B.Of ρ) (h' : B.Of ρ') : ρ = ρ' := by
  have e : ρ.r = ρ'.r := MonoidHom.ext fun s => (h.res s).symm.trans (h'.res s)
  apply NRep.ext e
  funext v i
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun s => Units.ext ?_
  rw [← h.psi, ← h'.psi, e]

private theorem star_mat (hc : F.del F.c = -1) (B : Box F n C) (h1 : (B.r F.c).mat = 1) (g : F.G0) :
    (B.r (F.star g)).mat = Jmat C n * (B.r g).matᵀ * Jmat C n := by
  have t1 : theta (1 : GL (Fin n) C) = 1 := by
    simpa [sgnAct_neg_one] using map_one (sgnAct n C (-1))
  have hX : B.r F.c = ⟨1, -1⟩ := SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext (by simpa [GG.mat] using h1)) ((B.nu _).trans hc)
  have hY : B.r (g : F.Γ) = ⟨(B.r g).left, 1⟩ := SemidirectProduct.ext rfl (frame_right_eq_one_of_G0 B g)
  rw [show ((F.star g : F.G0) : F.Γ) = F.c * (g : F.Γ)⁻¹ * F.c⁻¹ from rfl, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, map_inv, hX, hY]
  simp [GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.inv_left, map_inv, sgnAct_neg_one, sgnAct_one, theta_inv_val]
  rw [t1, t1, Units.val_one, mul_one, ← Matrix.coe_units_inv, theta_inv_val]

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

end UpperTheory.V.Fr

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_norm :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (p : C' →ₐ[O] C) (ρ : NRep F n C) (B' : Box F n C'), maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → (ρ.bar O).Good → (B'.map (p : C' →+* C)).Of ρ → B'.Cont (maximalIdeal C') → ∃ (h : GL (Fin n) C') (ρ' : NRep F n C'), (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C').map p = 1 ∧ ρ'.map (p : C' →+* C) = ρ ∧ ρ'.Cont (maximalIdeal C') ∧ (conjBy h B'.r F.c).mat = 1 ∧ ∀ s : F.G0, (conjBy h B'.r s).mat = ρ'.r s := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ C' _ _ _ C _ _ _ p ρ B' hmk hgood hof hcont
  have hkp : RingHom.ker p ≤ maximalIdeal C' := by
    intro x hx
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker] at hx
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O C', ← res_map p x, hx, map_zero]
  have hmat : ∀ (B : Box F n C') (s : F.Γ), ((B.map (p : C' →+* C)).r s).mat = (B.r s).mat.map p := fun B s => rfl
  have hx1 : (B'.r F.c).mat.map p = 1 := by
    rw [← hmat]
    exact hof.atc
  obtain ⟨h1, hh1, hc1⟩ := pr_norm1 O C' C p n (B'.r F.c) hmk hkp hgood.two
    (by rw [← map_mul, hF.c_sq, map_one]) ((B'.nu _).trans hF.del_c) hx1
  set B1 := cj h1 B' with hB1
  have hB1c : (B1.r F.c).mat = 1 := hc1
  have hB1map : B1.map (p : C' →+* C) = B'.map (p : C' →+* C) := cj_map _ h1 B' hh1
  have hB1cont : B1.Cont (maximalIdeal C') := cj_cont hF.del_open _ h1 B' hcont
  have hstar : ∀ g : F.G0, (B1.r (F.star g)).mat = Jmat C' n * (B1.r g).matᵀ * Jmat C' n :=
    star_mat hF.del_c B1 hB1c
  set Z := (B1.r F.g0).mat - (B1.r (F.star F.g0)).mat with hZ
  have hJ : ∀ X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C', Jmat C' n * (Jmat C' n * X) = X := by
    intro X
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.one_mul]
  have hθ : thetaLie Z = Z := by
    rw [hZ, hstar]
    simp only [thetaLie, Matrix.transpose_sub, Matrix.transpose_mul, Jmat_transpose, Matrix.transpose_transpose,
      Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, hJ, Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.mul_one]
    abel
  have hZp : Z.map p = ρ.r F.g0 - ρ.r (F.star F.g0) := by
    rw [hZ, Matrix.map_sub _ (fun a b => map_sub p a b), ← hmat, ← hmat, hB1map, hof.res, hof.res]
  have hoff : ∀ i j : Fin n, i ≠ j → p (Z i j) = 0 := by
    intro i j hij
    have e := congrFun (congrFun hZp i) j
    rw [Matrix.map_apply] at e
    rw [e]
    exact ρ.diag i j hij
  have hdist : ∀ i j : Fin n, i ≠ j → res O C' (Z i i) ≠ res O C' (Z j j) := by
    intro i j hij
    have e : ∀ a, p (Z a a) = (ρ.r F.g0 - ρ.r (F.star F.g0)) a a := fun a => by
      rw [← hZp, Matrix.map_apply]
    rw [← res_map p, ← res_map p, e i, e j]
    convert hgood.zd i j hij using 1 <;> simp [NRep.bar, NRep.map]
  obtain ⟨h2', hh2, hθ2, hdiag2⟩ := pr_norm2 O C' C p n Z hmk hkp hθ hoff hdist
  set B2 := cj h2' B1 with hB2
  have hB2c : (B2.r F.c).mat = 1 := by
    show (conjBy h2' B1.r F.c).mat = 1
    have hl : (B1.r F.c).left = 1 := Units.ext hB1c
    unfold GG.mat
    rw [conj_left, B1.nu, hF.del_c, hl, mul_one, map_inv, sgnAct_neg_one, hθ2, mul_inv_cancel, Units.val_one]
  have hB2diag : ∀ i j : Fin n, i ≠ j → ((B2.r F.g0).mat - (B2.r (F.star F.g0)).mat) i j = 0 := by
    intro i j hij
    have e1 := conj_mat h2' B1.r (F.g0 : F.Γ) (frame_right_eq_one_of_G0 B1 F.g0)
    have e2 := conj_mat h2' B1.r ((F.star F.g0 : F.G0) : F.Γ) (frame_right_eq_one_of_G0 B1 (F.star F.g0))
    show ((conjBy h2' B1.r F.g0).mat - (conjBy h2' B1.r (F.star F.g0)).mat) i j = 0
    rw [e1, e2, ← Matrix.sub_mul, ← Matrix.mul_sub]
    exact hdiag2 i j hij
  have hB2cont : B2.Cont (maximalIdeal C') := cj_cont hF.del_open _ h2' B1 hB1cont
  obtain ⟨ρ', hof', hcont'⟩ := pr_ofbox hF C' (maximalIdeal C') B2 hB2c hB2diag hB2cont
  refine ⟨h2' * h1, ρ', ?_, ?_, hcont', ?_, ?_⟩
  · show ((h2' : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C') * h1).map (p : C' →+* C) = 1
    rw [Matrix.map_mul]
    exact (congrArg₂ (· * ·) hh2 hh1).trans (Matrix.one_mul 1)
  · have e := hof'.map (p : C' →+* C)
    rw [show B2.map (p : C' →+* C) = B'.map (p : C' →+* C) from (cj_map _ h2' B1 hh2).trans hB1map] at e
    exact of_unique e hof
  · rw [conj_mul]
    exact hB2c
  · intro s
    rw [conj_mul]
    exact hof'.res s


[APD_SRC_MU] Full original pr_mu for an arbitrary annihilated ideal K
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrMu.lean#L5-L70
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_mu :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (C'' : Type) [CommRing C''] [Algebra O C''] [IsCO O C''] (K : Ideal C') (q : C' →ₐ[O] C'') (u : K →ₗ[O] ResidueField O), maximalIdeal C' * K = ⊥ → K ≤ maximalIdeal C' → Function.Surjective q → (∀ y : K, u y = 0 → q y = 0) → ∃ μ : ResidueField O →+ C'', IsSq O μ ∧ (∀ y : K, μ (u y) = q y) ∧ ∀ c, ∃ y : K, μ c = q y := by
  intro O _ _ C' _ _ _ C'' _ _ _ K q u hmK hKm hq hker
  classical
  have hwd : ∀ y y' : K, u y = u y' → q y = q y' := by
    intro y y' h
    have h0 : u (y - y') = 0 := by rw [map_sub, h, sub_self]
    have h1 := hker _ h0
    rw [Submodule.coe_sub, map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h1
    exact h1
  have hmem : ∀ y : K, q y ∈ maximalIdeal C'' := by
    intro y
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O C'', res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]
    exact hKm y.2
  by_cases hu : ∀ y, u y = 0
  · refine ⟨0, ⟨fun a c => by simp, fun c => by simp⟩, fun y => ?_, fun c => ⟨0, by simp⟩⟩
    show (0 : C'') = q y
    exact (hker y (hu y)).symm
  · push_neg at hu
    obtain ⟨y0, hy0⟩ := hu
    have hsurj : Function.Surjective u := by
      intro c
      obtain ⟨o, ho⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective (c * (u y0)⁻¹)
      refine ⟨o • y0, ?_⟩
      rw [map_smul, Algebra.smul_def]
      show residue O o * u y0 = c
      rw [ho, mul_assoc, inv_mul_cancel₀ hy0, mul_one]
    let s := Function.surjInv hsurj
    have hs : ∀ c, u (s c) = c := Function.surjInv_eq hsurj
    let μ : ResidueField O →+ C'' :=
      { toFun := fun c => q (s c)
        map_zero' := by
          show q (s 0 : C') = 0
          rw [hwd (s 0) 0 (by rw [hs, map_zero])]
          simp
        map_add' := by
          intro c d
          show q (s (c + d) : C') = q (s c : C') + q (s d : C')
          rw [hwd (s (c + d)) (s c + s d) (by rw [hs, map_add, hs, hs]), Submodule.coe_add, map_add] }
    have hμ : ∀ c, μ c = q (s c) := fun c => rfl
    refine ⟨μ, ⟨?_, ?_⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro a c
      obtain ⟨b, rfl⟩ := hq a
      obtain ⟨o, ho⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) b
      have hby : b * (s c : C') = o • (s c : C') := by
        have hz : (b - algebraMap O C' o) * (s c : C') = 0 := by
          have hin : (b - algebraMap O C' o) * (s c : C') ∈ maximalIdeal C' * K :=
            Ideal.mul_mem_mul ho (s c).2
          rw [hmK] at hin
          simpa using hin
        rw [Algebra.smul_def]
        linear_combination hz
      have hres : res O C' b = algebraMap O (ResidueField O) o := by
        rw [← AlgHom.commutes (res O C') o, ← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, res_eq_zero_iff]
        exact ho
      have hw := hwd (s (res O C'' (q b) * c)) (o • s c)
        (by rw [hs, res_map, hres, map_smul, hs, Algebra.smul_def])
      rw [hμ, hμ, hw, Submodule.coe_smul_of_tower, ← hby, map_mul]
    · intro c
      rw [hμ]
      exact hmem _
    · intro y
      rw [hμ]
      exact hwd _ _ (hs _)
    · intro c
      exact ⟨s c, rfl⟩


[APD_SRC_OBS] Full pr_obs with its one-sided quotient-kernel condition and arbitrary p
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ValSubOneMap.lean#L8-L118

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

variable {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {C' : Type} [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C']
  {C'' : Type} [CommRing C''] [Algebra O C''] [IsCO O C'']

private theorem mem_max_iff (q : C' →ₐ[O] C'') (x : C') : q x ∈ maximalIdeal C'' ↔ x ∈ maximalIdeal C' := by
  rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]

private theorem adicCont_map {Γ : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] (q : C' →ₐ[O] C'') {f : Γ → C'}
    (hf : AdicCont (maximalIdeal C') f) : AdicCont (maximalIdeal C'') fun g => q (f g) := by
  intro N
  have hle : maximalIdeal C' ^ N ≤ (maximalIdeal C'' ^ N).comap (q : C' →+* C'') := by
    refine le_trans (Ideal.pow_right_mono (fun y hy => ?_) N) (Ideal.le_comap_pow _ N)
    exact (mem_max_iff q y).2 hy
  exact (hf N).comp (Ideal.quotientMap _ (q : C' →+* C'') hle)

private theorem map_sgnAct {m : ℕ} {A A' : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing A'] (f : A →+* A') (s : ℤˣ)
    (g : GL (Fin m) A) :
    GeneralLinearGroup.map f (sgnAct m A s g) = sgnAct m A' s (GeneralLinearGroup.map f g) := by
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or s with rfl | rfl
  · rw [sgnAct_one, sgnAct_one]
  · rw [sgnAct_neg_one, sgnAct_neg_one, map_theta]

private theorem val_sub_one_map {m : ℕ} {A A' : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing A'] (f : A →+* A')
    (g : GL (Fin m) A) (i j : Fin m) :
    (((GeneralLinearGroup.map f g : GL (Fin m) A') : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) A') - 1) i j =
      f (((g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) A) - 1) i j) := by
  have e : ((GeneralLinearGroup.map f g : GL (Fin m) A') : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) A') - 1 =
      f.mapMatrix ((g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) A) - 1) := by
    rw [map_sub, map_one]
    rfl
  rw [e]
  rfl

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ}
  {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] {B : Box F n R}

private noncomputable def push (q : C' →ₐ[O] C'') (p'' : C'' →ₐ[O] R) (D : SetLift (p''.comp q) B) :
    SetLift p'' B where
  r s := GeneralLinearGroup.map (q : C' →+* C'') (D.r s)
  r_cont i j := adicCont_map q (D.r_cont i j)
  r_map s := D.r_map s
  g v := GeneralLinearGroup.map (q : C' →+* C'') (D.g v)
  g_map v := D.g_map v
  t v s := GeneralLinearGroup.map (q : C' →+* C'') (D.t v s)
  t_cont v i j := adicCont_map q (D.t_cont v i j)
  t_tri v s i j h := (congrArg q (D.t_tri v s i j h)).trans (map_zero q)
  t_map v s := D.t_map v s

private theorem push_om (q : C' →ₐ[O] C'') (p'' : C'' →ₐ[O] R) (D : SetLift (p''.comp q) B)
    (x : F.Γ × F.Γ) (i j : Fin n) : (push q p'' D).om x i j = q (D.om x i j) := by
  refine Eq.trans ?_ (val_sub_one_map (q : C' →+* C'') _ i j)
  rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, map_sgnAct]
  rfl

private theorem push_a (q : C' →ₐ[O] C'') (p'' : C'' →ₐ[O] R) (D : SetLift (p''.comp q) B)
    (v : F.V) (s : F.Gv v) (i j : Fin n) : (push q p'' D).a v s i j = q (D.a v s i j) := by
  refine Eq.trans ?_ (val_sub_one_map (q : C' →+* C'') _ i j)
  rw [map_mul, map_inv, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv]
  rfl

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_obs :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (ρ : NRep F n R) (B : Box F n R), ρ.Univ O → B.Of ρ → B.Cont (maximalIdeal R) → (ρ.bar O).Good → ∀ (C' : Type) [CommRing C'] [Algebra O C'] [IsCO O C'] (p : C' →ₐ[O] R) (D : SetLift p B) (u : RingHom.ker p →ₗ[O] ResidueField O), maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ → D.co u ∈ continuousCoboundaries2 F.loc (B.bar O).ad (B.bar O).b → ∀ (C'' : Type) [CommRing C''] [Algebra O C''] [IsCO O C''] (q : C' →ₐ[O] C'') (p'' : C'' →ₐ[O] R), p''.comp q = p → Function.Surjective q → (∀ y : RingHom.ker p, u y = 0 → q y = 0) → ∃ s : R →ₐ[O] C'', p''.comp s = AlgHom.id O R := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ R _ _ _ ρ B hU hOf hC hG C' _ _ _ p D u hsm hco C'' _ _ _ q p'' hpq hq hu
  subst hpq
  have hK : RingHom.ker (p''.comp q) ≤ maximalIdeal C' := fun y hy => by
    rw [← mem_max_iff (p''.comp q), (RingHom.mem_ker).1 hy]
    exact zero_mem _
  obtain ⟨μ, hμ, hμu, hμq⟩ := pr_mu O C' C'' _ q u hsm hK hq hu
  obtain ⟨hom, ha, -, -⟩ := pr_omk hF O C' R (p''.comp q) B D hsm
  obtain ⟨β, Y, hβ, h1, h2⟩ := hco
  have hev : ∀ y ∈ RingHom.ker (p''.comp q), q y = μ (evK _ u y) := fun y hy => by
    rw [evK_of_mem u hy, hμu]
  have hp0 : ∀ c, p'' (μ c) = 0 := fun c => by
    obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hμq c
    rw [hy]
    exact y.2
  obtain ⟨B', hB'c, hB'm, -⟩ := pr_correct hF O C'' R p'' B (push q p'' D) μ hμ hp0 β Y (D.co u).2 hβ
    (fun x => by
      rw [← h1]
      ext i j
      rw [push_om, hev _ (hom x i j)]
      rfl)
    (fun v s => by
      ext i j
      rw [push_a, hev _ (ha v s i j)]
      rfl) h2
  have hsm'' : maximalIdeal C'' * RingHom.ker p'' = ⊥ := by
    refine le_bot_iff.1 (Ideal.mul_le.2 fun a ha' b hb => ?_)
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := hq a
    obtain ⟨b, rfl⟩ := hq b
    have hab : a * b ∈ maximalIdeal C' * RingHom.ker (p''.comp q) :=
      Ideal.mul_mem_mul ((mem_max_iff q a).1 ha') hb
    rw [hsm, Ideal.mem_bot] at hab
    rw [← map_mul, hab, map_zero]
    exact zero_mem _
  obtain ⟨-, ρ', -, hρm, hρc, -, -⟩ := pr_norm hF O C'' R p'' ρ B' hsm'' hG (hB'm ▸ hOf) hB'c
  have hbar : ρ'.bar O = ρ.bar O := by
    have e : (res O C'' : C'' →+* ResidueField O) =
        (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O).comp (p'' : C'' →+* R) :=
      RingHom.ext fun x => (res_map p'' x).symm
    show ρ'.map _ = ρ.map _
    rw [e, ← NRep.map_map, hρm]
  obtain ⟨g, hg, -⟩ := hU.univ C'' ρ' hρc hbar
  obtain ⟨g', -, hg'⟩ := hU.univ R ρ hU.cont rfl
  refine ⟨g, (hg' _ ?_).trans (hg' _ rfl).symm⟩
  show ρ.map ((p'' : C'' →+* R).comp (g : R →+* C'')) = ρ
  rw [← NRep.map_map, hg, hρm]


[APD_SRC_TBOX] Full strict-cocycle dual-number box
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrTbox.lean#L6-L115
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_tbox :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ [IsCO O (ResidueField O)] [IsCO O (DualNumber (ResidueField O))] (B : Box F n (ResidueField O)), (IsLocallyConstant fun s => (B.r s).mat) → ∀ x ∈ Z1C F.loc B.ad B.b, ∃ B' : Box F n (DualNumber (ResidueField O)), B'.Cont (maximalIdeal _) ∧ B'.map (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom O (ResidueField O) (ResidueField O) : (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) →+* ResidueField O) = B ∧ ∀ s, (B'.r s).mat.map TrivSqZeroExt.snd = x.1 s * (B.r s).mat := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ _ _ B hB x hx
  have hres : ∀ y : ResidueField O, res O (ResidueField O) y = y := by
    intro y
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective y
    have h := (res O (ResidueField O)).commutes a
    rw [IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq] at h
    exact h
  have hbar : Box.bar O B = B := by
    apply Box.ext
    · ext s
      · simp [Box.bar, Box.map, GG.map, hres]
      · simp [Box.bar, Box.map, GG.map]
    · funext v
      ext i j
      simp [Box.bar, Box.map, hres]
  let ι : ResidueField O →+* DualNumber (ResidueField O) := algebraMap _ _
  let p := TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom O (ResidueField O) (ResidueField O)
  have hp : ∀ X : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O), GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : DualNumber (ResidueField O) →+* ResidueField O) (GeneralLinearGroup.map ι X) = X := by
    intro X
    ext i j
    simp [p, ι, TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl]
  have hloc : ∀ v, Continuous (F.loc v) := by
    intro v
    exact continuous_subtype_val.comp (hF.loc_cont v)
  have hlc : ∀ (f : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O) → DualNumber (ResidueField O)) (N : ℕ),
      IsLocallyConstant fun s => Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) ^ N) (f (B.r s).mat) :=
    fun f N => hB.comp (fun M => Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (f M))
  let D : SetLift p B :=
    { r := fun s => GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.r s).left
      r_cont := fun i j N => hlc (fun M => ι (M i j)) N
      r_map := fun s => hp _
      g := fun v => GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.g v)
      g_map := fun v => hp _
      t := fun v s => GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.g v * (B.r (F.loc v s)).left * (B.g v)⁻¹)
      t_cont := by
        intro v i j N
        have h := (hlc (fun M => ι (((B.g v).val * M * (B.g v)⁻¹.val) i j)) N).comp_continuous (hloc v)
        exact h
      t_tri := by
        intro v s i j hij
        have h := congrArg ι (B.tri v s i j hij)
        rw [map_zero] at h
        exact h
      t_map := fun v s => hp _ }
  let μ : ResidueField O →+ DualNumber (ResidueField O) :=
    (TrivSqZeroExt.inrHom (ResidueField O) (ResidueField O)).toAddMonoidHom
  have hresD : ∀ a : DualNumber (ResidueField O), res O (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) a = a.fst := by
    intro a
    rw [← res_map p a, hres]
    rfl
  have hμ : IsSq O μ := by
    constructor
    · intro a c
      rw [hresD]
      ext
      · simp [μ]
      · show (TrivSqZeroExt.inr (a.fst * c) : DualNumber (ResidueField O)).snd = (a * TrivSqZeroExt.inr c).snd
        rw [TrivSqZeroExt.snd_mul]
        simp only [TrivSqZeroExt.fst_inr, TrivSqZeroExt.snd_inr, MulOpposite.op_zero, zero_smul, add_zero, smul_eq_mul]
    · intro c
      rw [IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff]
      intro hu
      have := hu.map p
      simp [p, μ] at this
  have hR : ∀ s t : F.Γ, GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.r (s * t)).left =
      GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.r s).left * sgnAct n _ (F.del s) (GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.r t).left) := by
    intro s t
    have h1 := congrArg SemidirectProduct.left (map_mul ((GG.map ι).comp B.r) s t)
    have h2 : (((GG.map ι).comp B.r) s).right = F.del s := B.nu s
    rw [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, h2] at h1
    exact h1
  refine (pr_correct hF O _ _ p B D μ hμ (fun c => by simp [p, μ]) (fun s => -x.1 s) (fun v => -x.2 v)
    (fun _ _ => 0) ?g1 ?g2 ?g3 ?g4).imp fun B' h => ⟨h.1, h.2.1, fun s => ?g5⟩
  case g5 =>
    rw [h.2.2 s]
    ext i j
    show (((1 - (-x.1 s).map μ) * ((B.r s).left.val.map ι) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (DualNumber (ResidueField O))) i j).snd = _
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply, TrivSqZeroExt.snd_sum, GG.mat, Matrix.mul_apply]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun m _ => ?_
    rw [TrivSqZeroExt.snd_mul]
    simp [Matrix.one_apply, μ, ι, TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl, op_smul_eq_mul]
    left
    split_ifs <;> simp
  case g1 => exact hx.1.comp (fun M => -M)
  case g2 =>
    intro y
    rw [hbar]
    have hc := hx.2.1 y.1 y.2
    have h0 : dOne B.ad (fun s => -x.1 s) y = 0 := by
      simp only [dOne, map_neg, hc]
      abel
    rw [h0, Matrix.map_zero _ (map_zero μ)]
    show (GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.r y.1).left * sgnAct n _ (F.del y.1) (GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.r y.2).left) *
      (GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.r (y.1 * y.2)).left)⁻¹).val - 1 = 0
    rw [hR, mul_inv_cancel, Units.val_one, sub_self]
  case g3 =>
    intro v s
    rw [Matrix.map_zero _ (map_zero μ)]
    show (GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.r (F.loc v s)).left * ((GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.g v))⁻¹ *
      GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.g v * (B.r (F.loc v s)).left * (B.g v)⁻¹) * GeneralLinearGroup.map ι (B.g v))⁻¹).val - 1 = 0
    simp [map_mul, map_inv, mul_assoc]
  case g4 =>
    intro v s
    rw [hbar]
    have h := hx.2.2 v s
    convert h using 1
    simp only [map_neg]
    abel


[APD_SRC_DOF] Actual universal dual-number map and derivative identity on all Gamma
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ResDual.lean#L5-L58

private theorem res_dual (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsCO O (DualNumber (ResidueField O))]
    (x : DualNumber (ResidueField O)) : res O _ x = x.fst := by
  obtain ⟨o, ho⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective x.fst
  have ha : (algebraMap O (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) o).fst = x.fst := by
    rw [← ho]
    first
      | rfl
      | rw [TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl, TrivSqZeroExt.fst_inl, IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq]
  have hfst : (x - algebraMap O _ o).fst = 0 := by
    rw [TrivSqZeroExt.fst_sub, ha, sub_self]
  have hsq : (x - algebraMap O _ o) * (x - algebraMap O _ o) = 0 := by
    ext
    · rw [TrivSqZeroExt.fst_mul, hfst, mul_zero, TrivSqZeroExt.fst_zero]
    · rw [TrivSqZeroExt.snd_mul, hfst, zero_smul, MulOpposite.op_zero, zero_smul, add_zero, TrivSqZeroExt.snd_zero]
  have hmem : x - algebraMap O _ o ∈ maximalIdeal (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) := by
    rw [IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff]
    intro hu
    have h2 := hu.mul hu
    rw [hsq] at h2
    exact not_isUnit_zero h2
  have h0 := (res_eq_zero_iff O _ _).2 hmem
  rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at h0
  rw [h0, ← ha]
  first
    | rfl
    | rw [TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl, TrivSqZeroExt.fst_inl]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_dof :
