    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ [IsCO O (DualNumber (ResidueField O))] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (ρ : NRep F n R) (B : Box F n R), ρ.Univ O → B.Of ρ → ∀ (ρ' : NRep F n (DualNumber (ResidueField O))) (r' : F.Γ →* GG n (DualNumber (ResidueField O))), ρ'.Cont (maximalIdeal _) → ρ'.bar O = ρ.bar O → (∀ s, (r' s).right = F.del s) → (r' F.c).mat = 1 → (∀ s : F.G0, (r' s).mat = ρ'.r s) → ∃ D ∈ Tan O R, ∀ s, (r' s).mat.map TrivSqZeroExt.snd = B.tanCo O D s * ((B.bar O).r s).mat := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ _ R _ _ _ ρ B hU hOf ρ' r' hc' hbar hnu hatc hres
  obtain ⟨g, hg, -⟩ := hU.univ _ ρ' hc' hbar
  have hfst : ∀ x, (g x).fst = res O R x := fun x => by
    rw [← res_dual O (g x), res_map]
  let Dd : R →ₗ[O] ResidueField O :=
    { toFun := fun x => (g x).snd
      map_add' := fun x y => by
        simp only [map_add, TrivSqZeroExt.snd_add]
      map_smul' := fun a x => by
        simp only [map_smul, TrivSqZeroExt.snd_smul, RingHom.id_apply] }
  have hDd : ∀ x, Dd x = (g x).snd := fun _ => rfl
  have hof := hOf.map (g : R →+* DualNumber (ResidueField O))
  have hr : (Box.map (g : R →+* DualNumber (ResidueField O)) B).r = r' := by
    refine pr_ext hF _ _ _ (Box.nu _) hnu ?_ ?_
    · rw [hof.atc, hatc]
    · intro s
      rw [hof.res, hg, hres]
  refine ⟨Dd, ?_, fun s => ?_⟩
  · intro f h
    rw [hDd, hDd, hDd, map_mul, TrivSqZeroExt.snd_mul, hfst, hfst, op_smul_eq_smul]
  · rw [← hr]
    show ((B.r s).mat.map g).map TrivSqZeroExt.snd = ((B.r s).mat.map Dd * ((((Box.bar O B).r s).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O))) * ((((Box.bar O B).r s).left : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O))
    rw [mul_assoc, Units.inv_mul, mul_one, Matrix.map_map]
    rfl


[APD_SRC_TSURJ] Full original pr_tsurj, with no independent tangent-surjectivity assumption
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrTsurj.lean#L12-L119
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_tsurj :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (ρ : NRep F n R) (B : Box F n R), ρ.Univ O → B.Of ρ → B.Cont (maximalIdeal R) → (ρ.bar O).Good → ∀ x ∈ Z1C F.loc (B.bar O).ad (B.bar O).b, ∃ D ∈ Tan O R, ∃ X, ∀ s, B.tanCo O D s = x.1 s + ((B.bar O).ad s X - X) := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ R _ _ _ ρ B hU hOf hCont hGood x hx
  obtain ⟨hco1, hco2⟩ := co_res O
  haveI := hco1
  haveI := hco2
  have hresk : ∀ c : ResidueField O, res O (ResidueField O) c = c := by
    intro c
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective c
    rw [← IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq, AlgHom.commutes]
  let p := TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom O (ResidueField O) (ResidueField O)
  have hresd : ∀ a : DualNumber (ResidueField O), res O (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) a = a.fst := by
    intro a
    rw [← res_map p a, hresk]
    rfl
  obtain ⟨-, -, hlc⟩ := pr_open hF O R B hCont
  obtain ⟨B', hB'c, hB'map, hB'snd⟩ := pr_tbox hF O (B.bar O) hlc x hx
  have hOfbar : (Box.map (p : DualNumber (ResidueField O) →+* ResidueField O) B').Of (ρ.bar O) := by
    rw [hB'map]
    exact hOf.map _
  have hn := pr_norm hF O (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) (ResidueField O) p (ρ.bar O) B'
  have hker : maximalIdeal (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) * RingHom.ker p = ⊥ := by
    refine eq_bot_iff.2 (Ideal.mul_le.2 fun a ha b hb => ?_)
    have ha0 : a.fst = 0 := by
      by_contra hne
      exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).1 ha (TrivSqZeroExt.isUnit_iff_isUnit_fst.2 (isUnit_iff_ne_zero.2 hne))
    have hb0 : b.fst = 0 := by simpa [p] using RingHom.mem_ker.1 hb
    rw [Ideal.mem_bot]
    ext <;> simp [ha0, hb0]
  have hbb : NRep.bar O (NRep.bar O ρ) = NRep.bar O ρ := by
    refine NRep.ext ?_ ?_
    · refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => Matrix.ext fun i j => ?_
      simp only [NRep.bar, NRep.map_r]
      exact hresk _
    · funext v i
      refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => Units.ext ?_
      simp only [NRep.bar, NRep.map_ψ]
      exact hresk _
  obtain ⟨h, ρ', hh1, hρ'p, hρ'c, hrc, hr0⟩ := hn hker (by rw [hbb]; exact hGood) hOfbar hB'c
  have hfun : ((res O (DualNumber (ResidueField O))) : DualNumber (ResidueField O) →+* ResidueField O) = (p : DualNumber (ResidueField O) →+* ResidueField O) :=
    RingHom.ext fun a => hresd a
  have hbar' : NRep.bar O ρ' = NRep.bar O ρ := by
    show NRep.map _ ρ' = _
    rw [hfun, hρ'p]
  obtain ⟨D, hD, hDs⟩ := pr_dof hF O R ρ B hU hOf ρ' (conjBy h B'.r) hρ'c hbar'
    (fun s => by simp [conjBy, B'.nu s]) hrc hr0
  let μ : ResidueField O →+ DualNumber (ResidueField O) :=
    (TrivSqZeroExt.inrHom (ResidueField O) (ResidueField O)).toAddMonoidHom
  have hμ : IsSq O μ := by
    constructor
    · intro a c
      rw [hresd]
      ext
      · simp [μ]
      · simp [μ, TrivSqZeroExt.snd_mul, MulOpposite.smul_eq_mul_unop, smul_eq_mul, mul_comm]
    · intro c
      rw [IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, TrivSqZeroExt.isUnit_iff_isUnit_fst]
      simp [μ]
  obtain ⟨e, he1, he2, he3, he4, he5⟩ := pr_sqz O (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) n μ hμ
  let Y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O) := (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (DualNumber (ResidueField O))).map TrivSqZeroExt.snd
  have hhe : h = e Y := by
    refine Units.ext (Matrix.ext fun i j => ?_)
    rw [he1]
    have h0 := congrFun (congrFun hh1 i) j
    simp only [Matrix.map_apply] at h0
    refine TrivSqZeroExt.ext ?_ ?_
    · by_cases hij : i = j
      · subst hij
        simpa [μ, p] using h0
      · simpa [μ, p, Matrix.one_apply_ne hij] using h0
    · by_cases hij : i = j
      · subst hij
        simp [μ, Y]
      · simp [μ, Y, Matrix.one_apply_ne hij]
  refine ⟨D, hD, -Y, fun s => ?_⟩
  have hgbar : GG.map ((res O (DualNumber (ResidueField O))) : DualNumber (ResidueField O) →+* ResidueField O) (B'.r s) = (B.bar O).r s := by
    rw [hfun, ← hB'map]
    rfl
  have hconj : conjBy h B'.r s = GG.ofGL (e (Y + ((B.bar O).r s).adFun (-Y))) * B'.r s := by
    have h5 := he5 (B'.r s) (-Y)
    rw [hgbar] at h5
    rw [he2, map_mul, mul_assoc, ← h5, ← mul_assoc, ← he3, ← hhe, map_inv]
    rfl
  have hm : ∀ (A : GL (Fin n) (DualNumber (ResidueField O))) (g : GG n (DualNumber (ResidueField O))),
      (GG.ofGL A * g).mat = (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (DualNumber (ResidueField O))) * g.mat := by
    intro A g
    simp [GG.mat, GG.ofGL]
  have hmat : (conjBy h B'.r s).mat = (B'.r s).mat + ((Y + ((B.bar O).r s).adFun (-Y)) * ((B.bar O).r s).mat).map μ := by
    rw [hconj, hm, he1, add_mul, one_mul, (he4 _ _).2, ← hgbar, GG.map_mat]
    rfl
  have hsnd := hDs s
  rw [hmat] at hsnd
  have hadd : ∀ (P Q : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (DualNumber (ResidueField O))),
      (P + Q).map TrivSqZeroExt.snd = P.map TrivSqZeroExt.snd + Q.map TrivSqZeroExt.snd := by
    intro P Q
    ext i j
    simp
  have hmapsnd : ∀ W : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O), (W.map μ).map TrivSqZeroExt.snd = W := by
    intro W
    ext i j
    simp [μ]
  rw [hadd, hmapsnd, hB'snd s, ← add_mul] at hsnd
  have hM : ((B.bar O).r s).mat * (((B.bar O).r s).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O) ) = (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) := Units.mul_inv _
  have h6 := congrArg (· * ((((B.bar O).r s).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) (ResidueField O)) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField O))) hsnd
  simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, hM, Matrix.mul_one] at h6
  rw [← h6]
  show _ = x.1 s + (((B.bar O).r s).adFun (-Y) - -Y)
  abel


[APD_SRC_TOR] Full original weight-torus tangent construction pr_tor
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/GgMapOfGL.lean#L7-L165
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.IntegralCasts.CapelliIdentities.LocallyConstantOfKernel

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem lc_of_ker {G H : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] [Group H]
    (f : G →* H) (h : IsOpen (f.ker : Set G)) : IsLocallyConstant f :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.LocallyConstantOfKernel.lc_of_ker f h

private theorem res_dual (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsCO O (DualNumber (ResidueField O))]
    (x : DualNumber (ResidueField O)) : res O (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) x = x.fst := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := residue_surjective x.fst
  have h1 : x - algebraMap O _ a ∈ maximalIdeal (DualNumber (ResidueField O)) := by
    rw [mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, TrivSqZeroExt.isUnit_iff_isUnit_fst, isUnit_iff_ne_zero, not_not,
      TrivSqZeroExt.fst_sub, ← ha]
    exact sub_self _
  have h2 := (res_eq_zero_iff O _ _).2 h1
  rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at h2
  rw [h2, ← ha]
  rfl

private theorem of_unique {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ} {C : Type}
    [CommRing C] {B : Box F n C} {ρ ρ' : NRep F n C} (h : B.Of ρ) (h' : B.Of ρ') : ρ = ρ' := by
  have e : ρ.r = ρ'.r := MonoidHom.ext fun s => (h.res s).symm.trans (h'.res s)
  refine NRep.ext e (funext fun v => funext fun i => MonoidHom.ext fun s => Units.ext ?_)
  rw [← h.psi, ← h'.psi, e]

private theorem conj_mat {m : ℕ} {C : Type} [CommRing C] (h : GL (Fin m) C) (x : GG m C) (hx : x.right = 1) :
    (GG.ofGL h * x * (GG.ofGL h)⁻¹).left = h * x.left * h⁻¹ := by
  rw [← map_inv]
  simp [hx]

private theorem gg_map_ofGL {m : ℕ} {C C' : Type} [CommRing C] [CommRing C'] (f : C →+* C') (h : GL (Fin m) C) :
    GG.map f (GG.ofGL h) = GG.ofGL (GeneralLinearGroup.map f h) := rfl

private theorem dual_mul {K : Type} [Field K] (a : DualNumber K) (c : K) :
    (TrivSqZeroExt.inr (a.fst * c) : DualNumber K) = a * TrivSqZeroExt.inr c := by
  ext <;> simp [mul_comm]

private theorem dual_fst {K : Type} [Field K] {m : ℕ} (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) K) (i j : Fin m) :
    ((1 + X.map (TrivSqZeroExt.inr : K → DualNumber K)) i j).fst = (1 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) K) i j := by
  by_cases hij : i = j
  · subst hij; simp
  · simp [hij]

private theorem dual_snd {K : Type} [Field K] {m : ℕ} (X A : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) K) :
    ((1 + X.map (TrivSqZeroExt.inr : K → DualNumber K)) * A.map (TrivSqZeroExt.inl : K → DualNumber K)).map
      TrivSqZeroExt.snd = X * A := by
  ext i j
  rw [Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
  simp [Matrix.mul_apply, TrivSqZeroExt.snd_sum]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_tor :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (ρ : NRep F n R) (B : Box F n R), ρ.Univ O → B.Of ρ → B.Cont (maximalIdeal R) → (ρ.bar O).Good → thetaLie (lieT (ResidueField O) n) = lieT (ResidueField O) n → ∃ D ∈ Tan O R, ∀ s, B.tanCo O D s = (B.bar O).ad s (lieT _ n) - lieT _ n := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ R _ _ _ ρ B hU hB hC _ hT
  haveI := (co_res O).2
  let K := ResidueField O
  let C := DualNumber K
  let μ : K →+ C := (TrivSqZeroExt.inrHom K K).toAddMonoidHom
  let ι : K →+* C := (TrivSqZeroExt.inlHom K K)
  have hμ : IsSq O μ := by
    refine ⟨fun a c => ?_, fun c => ?_⟩
    · rw [res_dual]
      exact dual_mul a c
    · rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_dual]
      rfl
  have hres : (res O C : C →+* K).comp ι = RingHom.id K := RingHom.ext fun a => res_dual O _
  obtain ⟨e, e1, e2, e3, -, e5⟩ := pr_sqz O C n μ hμ
  set T := lieT K n with hTdef
  let B₀ : Box F n C := (B.bar O).map ι
  have hB₀ : B₀.bar O = B.bar O := by
    show ((B.bar O).map ι).map _ = _
    rw [Box.map_map, hres]
    rfl
  have hr₀ : ∀ s, GG.map (res O C : C →+* K) (B₀.r s) = (B.bar O).r s := fun s => by
    rw [← hB₀]; rfl
  let h := e (-T)
  have hinv : h⁻¹ = e T := by rw [e3, neg_neg]
  have hloc : ∀ s : F.G0, (B₀.r s).right = 1 := fun s => (B₀.nu s).trans s.2
  let B' : Box F n C :=
    { r := conjBy h B₀.r
      nu := fun s => by
        show (GG.ofGL h * B₀.r s * (GG.ofGL h)⁻¹).right = _
        rw [← map_inv]
        simp [B₀.nu]
      g := fun v => B₀.g v * h⁻¹
      tri := fun v s => by
        have : (conjBy h B₀.r (F.loc v s)).left = h * (B₀.r (F.loc v s)).left * h⁻¹ :=
          conj_mat h _ (hloc (F.loc0 v s))
        have h2 := B₀.tri v s
        unfold GG.mat at h2 ⊢
        rw [this, ← Units.val_mul, ← Units.val_mul]
        rw [← Units.val_mul, ← Units.val_mul] at h2
        convert h2 using 2
        group }
  have hform : ∀ s, B'.r s = GG.ofGL (e ((B.bar O).ad s T - T)) * B₀.r s := fun s => by
    show GG.ofGL h * B₀.r s * (GG.ofGL h)⁻¹ = _
    rw [← map_inv, hinv, mul_assoc, e5, hr₀, ← mul_assoc, ← map_mul, ← e2, neg_add_eq_sub]
    rfl
  have hmat : ∀ s, (B'.r s).mat = (1 + ((B.bar O).ad s T - T).map μ) * ((B.bar O).r s).mat.map ι := fun s => by
    rw [hform, ← e1]
    simp [GG.mat]
    rfl
  have hbar : B.bar O |>.Of (ρ.bar O) := hB.map _
  have hc : (B'.r F.c).mat = 1 := by
    have h1 : (B.bar O).ad F.c T = T := by
      have hl : ((B.bar O).r F.c).left = 1 := Units.ext hbar.atc
      have hd : ((B.bar O).r F.c).right ≠ 1 := by
        rw [(B.bar O).nu, hF.del_c]; decide
      show ((B.bar O).r F.c).adFun T = T
      unfold GG.adFun GG.mat
      rw [hl, if_neg hd, hT]
      simp
    rw [hmat, h1, hbar.atc, sub_self, Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero ι) (map_one ι), Matrix.map_zero _ (map_zero μ)]
    simp
  have hdiagE : ∀ a : K, ((e (a • T) : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) =
      Matrix.diagonal fun i => 1 + μ (a * (w n i : K)) := fun a => by
    rw [e1]
    ext1 i j
    by_cases hij : i = j
    · subst hij; simp [hTdef, lieT]
    · simp [hTdef, lieT, hij]
  have hd : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → ((B'.r F.g0).mat - (B'.r (F.star F.g0)).mat) i j = 0 := fun i j hij => by
    have h1 : ∀ s : F.G0, (B'.r s).mat = (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * ((ρ.bar O).r s).map ι *
        ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) := fun s => by
      rw [← hbar.res]
      exact congrArg Units.val (conj_mat h _ (hloc s))
    have h2 := (ρ.bar O).diag i j hij
    rw [h1, h1, ← Matrix.sub_mul, ← Matrix.mul_sub, hinv, ← one_smul K T, hdiagE, Matrix.mul_diagonal]
    have h3 : h = e ((-1 : K) • T) := by rw [neg_one_smul]
    rw [h3, hdiagE, Matrix.diagonal_mul, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.map_apply, Matrix.map_apply, ← map_sub,
      ← Matrix.sub_apply, h2]
    simp
  have hcont : B'.Cont (maximalIdeal C) := fun i j N => by
    have h1 := lc_of_ker _ (pr_open hF O R B hC).1
    exact h1.comp fun x => Ideal.Quotient.mk _ ((GG.ofGL h * GG.map ι x * (GG.ofGL h)⁻¹).mat i j)
  obtain ⟨ρ', hρ', hc'⟩ := pr_ofbox hF C _ B' hc hd hcont
  have hh : GeneralLinearGroup.map (res O C : C →+* K) h = 1 := by
    apply Units.ext
    ext i j
    show res O C ((e (-T) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) i j) = _
    rw [e1, res_dual]
    exact dual_fst _ i j
  have hB' : B'.bar O = B.bar O := by
    rw [← hB₀]
    refine Box.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun s => ?_) (funext fun v => ?_)
    · show GG.map _ (GG.ofGL h * B₀.r s * (GG.ofGL h)⁻¹) = GG.map _ (B₀.r s)
      rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, gg_map_ofGL, hh]
      simp
    · show GeneralLinearGroup.map _ (B₀.g v * h⁻¹) = GeneralLinearGroup.map _ (B₀.g v)
      rw [map_mul, map_inv, hh]
      simp
  have hρb : ρ'.bar O = ρ.bar O := of_unique (hB' ▸ hρ'.map _) hbar
  obtain ⟨D, hD, hD'⟩ := pr_dof hF O R ρ B hU hB ρ' B'.r hc' hρb B'.nu hc hρ'.res
  refine ⟨D, hD, fun s => ?_⟩
  have h1 : (B'.r s).mat.map TrivSqZeroExt.snd = ((B.bar O).ad s T - T) * ((B.bar O).r s).mat := by
    rw [hmat]
    exact dual_snd _ _
  have h2 := (hD' s).symm.trans h1
  exact (Units.mul_left_inj ((B.bar O).r s).left).1 h2


[APD_SRC_RES] Full original pr_res, exact k-to-k' map and the triangular alternative
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrRes.lean#L7-L73
    [IsCO O' C] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O' C)] :
    IsLocalRing.residue C =
      (IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map (algebraMap O' C)).comp (res O' C : C →+* ResidueField O') := by
  ext z
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O') z
  have h1 : (res O' C) z = (res O' C) (algebraMap O' C a) := by
    rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, res_eq_zero_iff]
    exact ha
  have h2 : IsLocalRing.residue C z = IsLocalRing.residue C (algebraMap O' C a) := by
    rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff]
    exact ha
  rw [RingHom.comp_apply, RingHom.coe_coe, h1, h2, AlgHom.commutes]
  exact (IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue _ a).symm

private theorem nRep_isBar_of_isBar_comp_map {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ}
    {K K' : Type} [Field K] [CommRing K'] [Nontrivial K'] (f : k →+* K) (j : K →+* K')
    (σ : NRep F n K) (h : NRep.IsBar (j.comp f) (NRep.map j σ)) : NRep.IsBar f σ := by
  unfold NRep.IsBar at h ⊢
  exact ⟨fun g i => j.injective (h.1 g i), fun v i g => j.injective (h.2 v i g)⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_res :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), (∃ φ : ResidueField O →+* ResidueField L.O', (L.ρ.bar L.O').IsBar φ) ∧ ((∀ g, UpT ((L.ρ.bar L.O').r g)) ∨ ∀ g, UpT ((L.ρ.bar L.O').r g)ᵀ) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L
  have hOloc : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O L.O') := by
    refine ⟨fun a ha => ?_⟩
    by_contra h
    have hm : algebraMap O L.O' a ∈ maximalIdeal L.O' := by
      rw [← L.unr]
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ((IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal a).mpr h)
    exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).mp hm ha
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap L.O' L.R) := IsCO.isLocalHom
  let f0 : ResidueField O →+* ResidueField L.O' := IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map (algebraMap O L.O')
  let jR : ResidueField L.O' →+* ResidueField L.R :=
    IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map (algebraMap L.O' L.R)
  have hloc := loc_rho hF hn S x L
  refine ⟨⟨f0, ?_⟩, ?_⟩
  · have hfR : IsResMap O L.R (jR.comp f0) := by
      intro a
      show jR (f0 (IsLocalRing.residue O a)) = _
      rw [IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue, IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue,
        IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O L.O' L.R a]
    have hbR := hloc.2.1 _ hfR
    rw [residue_eq_map_algebraMap_comp_res_of_isCO (O' := L.O') (C := L.R)] at hbR
    exact nRep_isBar_of_isBar_comp_map f0 jR _ hbR
  · by_cases hP : S.u.triP ≤ x
    · left
      have hle : L.ρ.triP ≤ maximalIdeal L.R := by
        rw [(hloc.2.2.1 (maximalIdeal L.R)).1]
        show S.u.triP ≤ Ideal.comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) (maximalIdeal L.R)
        rw [L.lies]
        exact hP
      intro g i j hji
      have hm : L.ρ.r g i j ∈ maximalIdeal L.R := by
        refine hle ?_
        unfold NRep.triP
        exact Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, i, j, hji, rfl⟩
      show res L.O' L.R (L.ρ.r g i j) = 0
      exact (res_eq_zero_iff _ _ _).mpr hm
    · right
      have hle := (hloc.2.2.2 hP).2
      intro g i j hji
      have hm : L.ρ.r g j i ∈ maximalIdeal L.R := by
        refine hle ?_
        unfold NRep.triM
        exact Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, j, i, hji, rfl⟩
      show res L.O' L.R (L.ρ.r g j i) = 0
      exact (res_eq_zero_iff _ _ _).mpr hm


[APD_SRC_GOOD] Full original pr_good for all field maps and both allowed ranks
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrGood.lean#L5-L70
private theorem two_ne {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] (h : l ≠ 2) : (2 : k) ≠ 0 := by
  intro h2
  have h3 : ((2 : ℕ) : k) = 0 := by simpa using h2
  rw [CharP.cast_eq_zero_iff k l] at h3
  exact h ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq Fact.out Nat.prime_two).1 h3)

private theorem sq_facts {k : Type} [Field k] {t : kˣ} (h : t ^ 2 ≠ 1) :
    (t : k) ≠ (t : k)⁻¹ ∧ (t : k) ≠ 1 ∧ (t : k)⁻¹ ≠ 1 := by
  have h0 : (t : k) ≠ 0 := t.ne_zero
  have h1 : (t : k) ≠ 1 := fun e => h (by ext; simp [e])
  refine ⟨fun e => h ?_, h1, fun e => h1 (inv_eq_one.1 e)⟩
  ext
  rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, sq, Units.val_one]
  nth_rewrite 2 [e]
  exact mul_inv_cancel₀ h0

private theorem pow_inj {k : Type} [Field k] {t : kˣ} (h : t ^ 2 ≠ 1) {n : ℕ} (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3)
    (i j : Fin n) (hij : i ≠ j) : ((t ^ w n i : kˣ) : k) ≠ ((t ^ w n j : kˣ) : k) := by
  obtain ⟨a, b, c⟩ := sq_facts h
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
    first
    | exact absurd rfl hij
    | simp [w, a, b, c, a.symm, b.symm, c.symm]

private theorem diff_inj {k : Type} [Field k] (h2 : (2 : k) ≠ 0) {t : kˣ} (h : t ^ 2 ≠ 1) {n : ℕ}
    (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) (i j : Fin n) (hij : i ≠ j) :
    ((t ^ w n i : kˣ) : k) - ((t⁻¹ ^ w n i : kˣ) : k) ≠ ((t ^ w n j : kˣ) : k) - ((t⁻¹ ^ w n j : kˣ) : k) := by
  obtain ⟨a, -, -⟩ := sq_facts h
  have b : (t : k) - (t : k)⁻¹ ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 a
  have c : (t : k)⁻¹ - (t : k) ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 a.symm
  have d : (t : k) - (t : k)⁻¹ ≠ (t : k)⁻¹ - (t : k) := fun e =>
    mul_ne_zero h2 b (by linear_combination e)
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
    first
    | exact absurd rfl hij
    | simp [w, b, c, d, b.symm, c.symm, d.symm]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_good :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] (φ : k →+* K) (ρ : NRep F n K), ρ.IsBar φ → ρ.Good ∧ lieT K n ≠ 0 ∧ thetaLie (lieT K n) = lieT K n := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF hn K _ φ ρ hρ
  have h2 : (2 : k) ≠ 0 := two_ne hF.l_odd
  have hs : F.Ψ (F.star F.g0) = (F.Ψ F.g0)⁻¹ := by
    have e : ((F.star F.g0 : F.G0) : F.Γ) = F.c * (F.g0 : F.Γ)⁻¹ * F.c⁻¹ := rfl
    rw [e, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, map_inv, hF.Ψ_c]
    simp
  refine ⟨⟨?_, fun i j hij => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
  · have := (map_ne_zero φ).2 h2
    rwa [map_ofNat] at this
  · rw [Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.sub_apply, hρ.1, hρ.1, hρ.1, hρ.1, hs, ← map_sub, ← map_sub]
    exact fun e => diff_inj h2 hF.baseSubgroup_spec hn i j hij (φ.injective e)
  · obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hF.f3 v
    refine ⟨s, fun i j hij e => pow_inj hs hn i j hij (φ.injective ?_)⟩
    rw [← hρ.2, ← hρ.2, e]
  · intro e
    rcases hn with rfl | rfl
    · have := congrFun (congrFun e 0) 0
      simp [lieT, w] at this
    · have := congrFun (congrFun e 0) 0
      simp [lieT, w] at this
  · rcases hn with rfl | rfl
    · ext i j
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
        simp [thetaLie, lieT, w, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two, Jmat, Matrix.diagonal, Fin.rev] <;> norm_num
    · ext i j
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
        simp [thetaLie, lieT, w, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Jmat, Matrix.diagonal, Fin.rev] <;> norm_num


[APD_SRC_OPEN] Full pr_open, including arbitrary coefficient residue fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrOpen.lean#L5-L43
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_open :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O], ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (B : Box F n R), B.Cont (maximalIdeal R) → IsOpen ((B.bar O).r.ker : Set F.Γ) ∧ IsOpen ((B.bar O).ad.ker : Set F.Γ) ∧ IsLocallyConstant fun s => ((B.bar O).r s).mat := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n hF O _ _ R _ _ _ B hB
  have hent : ∀ i j, IsLocallyConstant fun s => ((B.bar O).r s).mat i j := fun i j => by
    have hk : ∀ a ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ 1, (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O) a = 0 := fun a ha => by
      rw [pow_one] at ha
      exact (res_eq_zero_iff O R a).2 ha
    exact (hB i j 1).comp (Ideal.Quotient.lift _ (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O) hk)
  have hmat : IsLocallyConstant fun s => ((B.bar O).r s).mat := by
    rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
    intro x
    have h : ∀ᶠ y in nhds x, ∀ i j, ((B.bar O).r y).mat i j = ((B.bar O).r x).mat i j :=
      Filter.eventually_all.2 fun i => Filter.eventually_all.2 fun j => (hent i j).eventually_eq x
    exact h.mono fun y hy => Matrix.ext hy
  have hr : IsOpen ((B.bar O).r.ker : Set F.Γ) := by
    have e : ((B.bar O).r.ker : Set F.Γ) =
        {s | ((B.bar O).r s).mat = 1} ∩ (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ) := by
      ext s
      constructor
      · intro hs
        have hs' : (B.bar O).r s = 1 := hs
        refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
        · show ((B.bar O).r s).mat = 1
          rw [hs']
          rfl
        · show F.del s = 1
          rw [← (B.bar O).nu s, hs']
          rfl
      · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
        show (B.bar O).r s = 1
        refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext h1) ?_
        rw [(B.bar O).nu s]
        exact h2
    rw [e]
    exact (hmat.isOpen_fiber 1).inter hF.del_open
  refine ⟨hr, Subgroup.isOpen_mono (fun s hs => ?_) hr, hmat⟩
  have hs' : (B.bar O).r s = 1 := hs
  show GG.ad n _ ((B.bar O).r s) = 1
  rw [hs', map_one]


[APD_SRC_BDIM] Full pr_bdim for all fields, ranks and change-of-basis matrices
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrBdim.lean#L7-L51
private def borEquiv (K : Type) [Field K] (n : ℕ) (g : GL (Fin n) K) :
    bor g ≃ₗ[K] ({p : Fin n × Fin n // p.1 ≤ p.2} → K) where
  toFun X p := ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * X.1 * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)) p.1.1 p.1.2
  map_add' X Y := by
    funext p
    simp only [Submodule.coe_add, Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.add_apply, Pi.add_apply]
  map_smul' c X := by
    funext p
    simp only [Submodule.coe_smul, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.smul_apply, Pi.smul_apply,
      RingHom.id_apply]
  invFun f := ⟨((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) *
      (Matrix.of fun i j => if h : i ≤ j then f ⟨(i, j), h⟩ else 0) * (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K), by
    intro i j h
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc,
      Units.mul_inv, Matrix.mul_one, Matrix.of_apply, dif_neg (not_le.mpr h)]⟩
  left_inv X := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    have e : (Matrix.of fun i j => if i ≤ j then ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * X.1 *
        ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)) i j else 0) =
        (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * X.1 * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) := by
      ext i j
      rw [Matrix.of_apply]
      split_ifs with h
      · rfl
      · exact (X.2 i j (not_le.mp h)).symm
    show ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) *
      (Matrix.of fun i j => if i ≤ j then ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * X.1 *
        ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)) i j else 0) * (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) = X.1
    rw [e, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc,
      Units.inv_mul, Matrix.mul_one]
  right_inv f := by
    funext p
    show ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * (((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) *
      (Matrix.of fun i j => if h : i ≤ j then f ⟨(i, j), h⟩ else 0) * (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)) *
      ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)) p.1.1 p.1.2 = f p
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc,
      Units.mul_inv, Matrix.mul_one, Matrix.of_apply, dif_pos p.2]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_bdim :
    ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] (n : ℕ) (g : GL (Fin n) K), 2 * Module.finrank K (bor g) = n * (n + 1) := by
  intro K _ n g
  rw [(borEquiv K n g).finrank_eq, Module.finrank_fintype_fun_eq_card,
    ← Fintype.card_congr (Sym2.sortEquiv (α := Fin n)), Sym2.card, Fintype.card_fin, Nat.choose_two_right,
    Nat.add_sub_cancel, Nat.mul_comm (n + 1) n]
  exact Nat.mul_div_cancel' (Nat.even_mul_succ_self n).two_dvd


[APD_SRC_BRIG] Full original local Borel rigidity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrBrig.lean#L6-L56
    UpT (A * B) := by
  intro i j h
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun c _ => ?_
  rcases lt_or_ge c i with hc | hc
  · rw [hA i c hc, zero_mul]
  · rw [hB c j (lt_of_lt_of_le h hc), mul_zero]

private theorem upT_of_comm {K : Type} [Field K] {n : ℕ} {T Z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K} (hT : UpT T)
    (hd : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → T i i ≠ T j j) (h : UpT (T * Z - Z * T)) : UpT Z := by
  have key : ∀ m : ℕ, ∀ i j : Fin n, n - i.val + j.val = m → j < i → Z i j = 0 := by
    intro m
    induction m using Nat.strong_induction_on with
    | _ m ih =>
      intro i j hm hji
      have h0 := h i j hji
      have hji' : j.val < i.val := hji
      rw [Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single i, Finset.sum_eq_single j] at h0
      · have h1 : (T i i - T j j) * Z i j = 0 := by linear_combination h0
        exact (mul_eq_zero.1 h1).resolve_left (sub_ne_zero.2 (hd i j hji.ne'))
      · intro c _ hc
        rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hc with hc | hc
        · have hc' : c.val < j.val := hc
          rw [ih (n - i.val + c.val) (by omega) i c rfl (hc.trans hji), zero_mul]
        · rw [hT c j hc, mul_zero]
      · simp
      · intro c _ hc
        rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hc with hc | hc
        · rw [hT i c hc, zero_mul]
        · have hc' : i.val < c.val := hc
          have := c.isLt
          rw [ih (n - c.val + j.val) (by omega) c j rfl (hji.trans hc), mul_zero]
      · simp
  exact fun i j hij => key _ i j rfl hij

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_brig :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ}, ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] (ρ : NRep F n K) (B : Box F n K), B.Of ρ → ρ.Good → ∀ (v : F.V) (Y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K), (∀ s, B.ad (F.loc v s) Y - Y ∈ B.b v) → Y ∈ B.b v := by
  intro l _ k _ F n K _ ρ B hOf hG v Y hY
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hG.ψd v
  have hT := B.tri v s
  have hr : (B.r (F.loc v s)).right = 1 := (B.nu _).trans (F.loc0 v s).2
  have hres : (B.r (F.loc v s)).mat = ρ.r (F.loc0 v s) := hOf.res (F.loc0 v s)
  have h1 : UpT ((B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * ((B.r (F.loc v s)).adFun Y - Y) *
      (((B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)) := hY s
  show UpT ((B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Y * (((B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K))
  refine upT_of_comm hT (fun i j hij => ?_) ?_
  · rw [hres, hOf.psi, hOf.psi]
    exact fun e => hs i j hij (Units.ext e)
  · convert upT_mul h1 hT using 1
    simp only [GG.adFun, hr, if_true, GG.mat, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc,
      Units.inv_mul_cancel_left]


[APD_SRC_BSTAB] Full local Borel stability over every commutative ring
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrBstab.lean#L6-L31
    (hM : UpT M) (hN : UpT N) : UpT (M * N) := by
  intro i j h
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun a _ => ?_
  by_cases ha : a < i
  · rw [hM i a ha, zero_mul]
  · rw [hN a j (lt_of_lt_of_le h (not_lt.mp ha)), mul_zero]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_bstab :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ}, ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] (B : Box F n C) (v : F.V) (s : F.Gv v), ∀ X ∈ B.b v, B.ad (F.loc v s) X ∈ B.b v := by
  intro l _ k _ F n C _ B v s X hX
  have h1 : (B.r (F.loc v s)).right = 1 := (B.nu _).trans (F.loc0 v s).2
  have h2 : (B.r (F.loc v s⁻¹)).left = (B.r (F.loc v s)).left⁻¹ := by
    rw [map_inv, map_inv, SemidirectProduct.inv_left, h1, inv_one, sgnAct_one]
  have t1 := B.tri v s
  have t2 := B.tri v s⁻¹
  have t3 := upT_mul (upT_mul t1 hX) t2
  simp only [GG.mat, h2] at t3
  show UpT ((B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * (B.r (F.loc v s)).adFun X *
    (((B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C))
  have hg : ∀ Y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C,
      (((B.g v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * ((B.g v : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * Y) = Y :=
    fun Y => Units.inv_mul_cancel_left _ Y
  simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, hg] at t3
  simp only [GG.adFun, GG.mat, h1, if_true, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  exact t3


[APD_SRC_CFIX] Full signed-involution fixed-space count for every rank, characteristic not two
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrCfix.lean#L7-L134
  have e : {p : Fin n × Fin n // p.1 < p.2} ≃ Σ j : Fin n, {i : Fin n // i < j} :=
    { toFun := fun p => ⟨p.1.2, p.1.1, p.2⟩
      invFun := fun q => ⟨(q.2.1, q.1), q.2.2⟩
      left_inv := fun p => rfl
      right_inv := fun q => rfl }
  have hc : ∀ j : Fin n, Fintype.card {i : Fin n // i < j} = j := by
    intro j
    rw [Fintype.card_subtype]
    have : (Finset.univ.filter fun i : Fin n => i < j) = Finset.Iio j := by
      ext i; simp
    rw [this, Fin.card_Iio]
  rw [Fintype.card_congr e, Fintype.card_sigma]
  simp only [hc]
  rw [Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun i => i) n]
  have hs := Finset.sum_range_id_mul_two n
  cases n with
  | zero => simp
  | succ m =>
    simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel] at hs
    nlinarith [hs]

private theorem finrank_skew {K : Type} [Field K] {n : ℕ} (h2 : (2 : K) ≠ 0)
    (W : Submodule K (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)) (S T : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
    (hST : S * T = 1) (hTS : T * S = 1) (hW : ∀ X, X ∈ W ↔ (X * S)ᵀ = -(X * S)) :
    Module.finrank K W = Fintype.card {p : Fin n × Fin n // p.1 < p.2} := by
  classical
  let M : ({p : Fin n × Fin n // p.1 < p.2} → K) → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K :=
    fun f => Matrix.of fun i j =>
      if h : i < j then f ⟨(i, j), h⟩ else if h' : j < i then -f ⟨(j, i), h'⟩ else 0
  have hskew : ∀ f, (M f)ᵀ = -(M f) := by
    intro f
    ext i j
    simp only [M, Matrix.transpose_apply, Matrix.neg_apply, Matrix.of_apply]
    split_ifs with h1 h2 h3 h4 <;> first | simp | (exfalso; omega)
  let e : W ≃ₗ[K] ({p : Fin n × Fin n // p.1 < p.2} → K) :=
    { toFun := fun X p => ((X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * S) p.1.1 p.1.2
      map_add' := by
        intro X Y
        funext p
        simp [Matrix.add_mul]
      map_smul' := by
        intro c X
        funext p
        simp [Matrix.smul_mul]
      invFun := fun f => ⟨M f * T, by
        rw [hW, Matrix.mul_assoc, hTS, Matrix.mul_one]
        exact hskew f⟩
      left_inv := by
        intro X
        apply Subtype.ext
        have hX : ((X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * S)ᵀ = -((X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * S) :=
          (hW X).1 X.2
        show M (fun p => ((X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * S) p.1.1 p.1.2) * T = X
        have hM : M (fun p => ((X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * S) p.1.1 p.1.2)
            = (X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * S := by
          ext i j
          have hij := congrFun (congrFun hX i) j
          have hji := congrFun (congrFun hX j) i
          simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, Matrix.neg_apply] at hij hji
          simp only [M, Matrix.of_apply]
          split_ifs with h1 h3
          · rfl
          · rw [hij, neg_neg]
          · have he : i = j := le_antisymm (not_lt.mp h3) (not_lt.mp h1)
            subst he
            have h22 : 2 * ((X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * S) i i = 0 := by
              linear_combination hij
            exact ((mul_eq_zero.mp h22).resolve_left h2).symm
        rw [hM, Matrix.mul_assoc, hST, Matrix.mul_one]
      right_inv := by
        intro f
        funext p
        obtain ⟨⟨i, j⟩, h⟩ := p
        show (M f * T * S) i j = f ⟨(i, j), h⟩
        rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, hTS, Matrix.mul_one]
        simp [M, h] }
  rw [e.finrank_eq, Module.finrank_fintype_fun_eq_card]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_cfix :
    ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] (n : ℕ) (x : GG n K), (2 : K) ≠ 0 → x * x = 1 → x.right = -1 → 2 * Module.finrank K (LinearMap.ker (GG.ad n K x - 1)) + n = n * n := by
  intro K _ n x h2 hx hr
  have hg : x.left * theta x.left = 1 := by
    have h := congrArg SemidirectProduct.left hx
    simpa [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hr, sgnAct_neg_one] using h
  have hG : (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
      = Jmat K n * (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)ᵀ * Jmat K n := by
    rw [← theta_inv_val, inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_left hg]
  have hGJ : (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Jmat K n
      = Jmat K n * (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)ᵀ := by
    nth_rewrite 1 [hG]
    rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.mul_one]
  have hST : ((x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Jmat K n)
      * (Jmat K n * ((x.left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)) = 1 := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc (Jmat K n), Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.one_mul,
      Units.mul_inv]
  have hTS : (Jmat K n * ((x.left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K))
      * ((x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Jmat K n) = 1 := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc ((x.left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K),
      Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_mul, Jmat_mul_Jmat]
  have hne : x.right ≠ 1 := by
    rw [hr]; decide
  have key : ∀ X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K, X ∈ LinearMap.ker (GG.ad n K x - 1) ↔
      (X * ((x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Jmat K n))ᵀ
        = -(X * ((x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Jmat K n)) := by
    intro X
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.sub_apply, sub_eq_zero, GG.ad_apply]
    simp only [Module.End.one_apply, GG.adFun, GG.mat, if_neg hne, thetaLie]
    have hL : (x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * -(Jmat K n * Xᵀ * Jmat K n)
        * ((x.left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        * ((x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Jmat K n)
        = -(X * ((x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Jmat K n))ᵀ := by
      rw [Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_mul, Jmat_transpose, ← hGJ]
      simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.neg_mul, Matrix.mul_neg]
      rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc ((x.left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K),
        Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_mul, Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.mul_one]
    constructor
    · intro h
      rw [h] at hL
      nth_rewrite 2 [hL]
      rw [neg_neg]
    · intro h
      rw [h, neg_neg] at hL
      have h' := congrArg (· * (Jmat K n * ((x.left⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) K) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K))) hL
      rw [Matrix.mul_assoc _ ((x.left : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) * Jmat K n), hST, Matrix.mul_one,
        Matrix.mul_assoc X, hST, Matrix.mul_one] at h'
      exact h'
  rw [finrank_skew h2 _ _ _ hST hTS key]
  exact card_pairs n


[APD_SRC_DUAL] Full pr_dual including outer weights and the exact sign character
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PrDual.lean#L5-L143
private theorem tri_zero {K G ι : Type} [Field K] [Fintype ι] [LinearOrder ι] (M : G → Matrix ι ι K) (ω : G → K)
    (Φ : Matrix ι ι K) (hM : ∀ g i j, j < i → M g i j = 0)
    (h : ∀ g i j, ∑ p, M g i p * Φ p j = ω g * ∑ q, Φ i q * M g q j)
    (key : ∀ i j, (∀ g, M g i i * Φ i j = ω g * (Φ i j * M g j j)) → Φ i j = 0) : ∀ i j, Φ i j = 0 := by
  intro i
  induction i using WellFoundedGT.induction with
  | _ i ih =>
  intro j
  induction j using WellFoundedLT.induction with
  | _ j ih' =>
  refine key i j fun g => ?_
  have := h g i j
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i, Finset.sum_eq_single j] at this
  · exact this
  · intro q _ hq
    rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hq with h1 | h1
    · rw [ih' q h1, zero_mul]
    · rw [hM g q j h1, mul_zero]
  · simp
  · intro p _ hp
    rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hp with h1 | h1
    · rw [hM g i p h1, zero_mul]
    · rw [ih p h1 j, mul_zero]
  · simp

private theorem om_map {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] (F : Frame l k) {K : Type} [Field K]
    [CharP K l] (φ : k →+* K) (g : F.Γ) : φ (F.om k g : k) = (F.om K g : K) := by
  have : φ.comp (ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k) = ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) K := Subsingleton.elim _ _
  exact RingHom.congr_fun this _

private theorem w_cases {n : ℕ} (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) (i j : Fin n) :
    w n j - w n i = 0 ∨ (w n j - w n i = 1 ∨ w n j - w n i = -1) ∨
      ((w n j - w n i = 2 ∨ w n j - w n i = -2) ∧ j = i.rev) := by
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl
  · revert i j; decide
  · revert i j; decide

private theorem thetaLie_single {K : Type} [Field K] {n : ℕ} (i : Fin n) :
    thetaLie (Matrix.single i i.rev (1 : K)) = -Matrix.single i i.rev (1 : K) := by
  unfold thetaLie
  congr 1
  ext a b
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single b.rev, Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single a.rev]
  · simp only [Jmat, Matrix.transpose_apply, Matrix.single_apply, Fin.rev_rev, if_true, one_mul, mul_one,
      Fin.rev_inj]
    congr 1
    refine propext ⟨?_, ?_⟩
    · rintro ⟨rfl, rfl⟩; simp
    · rintro ⟨rfl, rfl⟩; simp
  · intro p _ hp
    simp [Jmat, hp]
  · simp
  · intro p _ hp
    have : b ≠ p.rev := fun h => hp (by simp [h])
    simp [Jmat, this]
  · simp

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pr_dual :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [CharP K l] (φ : k →+* K) (ρ : NRep F n K) (B : Box F n K), B.Of ρ → ρ.IsBar φ → ((∀ g, UpT (ρ.r g)) ∨ ∀ g, UpT (ρ.r g)ᵀ) → ∀ f : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K →ₗ[K] K, (∀ g X, f (B.ad g X) = F.om K g * f X) → f = 0 := by
  intro l _ k _ _ F C₀ n ok hn K _ _ φ ρ B hB hbar htri f hf
  set Φ : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K := fun i j => f (Matrix.single i j 1) with hΦ
  have hsum : ∀ X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K, f X = ∑ i, ∑ j, X i j * Φ i j := fun X => by
    conv_lhs => rw [Matrix.matrix_eq_sum_single X]
    simp only [map_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
    rw [hΦ, ← smul_eq_mul, ← map_smul, Matrix.smul_single, smul_eq_mul, mul_one]
  have h0 : ∀ (g : F.G0) X, f (ρ.r g * X) = F.om K g * f (X * ρ.r g) := fun g X => by
    rw [← hf]
    congr 1
    have hr : (B.r g).right = 1 := (B.nu g).trans g.2
    show _ = (B.r g).adFun _
    rw [GG.adFun, if_pos hr, ← hB.res g, GG.mat]
    simp [Matrix.mul_assoc]
  have h1 : ∀ (g : F.G0) i j, ∑ p, ρ.r g p i * Φ p j = F.om K g * ∑ q, Φ i q * ρ.r g j q := fun g i j => by
    have := h0 g (Matrix.single i j 1)
    rw [hsum, hsum] at this
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i (s := Finset.univ) (f := fun a => ∑ b, (Matrix.single i j (1 : K) * ρ.r g) a b * Φ a b)]
      at this
    · convert this using 2 with p
      · rw [Finset.sum_eq_single j]
        · simp
        · intro b _ hb
          rw [Matrix.mul_single_apply_of_ne _ _ _ _ _ hb, zero_mul]
        · simp
      · simp [mul_comm]
    · intro a _ ha
      exact Finset.sum_eq_zero fun b _ => by rw [Matrix.single_mul_apply_of_ne _ _ _ _ _ ha, zero_mul]
    · simp
  have hc : ∀ i, -Φ i i.rev = F.om K F.c * Φ i i.rev := fun i => by
    rw [hΦ]
    dsimp only
    rw [← hf, ← map_neg, ← thetaLie_single]
    congr 1
    have hr : (B.r F.c).right ≠ 1 := by rw [B.nu, ok.del_c]; decide
    have hl : (B.r F.c).left = 1 := Units.ext hB.atc
    show _ = (B.r F.c).adFun _
    rw [GG.adFun, if_neg hr, GG.mat, hl]
    simp
  have key : ∀ i j, (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g i i * Φ i j = F.om K g * (Φ i j * ρ.r g j j)) → Φ i j = 0 := by
    intro i j h
    by_contra hne
    have h2 : ∀ g : F.G0, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ (w n j - w n i) = 1 := fun g => by
      have e := h g
      rw [hbar.1, hbar.1, ← om_map F φ, mul_comm (Φ i j), ← mul_assoc, ← map_mul] at e
      have e2 := φ.injective (mul_right_cancel₀ hne e)
      rw [← Units.val_mul, Units.val_inj] at e2
      rw [_root_.zpow_sub, ← mul_assoc, ← e2, mul_inv_cancel]
    rcases w_cases hn i j with h3 | h3 | ⟨h3, rfl⟩
    · obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := ok.f2a
      exact hg (by simpa [h3] using h2 g)
    · obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := ok.f2b _ h3
      exact hg (h2 g)
    · obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := ok.f2c _ h3
      apply hg
      rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (F.del g) with hd | hd
      · rw [h2 ⟨g, hd⟩, one_mul]
        simp [Frame.delK, hd]
      · have hm : g * F.c ∈ F.del.ker := by rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, hd, ok.del_c]; rfl
        have e := h2 ⟨_, hm⟩
        have ec : F.om k F.c = -1 := by
          apply Units.ext
          apply φ.injective
          rw [om_map]
          have := hc i
          rw [← neg_one_mul] at this
          rw [← mul_right_cancel₀ hne this]
          simp
        have ed : F.delK k g = -1 := by
          apply Units.ext
          simp [Frame.delK, hd]
        simp only [map_mul, ec, ok.Ψ_c, mul_one] at e
        rw [ed, mul_right_comm, e]
  have hz : ∀ i j, Φ i j = 0 := by
    rcases htri with ht | ht
    · exact tri_zero (ι := (Fin n)ᵒᵈ) (fun g : F.G0 => (ρ.r g)ᵀ) (fun g => F.om K g) Φ
        (fun g i j hij => ht g j i hij) h1 key
    · exact tri_zero (fun g : F.G0 => (ρ.r g)ᵀ) (fun g => F.om K g) Φ ht h1 key
  ext1 X
  simp [hsum, hz]


[APD_SRC_FLAG] Actual ordinary-flag input from the supplied Space and separating characters
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ZpowWNe.lean#L5-L48

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem zpow_w_ne {k : Type} [Field k] {n : ℕ} (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) (a : kˣ) (ha : a ^ 2 ≠ 1)
    (i j : Fin n) (hij : i ≠ j) : a ^ w n i ≠ a ^ w n j := by
  have h1 : a ≠ 1 := by
    rintro rfl
    simp at ha
  have h2 : a ≠ a⁻¹ := by
    intro h
    apply ha
    rw [sq]
    nth_rewrite 2 [h]
    simp
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl
  · fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [w] at hij ⊢
    all_goals first | exact h1 | exact Ne.symm h1 | exact h2 | exact Ne.symm h2
  · fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [w] at hij ⊢
    all_goals first | exact h1 | exact Ne.symm h1 | exact h2 | exact Ne.symm h2

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.flag :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] (f : S.A →+* C) (v : F.V), UpperTheory.V.W.HasFlag ((S.u.map f).r.comp (F.loc0 v)).toHomUnits ((S.u.map f).ψ v) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S C _ _ f v
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := hF.f3 v
  obtain ⟨hB, hm⟩ := co_B hF hn S
  refine flag_loc _ _ g (fun i j hij => ?_) ?_ (fun s => (S.u.map f).ord v s)
  · have hu : IsUnit (S.ψB v i g - S.ψB v j g) := by
      by_contra hu
      have h1 : S.ψB v i g - S.ψB v j g ∈ S.mB := by
        rw [← hm]
        exact hu
      have h2 : (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R) - S.ρ.ψ v j g ∈ maximalIdeal S.R := h1
      have h3 := (res_eq_zero_iff O S.R _).2 h2
      have h4 : ∀ i, res O S.R (S.ρ.ψ v i g) = ((F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O) :=
        fun i => S.lift.2.1.2 v i g
      rw [map_sub, h4, h4, sub_eq_zero] at h3
      exact zpow_w_ne hn _ hg i j hij (Units.ext h3)
    have h5 := (hu.map S.inc).map f
    rw [map_sub, map_sub] at h5
    exact h5
  · have h := congrArg (Polynomial.map ((residue C).comp f)) (o1 hF hn S v g)
    rw [← Matrix.charpoly_map, Polynomial.map_prod] at h
    simp only [Polynomial.map_sub, Polynomial.map_X, Polynomial.map_C] at h
    exact h


[APD_SRC_PRES] Full original pres on every supplied Loc, both ranks, exact N=N'+n*d+1
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/HasObs.lean#L26-L124
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.pres :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), L.Pres := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField O) l := IsCoef.char
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing L.O' := inferInstance
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal L.O') L.O' := IsCoef.complete
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField L.O') := IsCoef.fin
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField L.O') l := IsCoef.char
  have hU : NRep.Univ L.O' L.ρ := by
    refine ⟨(loc_rho hF hn S x L).1, ?_⟩
    intro C _ _ _ ρ' hc hb
    exact def_iso hF hn S x L C ρ' hc hb
  obtain ⟨B, hBo, hBc⟩ := pr_box hF L.R (maximalIdeal L.R) L.ρ (loc_rho hF hn S x L).1 (fun v => flag hF hn S L.R (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) v)
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := (pr_res hF hn S x L).1
  have hG := pr_good hF hn (ResidueField L.O') φ _ hφ
  haveI : Module.Finite (ResidueField L.O') (Tan L.O' L.R) := (pr_tan L.O' L.R).1
  have hBo' := hBo.map (res L.O' L.R : L.R →+* ResidueField L.O')
  have hres := pr_res hF hn S x L
  have hcount := pr_count (K := ResidueField L.O') F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b
    ((Box.tanCo L.O' B).comp (Tan L.O' L.R).subtype)
    (fun v Y hY => pr_brig (ResidueField L.O') _ (Box.bar L.O' B) hBo' hG.1 v Y hY)
    (fun z hz => by
      obtain ⟨D, hD, X, hX⟩ := pr_tsurj hF L.O' L.R L.ρ B hU hBo hBc hG.1 z hz
      exact ⟨⟨D, hD⟩, X, hX⟩)
  obtain ⟨D₀, hD₀, hD₀s⟩ := pr_tor hF L.O' L.R L.ρ B hU hBo hBc hG.1 hG.2.2
  have h1 := hcount.2 _ ⟨D₀, hD₀⟩ hG.2.1 hD₀s
  have hec := hF.ec (ResidueField L.O') (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (ResidueField L.O')) (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b
    (pr_open hF L.O' L.R B hBc).2.1
    (fun v s X hX => pr_bstab _ (Box.bar L.O' B) v s X hX)
    (fun f hf => pr_dual hF hn (ResidueField L.O') φ _ (Box.bar L.O' B) hBo' hφ hres.2 f hf)
  have h2K : (2 : ResidueField L.O') ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have hd := (CharP.cast_eq_zero_iff (ResidueField L.O') l 2).1 (by exact_mod_cast h)
    have hl := (Fact.out : l.Prime)
    have h4 := Nat.le_of_dvd (by norm_num) hd
    have h5 := hl.two_le
    have h6 := hF.l_odd
    omega
  have hκ : ∀ w : F.Inf, Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') ((Box.bar L.O' B).ad (F.cw w) - 1).ker = (n * n - n) / 2 := by
    intro w
    have hsq : (Box.bar L.O' B).r (F.cw w) * (Box.bar L.O' B).r (F.cw w) = 1 := by
      rw [← map_mul, hF.cw_sq w, map_one]
    have hr : ((Box.bar L.O' B).r (F.cw w)).right = -1 := by
      rw [(Box.bar L.O' B).nu, hF.del_cw w]
    have key : 2 * Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') ((Box.bar L.O' B).ad (F.cw w) - 1).ker + n = n * n :=
      pr_cfix (ResidueField L.O') n _ h2K hsq hr
    have hle : n ≤ n * n := Nat.le_mul_self n
    omega
  have hβ : ∀ v : F.V, Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') ((Box.bar L.O' B).b v) = (n * n - n) / 2 + n := by
    intro v
    have key : 2 * Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') ((Box.bar L.O' B).b v) = n * (n + 1) :=
      pr_bdim (ResidueField L.O') n ((Box.bar L.O' B).g v)
    have hle : n ≤ n * n := Nat.le_mul_self n
    have e : n * (n + 1) = n * n + n := by ring
    omega
  have hsum1 : ∑ v : F.V, F.dv v * Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') ((Box.bar L.O' B).b v) = F.d * ((n * n - n) / 2) + F.d * n := by
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun v _ => by rw [hβ v]), ← Finset.sum_mul]
    show F.d * ((n * n - n) / 2 + n) = _
    ring
  have hsum2 : ∑ w : F.Inf, Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') ((Box.bar L.O' B).ad (F.cw w) - 1).ker = F.d * ((n * n - n) / 2) := by
    simp only [hκ, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, smul_eq_mul, hF.card_inf]
  have hT : Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') (coneH2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b) + n * F.d + 1 ≤ Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') (Tan L.O' L.R) := by
    have e := hec.2
    rw [hsum1, hsum2] at e
    have : n * F.d = F.d * n := Nat.mul_comm _ _
    omega
  have hobs : HasObs L.O' L.R (coneH2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b) := by
    intro C' _ _ _ p hp hm
    obtain ⟨D⟩ := pr_setlift hF L.O' C' L.R p B hp hm hBc
    have hZ : ∀ u, D.co u ∈ continuousCocycles2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b := by
      intro u
      obtain ⟨_, _, hlc1, hlc2⟩ := pr_omk hF L.O' C' L.R p B D hm
      refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
      · exact hlc1.comp (fun M => M.map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u))
      · intro v
        exact (hlc2 v).comp (fun M => M.map (evK (RingHom.ker p) u))
      · intro s t r
        exact borelProjection_isTwoCocycle hF L.O' C' L.R p B D u hm s t r
      · intro v s t
        exact pr_cocv hF L.O' C' L.R p B D u hm v s t
    refine ⟨(Submodule.mkQ _).comp (LinearMap.codRestrict _ D.co hZ), ?_⟩
    intro u hu C'' _ _ _ q p'' hc hq hk
    refine pr_obs hF L.O' L.R L.ρ B hU hBo hBc hG.1 C' p D u hm ?_ C'' q p'' hc hq hk
    have h' : (⟨D.co u, hZ u⟩ : continuousCocycles2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b) ∈
        Submodule.comap (continuousCocycles2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b).subtype (continuousCoboundaries2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b) :=
      (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 hu
    exact h'
  haveI : Module.Finite (ResidueField L.O') (coneH2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b) := hec.1
  have hobs' : HasObs L.O' L.R (coneH2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b ×
      (Fin (Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') (Tan L.O' L.R) - (n * F.d + 1) -
        Module.finrank (ResidueField L.O') (coneH2 F.loc (Box.bar L.O' B).ad (Box.bar L.O' B).b)) → ResidueField L.O')) := by
    intro C' _ _ _ p hp hm
    obtain ⟨ob, hob⟩ := hobs C' p hp hm
    refine ⟨(LinearMap.inl _ _ _).comp ob, fun u hu => hob u ?_⟩
    simpa using hu
  obtain ⟨p, f, hs, hk⟩ := pr_mazur L.O' L.R _ hobs'
  refine ⟨_, _, p, f, ?_, hs, hk⟩
  rw [Module.finrank_prod, Module.finrank_fin_fun]
  omega


[APD_SRC_C1CH] Retained full component-chain supplier c1ch and exact dimension threshold
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/EnnrealVolumeRatioStepCancel.lean#L24-L51
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.c1ch :
    ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [IsNoetherianRing R] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal R) R] (h D : ℕ) (f : Fin h → R), (nilradical R).IsPrime → (∃ (O : Type) (_ : CommRing O) (_ : IsDomain O) (_ : IsDiscreteValuationRing O) (_ : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O) (_ : Algebra O R), IsCO O R) → (∀ i, f i ∈ maximalIdeal R) → ((D + 1 + h : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim R → Chained (Ideal.span (Set.range f)) D := by
  intro R _ _ _ _ h D f hnil hO hf hdim
  obtain ⟨O, _, _, _, _, _, hCO⟩ := hO
  haveI : IrreducibleSpace (PrimeSpectrum R) :=
    (PrimeSpectrum.irreducibleSpace_iff_isPrime_nilradical).2 hnil
  have hCL : UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCL O R := UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCL.of_isCO O R
  set a : Ideal R := Ideal.span (Set.range f) with ha_def
  have hara : ReciprocityTheory.CA.AraLE a h := by
    classical
    refine ⟨Finset.univ.image f, ?_, ?_⟩
    · exact (Finset.card_image_le).trans (by rw [Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin])
    · rw [ha_def, Finset.coe_image, Finset.coe_univ, Set.image_univ]
  have hne : a ≠ ⊤ := by
    intro htop
    have hle : a ≤ maximalIdeal R := by
      rw [ha_def, Ideal.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
      exact hf i
    exact (maximalIdeal.isMaximal R).ne_top (top_le_iff.1 (htop ▸ hle))
  refine ⟨fun P hP => ?_, fun P hP Q hQ => ?_⟩
  · have hc := UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.Conn.clComp hCL a P h hara hP
    exact ennreal_volume_ratio_step_cancel (D + 1) h _ (hdim.trans hc)
  · have hdim' : ((D + h + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim R := by
      have he : D + h + 1 = D + 1 + h := by omega
      rw [he]
      exact hdim
    exact UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.Conn.clChain hCL a h D hne hara hdim' P hP Q hQ


[APD_SRC_CLCOMP] Retained actual complete-local component dimension body
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AlgebraTools/ConnectedPrimeFields/ClComp.lean#L1-L42
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebraHeight.IsLocalHom
import Mathlib
import ReciprocityTheory.CommutativeAlgebra.ChainConnInDim
import ReciprocityTheory.CommutativeAlgebra.SaturatedChains.IsSaturatedChain
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.ConnectedPrimeFields.IsWeaklyRegularPairModOneLeft
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.ConnectedPrimeFields.Cohen
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.ConnectedPrimeFields.CompDim

open IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.Conn

private theorem dim_le_nil {R : Type} [CommRing R] :
    ringKrullDim R ≤ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ nilradical R) := by
  rw [ringKrullDim_quotient]
  have h : PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus (nilradical R : Set R) = Set.univ := by
    ext p
    simp only [Set.mem_univ, iff_true, PrimeSpectrum.mem_zeroLocus, SetLike.coe_subset_coe]
    exact nilradical_le_prime p.asIdeal
  rw [h]
  exact le_of_eq (Order.krullDim_eq_of_orderIso OrderIso.Set.univ).symm

theorem UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.Conn.clComp :
    ∀ {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O]
      [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R] [IrreducibleSpace (PrimeSpectrum R)], UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCL O R → ∀ (a q : Ideal R) (h : ℕ),
      ReciprocityTheory.CA.AraLE a h → q ∈ a.minimalPrimes → ringKrullDim R ≤ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ q) + h := by
  intro O R _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ hR a q h ha hq
  haveI := hR.noeth
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hrad⟩ := ha
  have hnil : (nilradical R).IsPrime := by
    have hirr : IsIrreducible (PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus ((⊥ : Ideal R) : Set R)) := by
      rw [Submodule.bot_coe, PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus_singleton_zero]
      exact IrreducibleSpace.isIrreducible_univ _
    exact (PrimeSpectrum.isIrreducible_zeroLocus_iff ⊥).mp hirr
  have hsup : (nilradical R ⊔ Ideal.span (s : Set R)).radical = a.radical := by
    rw [Ideal.radical_sup, nilradical, Ideal.radical_idem, ← Ideal.radical_sup, Submodule.zero_eq_bot,
      bot_sup_eq, hrad]
  have hq' : q ∈ (nilradical R ⊔ Ideal.span (s : Set R)).minimalPrimes := by
    rw [← Ideal.radical_minimalPrimes, hsup, Ideal.radical_minimalPrimes]
    exact hq
  calc ringKrullDim R ≤ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ nilradical R) := dim_le_nil
    _ ≤ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ q) + s.card := compDim (cohen hR) _ q s hnil hq'
    _ ≤ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ q) + h := by
      gcongr


[APD_SRC_CLCHAIN] Retained actual complete-local connected-chain body
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AlgebraTools/ConnectedPrimeFields/ClChain.lean#L1-L20
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebraHeight.IsLocalHom
import Mathlib
import ReciprocityTheory.CommutativeAlgebra.ChainConnInDim
import ReciprocityTheory.CommutativeAlgebra.SaturatedChains.IsSaturatedChain
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.ConnectedPrimeFields.IsWeaklyRegularPairModOneLeft
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.ConnectedPrimeFields.ClConn
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.ConnectedPrimeFields.DimLeOfForall
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.ConnectedPrimeFields.Bridge

open IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.Conn

theorem UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.Conn.clChain :
    ∀ {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O]
      [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R] [IrreducibleSpace (PrimeSpectrum R)], UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCL O R → ∀ (a : Ideal R) (h k : ℕ), a ≠ ⊤ →
      ReciprocityTheory.CA.AraLE a h → ((k + h + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim R →
      ∀ p ∈ a.minimalPrimes, ∀ q ∈ a.minimalPrimes,
        Relation.ReflTransGen (fun x y : Ideal R => x ∈ a.minimalPrimes ∧ y ∈ a.minimalPrimes ∧ ReciprocityTheory.CA.Linked R k x y) p q := by
  intro O R _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ hCL a h k ha hara hdim p hp q hq
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing R := hCL.noeth
  exact chain a (k : WithBot ℕ∞) ((bridge a _).2 (clConn hCL a h k ha hara hdim)) p hp q hq


[APD_SRC_SHARP2] Actual rank-two consumer of pres, c1ch and strict triangular dimension
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ChainedBot.lean#L51-L86
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.sharp2 :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ S : Space O F 2, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), F.Big C₀ → ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → Chained (⊥ : Ideal L.R) (2 * F.d + 1) ∧ ∀ P ∈ minimalPrimes L.R, L.ρ.TI P := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hok S x L hbig hx
  obtain ⟨N, N', p, f, hN, hs, hk⟩ := pres hok (Or.inl rfl) S x L
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal L.O') L.O' := IsCoef.complete (l := l)
  haveI : IsCO L.O' (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') := co_mvps _ _ _
  have hdim := ((UpperTheory.V.CA.cl_cm).1 L.O' N).2
  have hch : Chained (Ideal.span (Set.range f)) (2 * F.d + 1) := by
    apply c1ch _ N' (2 * F.d + 1) f
    · rw [nilradical_eq_zero, Ideal.zero_eq_bot]
      exact Ideal.bot_prime
    · exact ⟨L.O', inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance⟩
    · intro i
      have hi : f i ∈ RingHom.ker p := hk ▸ Ideal.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩
      exact IsLocalRing.le_maximalIdeal (RingHom.ker_ne_top p) hi
    · rw [hdim]
      exact le_of_eq (congrArg (fun n : ℕ => (n : WithBot ℕ∞)) (by omega))
  have h1 : Chained (⊥ : Ideal L.R) (2 * F.d + 1) := by
    refine chained_bot_of_chained_ker_of_surjective (p : MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O' →+* L.R) hs _ ?_
    rw [← hk] at hch
    exact hch
  refine ⟨h1, fun P hP => ?_⟩
  have hr := (loc_rho hok (Or.inl rfl) S x L).2.2.2 hx
  have hl := (krull_dim_lt_of_not_le_triangular hok (Or.inl rfl) S x L hbig hx).1
  have hPp : P.IsPrime := hP.1.1
  refine ⟨⟨?_, ?_⟩, fun h => absurd h (by decide)⟩
  · rw [hr.1, top_le_iff]
    exact hPp.ne_top
  · intro hle
    have hge := h1.1 P hP
    have hmono : ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) :=
      ringKrullDim_le_of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.factor hle) (Ideal.Quotient.factor_surjective hle)
    have hlt : ((2 * F.d + 1 + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) < ((2 * F.d : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) :=
      lt_of_le_of_lt (hge.trans hmono) hl
    have : 2 * F.d + 1 + 1 < 2 * F.d := by exact_mod_cast hlt
    omega


[APD_SRC_SHARP3] Actual rank-three Sharp consumer, including separate parity presentation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ChainedOfComap.lean#L72-L142
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.sharp3 :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ S : Space O F 3, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), F.Big C₀ → ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → ∃ I : Ideal L.R, L.Sharp I := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF S x L hB hx
  obtain ⟨-, -, hcmp, htri⟩ := loc_rho hF (Or.inr rfl) S x L
  have hoff : ∀ I : Ideal L.R, Chained I (3 * F.d + 1) → ∀ P ∈ I.minimalPrimes, L.ρ.OffTri P := by
    intro I hI P hP
    refine ⟨fun h => hP.1.1.ne_top (top_le_iff.1 ((htri hx).1 ▸ h)), fun h => ?_⟩
    have h1 := (krull_dim_lt_of_not_le_triangular hF (Or.inr rfl) S x L hB hx).1
    have h2 : ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) :=
      ringKrullDim_le_of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.factor h) (Ideal.Quotient.factor_surjective h)
    have h3 := ((hI.1 P hP).trans h2).trans_lt h1
    have h4 : 3 * F.d + 1 + 1 < 3 * F.d := by exact_mod_cast h3
    omega
  by_cases hee : S.u.ee ≤ x
  · obtain ⟨E⟩ := epres hF S x L hee
    have hG : ∀ i, E.Go i ∈ RingHom.ker E.p := fun i =>
      E.ker ▸ Ideal.subset_span (Or.inr ⟨i, rfl⟩)
    have hFe : ∀ i, E.Fe i ∈ RingHom.ker E.p := fun i =>
      E.ker ▸ Ideal.subset_span (Or.inl ⟨i, rfl⟩)
    choose M hM hGM using fun i => oddm L.O' E.a E.g (E.Go i) (E.odd i) (E.min _ (hG i))
    have hGo : E.Go = Matrix.of M *ᵥ fun j => MvPowerSeries.X (.inr j) := funext hGM
    have hch : Chained (Ideal.span {E.p (Matrix.of M).det}) (3 * F.d + 1) := by
      refine chained_of_comap E.p.toRingHom E.surj _ _ ?_
      have h1 := detsec' L.O' E.a E.b E.g _ (gpos hF S x L hx E) E.Fe (Matrix.of M) E.num
        (fun i => cc_mem E.p.toRingHom _ (hFe i)) hM
      rw [← hGo, Ideal.span_union, ← E.ker] at h1
      convert h1 using 1
      have : Ideal.span {E.p (Matrix.of M).det} = (Ideal.span {(Matrix.of M).det}).map E.p.toRingHom := by
        rw [Ideal.map_span, Set.image_singleton]; rfl
      rw [this, Ideal.comap_map_of_surjective E.p.toRingHom E.surj, sup_comm]
      rfl
    refine ⟨_, fun h => absurd hee h, fun P hP hne => ?_, hch, fun P hP => ?_⟩
    · rw [E.ee, Ideal.span_le, Set.range_subset_iff, not_forall] at hne
      obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hne
      rw [Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem]
      refine (hP.mem_or_mem ?_).resolve_right hj
      have h1 : ((Matrix.of M).adjugate *ᵥ E.Go) j = (Matrix.of M).det * MvPowerSeries.X (.inr j) := by
        rw [hGo, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, Matrix.adjugate_mul, Matrix.smul_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec]; rfl
      have h2 : ((Matrix.of M).adjugate *ᵥ E.Go) j ∈ RingHom.ker E.p := by
        rw [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct]
        exact Ideal.sum_mem _ fun i _ => Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ (hG i)
      rw [h1, RingHom.mem_ker, map_mul] at h2
      rw [h2]
      exact P.zero_mem
    · by_cases he : L.ρ.ee ≤ P
      · exact Or.inr (thick hF S x L E _ hGo P hP.1.1 ⟨hoff _ hch P hP, rfl, he⟩
          (hP.1.2 (Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self _)))
      · exact Or.inl ⟨hoff _ hch P hP, fun _ => he⟩
  · obtain ⟨N, N', p, f, hN, hp, hk⟩ := pres hF (Or.inr rfl) S x L
    have hch : Chained (⊥ : Ideal L.R) (3 * F.d + 1) := by
      refine chained_of_comap p.toRingHom hp _ _ ?_
      haveI hco : IsCO L.O' (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') := co_mvps L.O' L.O' (Fin N)
      haveI : IsNoetherianRing (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') := hco.toIsNoetherian
      haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O')) (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') :=
        hco.complete
      have h1 := c1ch (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') N' (3 * F.d + 1) f
        (by rw [nilradical_eq_zero]; exact Ideal.isPrime_bot) ⟨L.O', L.cO, L.dO, L.vO, inferInstance, _, hco⟩
        (fun i hu => by
          have h0 : f i ∈ RingHom.ker p := hk ▸ Ideal.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩
          have := hu.map p
          rw [RingHom.mem_ker.1 h0] at this
          exact not_isUnit_zero this)
        (by
          rw [GlobalTheory.FormalIsogeny.ringKrullDim_mvPowerSeries, IsDiscreteValuationRing.ringKrullDim_eq_one,
            Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_fin, hN]
          norm_cast
          omega)
      rw [← hk] at h1
      exact h1
    refine ⟨⊥, fun _ => rfl, fun _ _ _ => bot_le, hch, fun P hP => Or.inl ⟨hoff _ hch P hP, fun _ h => hee ?_⟩⟩
    exact ((hcmp P).2.2.1 h).trans ((Ideal.comap_mono (le_maximalIdeal hP.1.1.ne_top)).trans L.lies.le)


[APD_SRC_DIM] Retained exact triangular and coincident-character strict dimension bounds
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/KrullDimLtOfNotLeTriangular.lean#L36-L58
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.krull_dim_lt_of_not_le_triangular :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), F.Big C₀ → ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) < (n * F.d : ℕ) ∧ ∀ I : Ideal S.B, S.FinLam I → ∀ v i j, i ≠ j → ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ (I.map L.b ⊔ (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O L.R) ⊔ Ideal.span {y | ∃ g ∈ F.Nv v, y = (L.ρ.ψ v i g : L.R) - L.ρ.ψ v j g})) < (n * F.d : ℕ) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L hB hx
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := l51 hF hn S x L hx
  obtain ⟨hd, hdv⟩ := hB
  have hlt : ((n * F.d - 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) < (n * F.d : ℕ) := by
    have : n * F.d - 1 < n * F.d := by rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> omega
    exact_mod_cast this
  have hT : ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) ≤ (n * F.d - 1 : ℕ) := by
    refine h1.trans ?_
    have : (n - 1) * F.d + 2 + (3 * C₀ + 5) ≤ n * F.d - 1 := by rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> omega
    exact_mod_cast this
  refine ⟨hT.trans_lt hlt, fun I hI v i j hij => lt_of_le_of_lt (dim_le_of_primes _ _ fun P hP hle => ?_) hlt⟩
  by_cases hM : L.ρ.triM ≤ P
  · exact (dim_anti hM).trans hT
  have hoff : L.ρ.OffTri P := ⟨fun h => hP.ne_top (top_le_iff.1 (h2 ▸ h)), hM⟩
  have hIP : I ≤ L.π P :=
    (Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap (f := L.b)).1 (le_sup_left.trans (le_sup_left.trans hle))
  have hco : L.ρ.Coin P v i j := fun g hg => (le_sup_right.trans hle) (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩)
  have h := (l52 hF hn S x L I hI v i j hij P hP hoff hIP hco).2 (le_sup_right.trans (le_sup_left.trans hle))
  refine le_of_add_le h ?_
  have := hdv v
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> omega


[APD_SRC_L51] Retained actual triangular quotient dimension bound
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/CharpolyCoeffsInSubalgebra.lean#L50-L84
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.l51 :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) ≤ ((n - 1) * F.d + 2 + (3 * C₀ + 5) : ℕ) ∧ L.ρ.triP = ⊤ := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L hx
  refine ⟨?_, ((loc_rho hF hn S x L).2.2.2 hx).1⟩
  obtain ⟨s, s', h, hh, hs, hs', H⟩ := ab_gen hF
  obtain ⟨ϖ, hϖ⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible O
  have hϖ' := hϖ.maximalIdeal_eq
  obtain ⟨q1, q2, -⟩ := local_deformation_trace_mod_maximal hF hn S x L
  have hs1 : ∀ a, F.Ψ (s' a) = 1 := fun a => by
    obtain ⟨v, g, -, e, e'⟩ := hs' a
    rw [e']
    exact e
  let i0 : Fin n := ⟨0, by omega⟩
  let t : Unit ⊕ Fin (1 + C₀ + F.d) ⊕ ({i : Fin n // w n i = 0} × Fin F.d) → L.R :=
    Sum.elim (fun _ => algebraMap O L.R ϖ)
      (Sum.elim (fun a => L.ρ.r (s a) i0 i0 - 1) fun p => L.ρ.r (s' p.2) p.1 p.1 - 1)
  have hc := krull_dim_quotient_bound L.ρ.triM (S.mB.map L.b) t (by
      rintro (_ | a | p)
      · exact q1 (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ (hϖ' ▸ Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self ϖ))
      · exact q2 _ _ (hs a)
      · exact q2 _ _ (hs1 p.2))
    (fun P hP h0 ht => localization_prime_generation hF hn S x L s s' h hh hs H P hP h0
      (by rw [hϖ', Ideal.map_span, Set.image_singleton, Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem]; exact ht (.inl ()))
      (fun a i hi => by
        obtain rfl : i = i0 := Fin.ext hi
        exact ht (.inr (.inl a)))
      (fun a i hi => ht (.inr (.inr (⟨i, hi⟩, a)))))
  have hw : Fintype.card {i : Fin n // w n i = 0} + 2 = n := by
    rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> decide
  refine hc.trans ((add_le_add_left (fib_dim hF hn S x L hx) _).trans ?_)
  rw [← Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_le]
  simp only [Fintype.card_sum, Fintype.card_prod, Fintype.card_fin, Fintype.card_unit]
  have e : n - 1 = Fintype.card {i : Fin n // w n i = 0} + 1 := by omega
  rw [e, add_mul]
  omega


[APD_SRC_L52] Retained actual coincident-character prime dimension bound
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/TameInertiaCondition.lean#L257-L280
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.l52 :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), ∀ I : Ideal S.B, S.FinLam I → ∀ v i j, i ≠ j → ∀ P : Ideal L.R, P.IsPrime → L.ρ.OffTri P → I ≤ L.π P → L.ρ.Coin P v i j → ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) + F.dv v ≤ (n * F.d + 6 : ℕ) ∧ ((maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O L.R) ≤ P → ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) + F.dv v ≤ (n * F.d + 5 : ℕ)) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L I hI v i j hij P hP hoff hIP hcoin
  haveI := hP
  have hrho := loc_rho hF hn S x L
  have hiff := hrho.2.2.1 P
  have hη : (L.im P).IsPrime := Ideal.comap_isPrime _ P
  have hoffS : S.u.OffTri (L.im P) := ⟨fun h => hoff.1 (hiff.1.2 h), fun h => hoff.2 (hiff.2.1.2 h)⟩
  have hfib := fibre hF hn S (L.im P) hη hoffS
  have hdim := (loc_dim hF hn S x L).1 P hP 1 (by simpa using hfib)
  have hc : ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, S.ψB v i g - S.ψB v j g ∈ L.π P := by
    intro g hg
    have h := hcoin g hg
    show L.ι (S.inc (S.ψB v i g - S.ψB v j g)) ∈ P
    rw [map_sub, map_sub]
    exact h
  obtain ⟨m, hm1, hm2⟩ := dim_B hF hn S I hI v i j hij (L.π P) hIP hc
  have key : ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) + F.dv v ≤ ((m + 1 + F.dv v : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) := by
    calc ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) + F.dv v ≤ ((m : WithBot ℕ∞) + ((1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞)) + F.dv v :=
          add_le_add (hdim.trans (add_le_add hm1 le_rfl)) le_rfl
      _ = ((m + 1 + F.dv v : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) := by push_cast; rfl
  refine ⟨key.trans ?_, fun _ => key.trans ?_⟩
  · exact_mod_cast (by omega)
  · exact_mod_cast (by omega)


[APD_SRC_GL2] Retained full rank-two propagation, incidence and seeds
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/ComponentsGoodRankTwo.lean#L31-L163
  {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}

private def gd (S : Space O F 2) (M : Ideal S.B) : GlobData (PrimeSpectrum S.A) where
  TI := {p | S.u.TI p.asIdeal}
  Good := {p | M ≤ S.π p.asIdeal}
  Core := S.BB
  Cl := {p | p ∈ S.BB ∧ p.asIdeal.IsMaximal}
  EE := ∅

private def ld {S : Space O F 2} (M : Ideal S.B) {x : Ideal S.A} (L : S.Loc x) : LocData L.R where
  D1 := 2 * F.d + 1
  TI := {p | L.ρ.TI p.asIdeal}
  TE := ∅
  Good := {p | M ≤ L.π p.asIdeal}
  Nice := {p | Nice L.ρ p.asIdeal}
  Thick := ∅
  sharp := ⊥

private theorem ti_down {R : Type} [CommRing R] (ρ : NRep F 2 R) {P Q : Ideal R} (h : P ≤ Q) (hQ : ρ.TI Q) :
    ρ.TI P :=
  ⟨⟨fun a => hQ.1.1 (a.trans h), fun a => hQ.1.2 (a.trans h)⟩, fun a => absurd a (by decide)⟩

private theorem ld_ok (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hB : F.Big C₀) {S : Space O F 2} {M : Ideal S.B} (hM : S.FinLam M)
    (h1 : P1' S M) {x : Ideal S.A} (hx : ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x) (L : S.Loc x) : (ld M L).Ok := by
  obtain ⟨hc, ht⟩ := sharp2 hF S x L hB hx
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro P Q h hP
    exact le_trans hP (Ideal.comap_mono (Ideal.comap_mono h))
  · intro P Q h hQ
    exact ti_down _ h hQ
  · intro p hp
    exact Or.inl ⟨hp.off, fun a => absurd a (by decide)⟩
  · intro I hI hd
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hle⟩ := exists_nice_prime_above_of_large_dim l O F C₀ 2 hF hB (Or.inl rfl) S x L hx M hM I.asIdeal hI hd
    exact ⟨⟨p, hp.prime⟩, hp, hle⟩
  · intro e he
    exact absurd he (Set.notMem_empty _)
  · intro P _ hP
    exact hP
  · intro P Q hP hQ
    have key : ∀ q : Ideal L.R, Relation.ReflTransGen
        (fun P Q => P ∈ (⊥ : Ideal L.R).minimalPrimes ∧ Q ∈ (⊥ : Ideal L.R).minimalPrimes ∧
          ((2 * F.d + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ (P ⊔ Q))) P.asIdeal q →
        ∀ Q : PrimeSpectrum L.R, Q.asIdeal = q → Relation.ReflTransGen (ld M L).Adj P Q := by
      intro q h
      induction h with
      | refl =>
        intro Q hQ
        rw [PrimeSpectrum.ext hQ]
      | @tail b c _ hbc ih =>
        intro Q hQ
        subst hQ
        exact (ih ⟨b, hbc.1.1.1⟩ rfl).tail hbc
    exact key _ (hc.2 _ hP _ hQ) Q rfl
  · intro P hP
    exact Or.inl (ht _ hP)
  · intro p hp hI hG P hP
    exact h1 x L p.asIdeal hp hI hG P.asIdeal P.2 hP
  · intro p _ he
    exact absurd he (Set.notMem_empty _)

private theorem ti_open (S : Space O F 2) (M : Ideal S.B) : IsOpen (gd S M).TI := by
  have : (gd S M).TI = (PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus (S.u.triP : Set S.A))ᶜ ∩
      (PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus (S.u.triM : Set S.A))ᶜ := by
    ext p
    exact ⟨fun h => h.1, fun h => ⟨h, fun a => absurd a (by decide)⟩⟩
  rw [this]
  exact (PrimeSpectrum.isClosed_zeroLocus _).isOpen_compl.inter (PrimeSpectrum.isClosed_zeroLocus _).isOpen_compl

private theorem good_closed (S : Space O F 2) (M : Ideal S.B) : IsClosed (gd S M).Good := by
  have : (gd S M).Good = PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus (M.map (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A) : Set S.A) := by
    ext p
    exact Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.symm
  rw [this]
  exact PrimeSpectrum.isClosed_zeroLocus _

private theorem at_pt (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hB : F.Big C₀) {S : Space O F 2} {M : Ideal S.B} (hM : S.FinLam M)
    (h1 : P1' S M) {x : PrimeSpectrum S.A} (hx : ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x.asIdeal) (L : S.Loc x.asIdeal) :
    (∀ Y Y', (gd S M).CompI Y → (gd S M).CompI Y' → x ∈ Y → x ∈ Y' → Y ⊆ (gd S M).Good → Y' ⊆ (gd S M).Good) ∧
      (∃ Y, (gd S M).CompI Y ∧ x ∈ Y) ∧
      ((∃ I ∈ (ld M L).Good, I ∈ (ld M L).TI ∧ ((ld M L).D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim I.asIdeal) →
        ∃ Y, (gd S M).CompI Y ∧ Y ⊆ (gd S M).Good) := by
  obtain ⟨hc, ht⟩ := sharp2 hF S x.asIdeal L hB hx
  letI : Algebra S.A L.R := (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R).toAlgebra
  haveI : Module.Flat S.A L.R := (loc_flat hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal L).1
  have ch := chartOfFlat (PrimeSpectrum S.A) S.A id Topology.IsOpenEmbedding.id L.R
  have hpt : id (PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap S.A L.R) (closedPoint L.R)) = x := PrimeSpectrum.ext L.lies
  rw [hpt] at ch
  have hr := (loc_rho hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal L).2.2.1
  obtain ⟨a, b, c⟩ := globOfCharts _ (gd S M) (ti_open S M) (good_closed S M) x L.R _ ch (ld M L) (by
    ext p
    exact and_congr (and_congr (hr p.asIdeal).1.not (hr p.asIdeal).2.1.not)
      (imp_congr Iff.rfl (hr p.asIdeal).2.2.not)) rfl (locProp _ _ (ld_ok hF hB hM h1 hx L))
  refine ⟨a fun P hP => le_trans ?_ (hc.1 P hP), b fun P hP => ht _ hP, c⟩
  exact Nat.cast_le.2 (Nat.le_succ _)

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.components_good_rank_two :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.PropagationTheoremGL2 := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF hB S M hM h1 hS
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing S.A := (gr_A hF (Or.inl rfl) S).2.2.2.2.2.1
  have hok : (gd S M).Ok := by
    refine ⟨fun p hp => hp.1, preconnected_rank_two hF S, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rintro C hC ⟨p, hp, hp'⟩
      obtain ⟨x, ⟨⟨a, -⟩, b⟩, c⟩ := jac hF (Or.inl rfl) S C hC Set.univ isOpen_univ ⟨p, ⟨hp, trivial⟩, hp'⟩
      exact ⟨x, a, b, c⟩
    · intro p hp
      rw [mem_closure_iff]
      intro U hU hpU
      obtain ⟨x, ⟨⟨-, a⟩, b⟩, c⟩ := jac hF (Or.inl rfl) S Set.univ isClosed_univ U hU ⟨p, ⟨trivial, hpU⟩, hp⟩
      exact ⟨x, a, ⟨b, c⟩, Set.notMem_empty _⟩
    · rintro Y ⟨hY, p, hp, hpI⟩
      obtain ⟨η, hη, hi⟩ := comp_min hY
      obtain ⟨x, -, b, c⟩ := l41 hF (Or.inl rfl) S η hη.1.1 (min_hom hF (Or.inl rfl) S η hη)
        fun h => hpI.1.1 (h.trans ((hi p).1 hp))
      exact ⟨x, (hi x).2 c, b⟩
    · intro x hx
      obtain ⟨L⟩ := loc_ex hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal hx.2 hx.1.1
      exact (at_pt hF hB hM h1 hx.1.2 L).1
    · rintro x ⟨hx, -⟩
      obtain ⟨L⟩ := loc_ex hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal hx.2 hx.1.1
      exact (at_pt hF hB hM h1 hx.1.2 L).2.1
    · obtain ⟨ξ, Z, hξ, hI, hZ, hMZ, hZξ, hd⟩ := hS
      obtain ⟨x, hm, hx, H⟩ := seedpt hF (Or.inl rfl) S Z ξ hZ hξ hI hZξ
      obtain ⟨L⟩ := loc_ex hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal hm hx.1
      obtain ⟨P, hP, hPI, hπ, hdim⟩ := H L
      refine (at_pt hF hB hM h1 hx.2 L).2.2 ⟨⟨P, hP⟩, hMZ.trans hπ.ge, hPI, ?_⟩
      show ((2 * F.d + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P)
      rw [hdim, show ringKrullDim (S.B ⧸ Z) = _ from hd]
      exact_mod_cast (by omega : 2 * F.d + 1 ≤ 1 + 2 * F.d + 1)
  intro p hp hI
  exact globProp _ (gd S M) hok (a := ⟨p, hp⟩) hI


[APD_SRC_GL3] Retained full rank-three propagation and typed local/global data
